# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Nambya translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

Ibumbilo Lyemilawu Yezimbabwe Inshanduko (No. 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

MULAWU Kulamba nokubhisa iBumbilo lyemilawu yeZimbabwe. WAKAVUMILWA nonTungamili weNyika neGota lyePalamende yeZimbabwe. 1 5

Unsholo mubufupi

Mulawu yoyu ungashobwa uwhi Ishanduko yeBumbilo yoMulawu we Zimbabwe (No. 20), 2013. 2

Kupandulula Mumulawu yoyu— “mawhi anoti iBumbilo lyeMilawu limushinga” anova iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeZimbabwe yakatanga kushinga unsi wa18 Ihomba, 1980, likobwilululwa mushule mwezo;


“iBumbilo lyeMilawu ibhya” zwinoleba iBumbilo lyeMilawu weZimbabwe lilokwalwa muShejulu. 15


Kujiba kwokushinga kweBumbilo lyeMilawu limushinga feno neBumbilo ibhya

(1) Kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (2), iBumbilo lyeMilawu linolambwa akale bugalo bwalyo bunotolwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu ibhya.

(2) Kutila kuti zwibe pachena panosumwa kuti— Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

(a)

.

2013

iBumbilo ibhya linotanga kushinga pezhuba lyokuzibiswa sezwilobhaunulwa muShejulu yashanu nakamwempela yebumbilo yelo; yezwi zwinoleba kuti, izhuba mulawu yoyu pawunoshambajwa muGazeti kutobelwa chikamu 51(5) muBumbilo limushinga; akale

(b) kuvila pezhuba lyokutanga kushinga kweBumbilo lyeMilawu ibhya sokubhaunulwa kwatiwa mundima (a), iBumbilo limushinga feno linondilila mbeli lishinga sezwilobhaunulwa muShejulu yashanu nakamwempela yeBumbilo ibhya.

ISHEJULU (CHIKAMU 2) IBUMBILO LYEMILAWU YEZIMBABWE _______________ Matangisilo.

KUMISWA KWEZWIBABI CHISIYANISO 1

## Section 7

Inyika yeZimbabwe. Kukosha kweBumbilo lyeMilawu. Zwisungo zwilokosha. Indembela, Lwimbo lweNyika neMhande. Umbambanyilo wenhabi jafulumende. Indimi. Kukutaija kuzibwa kwozweBumbilo lyeMilawu kubanhu bose. CHISIYANISO 2

Zwibabalilo Zwenyika

## Section 34

Zwibabalilo zwichatungamila iNyika nenhabi jose jafulumende. Butongi bulolulama. Kubhatana kwabanhu bose, lunyalalo nokugajikana. Kusimiswa kwelulamilo jilokosha nolusungunuko. Zwisungo zwokushingilana nejimwe inyika/bokuzhe. Kubhudilila kwenyika. Kupiwa kwamasimba nokubumbwa kwemishingo. Kulya kunji. Magalilo. Kukwelaniswa kwabechinhukaji nabechinhulume. Kumililwa kwamatunhu zwilokwelana. Bana. Bachinokubuka. Bakwegula. Banhu banabukosha. Bakalwila iHondo yoLusungunuko. Mishingo nokulyijana kumishingo. Kutabilijwa kwemhuli. Kulobolana. Indayijo. Pokugala. Zwobuchilo. Lubhancho kunabanolushaka. Lubhancho penyaya jemilawu. Zwemizano nokuzwibalaija. Kulondotwa kwoluzibo lwechibanhu. Kutambulwa kwemilawu ilovumilwana nenyika jopasi.

## Section 43

Bunizi muZimbabwe. Bunizi bwokuzwalwa. Bunizi bwebhatuko. Bunizi bwokukwalisa. Kutolelwa bunizi. Kundila mbeli uli unizi mushule mokulobola kana kulobolwa kana kupalajana kokulobolana. Ibhodi yobunizi nokungwina kwabanhu. Masimba ePalamende penyaya jobunizi. Kundila mbeli kana kubwiliza bunizi bwakabelipo.

## Section 69 — Ilulamilo Jilokosha Nolusungunuko

Ilulamilo yobupenyu. Ilulamilo yolusungunuko lwonhu. Ilulamilo jabanhu balosungwa nabalobhatwa mubusungwa. Ilulamilo yokulemekejwa sonhu. Ilulamilo yokutabilijwa konhu. Lusungunuko pokushungulujwa kana kubhatwa nenzila kana kulangwa nenzila inolelupisa bunhu. Lusungunuko kubutapwa. Lusungunuko pokushungulujwa. Kukwelaniswa nokushalujwa. Ilulamilo yokuba nesiswa. Lusungunuko lwokubungana nokulyijana. Lusungunuko lwokutondejela nokusenga zwililijiko. Lusungunuko lwokuzwiyeyela zwaunoda. Lusungunuko lwokutula indangalilo nolusungunuko lwenzila jokwendesa inyaya. Kutubula kuwana luzibo. Lulimi namagalilo. Lusungunuko lwobumhizha kana unshingo. Ilulamilo jemishingo. Lusungunuko lokukwendenda nokugala. Ilulamilo jezwamatongelo enyika. Ilulamilo yokuwana indulamo ilokwelela. Ilulamilo yokubeteswa kunabululami. 4

## Section 78

Ilulamilo jabanhu bamutamijilwa. Ilulamilo yefuma. Ilulamilo yevu. Ilulamilo yezwilokombeleja. Lusungunuko lwokusomanikijwa kutatwa. Ilulamilo yendayijo. Ilulamilo yokuwana bushanana. Ilulamilo yokulya nevula. Ilulamilo yokulobolana. IMHINGU 3

## Section 87 — Kujibilijwa Kwelulamilo Nolusunguko Lukulugulu

Kujibilijwa kwelulamilo nolusungunuko. Ijibilijo ponshaji wegubaji linowila banhu banji. CHISIYANISO 5 Butongi

Masimba obutongi.

Masimba Obutongi IMHINGU 2

## Section 96 — Untungamili Wenyika Nabatobeli Bontungamili Wenyika

UnTungamili weNyika. Mishingo yoNtungamili weNyika. Unkwendeselo wokuvumilwa kushalwa soNtungamili kana kuti unTobeli weNyika. Kushalwa koNtungamili weNyika. Kunyenyeja inshalujo joNtungamili weNyika. Kungwina mu-ofisi koNtungamili weNyika nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika. Unshaji wokushinga koNtungamili weNyika nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika. Kusiya unshingo koNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika.

Chikamu

## Section 97 — Kubhiswa koNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika

ponshingo.

## Section 98

Kusosungwa koNtungamili weNyika .

## Section 99 — Mishingo yabaTobeli boNtungamili

## Section 100

Unowana ekabhata chibhula choNtungamili weNyika.

## Section 101

Kutolwa kwechibhula mushule mwolufu, kusiya unshingo kana kusotubula koNtungamili weNyika kana unTobeli wonTungamili weNyika.

## Section 102

Indipo yoNtungamili weNyika nabaTobeli babaTungamili beNyika.

## Section 103

UnTungamili weNyika nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika nayabo bakababa pozugalo yozu abatokwelela kutola zwimwe zwigalo kana kungwina mishingo. IMHINGU 3

## Section 104

## Section 105

## Section 106

## Section 107

## Section 108

## Section 109 — Makolokota, Batobeli Bamakolokota Negota Lyamakolokota

Kudomwa kwaMakolokota naBatobeli baMakolokota. Igota lyaMakolokota. Kuzwibhata kwabaTobeli boNtungamili, Makolokota. Undandu wabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika, Makolokota nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika. Mishaji yokushinga kwaMakulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota. Inshalujo jokushayiwa chibimbo munaFulumende. IMHINGU 4

## Section 110

## Section 111

## Section 112

## Section 113 — Mishingo Yobutongi Bwenyika

Mishingo yobutongi yoNtungamili naMakolokota. IHondo nolunyalalo. Masimba okulekejela. Unshaji wegubaji kubanji. IMHINGU 5

## Section 114

## Section 115 — Igweta Igulwana

IGweta igulwana. Kubhiswa unshingo kweGweta igulwana.

CHISIYANISO 6

Zwokubumba Milawu IMHINGU 1

## Section 116

## Section 117 — Masimba Okubumba Milawu

Babumbi bomulawu. Chimo namasimba enhabi inobumba milawu. IMHINGU 2 Ipalamende

## Section 118

## Section 119

IPalamende. Mishingo yePalamende.

IMHINGU 3 Iseneti

Chikamu

## Section 120

## Section 121

## Section 122

## Section 123

Imhingu jeSeneti. Kuvumilwa kushalwa saSeneta. UNtungamili weSeneti. UNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti. IMHINGU 4

## Section 124

## Section 125

## Section 126

## Section 127 — Igota Lyabamilili

Chimo cheGota lyabaMilili. Kuvumilwa nokusovumilwa kushalwa kuba imhingu yeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende. Undebelekeli weGota lyabaMilili. UnTobeli woNdebelekeli weGota lyabaMilili. IMHINGU 5

## Section 128

## Section 129 — Bulefu Bwonshaji Wokushinga Kwabamilili

Ikowhe lyoMilili muPalamende. Bulefu bwonshaji wokushinga kwoMilili muPalamende. IMHINGU 6

## Section 130

## Section 131

## Section 132

## Section 133

## Section 134 — Masimba Okubumba Mulawu Naamwe

Masimba nemishingo yeSeneti neGota lyabaMilili. Milawu yePalamende nenzila jokuyibumba. Kutanga kushinga koMulawu wePalamende. Kukwaliswa koMulawu wePalamende. Milawu ipo pasi peMilawu yePalamende. IMHINGU 7

## Section 135

## Section 136

## Section 137

## Section 138

## Section 139

## Section 140

## Section 141

## Section 142 — Unkwendeselo Wonshingo Mupalamende

UNtungamili wePalamende. Banhu banotungamila muPalamende. Kuwanda kwabanhu banodikana kuti unshingo utiwe muPalamende. Kushala nelulamilo yokuwhikwa muPalamende. Milawu yokushinga kwePalamende. Kulebeleka koNtungamili weNyika mupalamende neNdebo iye kuPalamende. Kuswika nokubhatikana kwabanji muPalamende. Kutambulika kwezwitiko zwomuPalamende. IMHINGU 8

## Section 143

## Section 144

## Section 145

## Section 146

## Section 147 — Bulefu Bwonshaji Nokuzakunulwa Kwepalamende

Bulefu bwonshaji nokuzakunulwa kwePalamende. Inshalujo ihulwana jinobuzwa nokuzakunulwa kwePalamende. Unshangano wokutanga wePalamende mushule mwenshalujo ihulwana. Mishangano nonshaji wokunyetutukwa. Kupela kwokushinga kwomulawu wafhulwa, iSundijilo, zwilijiko neimwe mishingo yePalamende.

MHINGU 9

Zwimwe Zwilosiyanasiyana Zwinanokuta Nepalamende Chikamu

## Section 148

## Section 149

## Section 150

## Section 151

## Section 152

## Section 153

## Section 154

Zwinowanika nezwinotabilijika mugota lyePalamende. Ilulamilo yokusenga chililijika kuPalamende. Bugalo bwokutila mishangano yePalamende. Ikomiti yemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende. IKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu. Imali jinoholwa noNtungamili weSeneti, uNdebelekeli neMhingu jePalamende. UnKwali wePalamende nabamwe bashing. CHISIYANISO 7 Inshalujo

Bulongwa Nezwitiko Zwenshalujo

## Section 155

## Section 156

## Section 157

Zwisungo zwobulongwa bwenshalujo. Kutiwa kwenshalujo namareferendamu. Milawu yeShalujo. IMHINGU 2

## Section 158

## Section 159 — Unshaji Wokutiwa Kwenshalujo

Unshaji wokutiwa kwenshalujo. Kuzhajikijwa kwezwibhula zwenshalujo. IMHINGU 3

## Section 160

## Section 161 — Kupambulanywa Kwemiganu Yenshalujo

Kuwanda kwemiganu inoshingiswa peshalujo namawadi. Kupambulanywa kwemiganu yeshalujo. CHISIYANISO 8

Ijudishiyali Namakota IMHINGU 1

## Section 162

## Section 172 — Undonganyilo Wamakota

Masimba eJudishuyali anova kubanhu beZimbabwe akale alopiwa makota, anoti. IJudishiyali. Kuzwimilila kwejudishiyali. Zwisungo zwinolulamisa ijudishiyali. IGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu. Unganu wemasimba eGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu. Igota lyopezhuluzhulu. Unganu wemasimba eGota lyopezhuluzhulu. Igota lyopezhulu. Unganu wemasimba eGota lyopezhulu. IGota lyezwemishingo.

Chikamu

## Section 173

## Section 174

## Section 175

## Section 176

IGota linobona nonkwendeselo wonshingo. Amwe makota namakota anobumbilwa kutonga inyaya jonshaji wakaleyo. Masimba aMakota pezhulu penyaya jozwebumbilo. Masimba osiyilwa eGota lyozweBumbilo, iGota lyopezhuluzhulu neGota lyopezhulu. IMHINGU 2

## Section 177

## Section 178

## Section 187

## Section 188 — Kugajikwa Nokumusilijwa Kwabamilili Bezokubetekeswa Kwemilandu

Bumhizha bwababetekesi beGota lyozweBumbilo. Bumhizha bwababetekesi beGota lyoPezhuluzhulu. Bumhizha bwababetekesi bemilandu yeGota lyopezhulu, iGota lyemishingo nawe Gota linobona nonkwendeselo wonshingo. Kudomwa kwababetekesi. Kuta unshingo wokubhatila zokubetekeswa kwemilandu. Kugajikwa kwababetekesi nabamwe bezokubeteswa kwemilandu. Bashingi bezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu besigajikwe pamakota anopinda limwempela. Kugajikwa kwabanobona nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu kunokwelela kutondeja bugali. Ikowhe jokutola chibhula. Unshaji wokushinga wehofesi yabatongi. Kubhiswa kwabatongi pemishingo. Mamilo onshingilo nombhatilo wabanolinga nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu. IMHINGU 3

## Section 189

## Section 190

## Section 191 — Igapo Lyezokubetekeswa Kwemilandu

Kusungulwa nokumiswa kwegapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu. Unshingilo wegota lyezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu. Kukwelanisa nokuba pachena konkwendeselo wegota lyezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu. IMHINGU 4

## Section 192

## Section 193 — Zwimwe Zwazo

Mulawo ulokwelela kukwendelejwa. Kubetekeswa kobugebenga mumakota.

CHISIYANISO 9

## Section 194

## Section 195

## Section 196

## Section 197

## Section 198 — Zwisungo Zokukwendelejwa Nokutungamilwa Kwabanji

Milawo ilokosha yokukwendelejwa kwabanji. Makampani ezwokutengeselana anolulamiswa nafulumende. Kuzwibhata kwabanoshingila fulumende nemilawu yobutungamili. Bulefu mashingilo echigalo chamakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende. Masimba okusimisa Chisiyaniso 9.

CHISIYANISO 10

Kushingila Fulumende Chikamu

## Section 199

## Section 200

## Section 201

## Section 202

## Section 203

## Section 204

## Section 205

Kushingila Fulumende. Kuzwibhata kweZwishingi zwaFulumende. IGolokota linomila Zwishingi zwaFulumende. Kumiswa nechimo cheKomishini yeZwishingi zwaFulumende. Kushinga kweKomishini yeZwishingi zwaFulumende. Bamilili benyika nabamwe balokosha banomilila iZimbabwe kunejimwe inyika. Bakwali bakulwana munhabi. CHISIYANISO 11

Igapo Lyezokulondotwa Kwenyika IMHINGU 1

## Section 206

## Section 207

## Section 208

## Section 209

## Section 210 — Zwilotulwa

Kulondotwa kwenyika. Igapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyika. Kuzwibhata kwabegapo lwezokulondotwa kwenyika. Ikansili yokulondotwa kwenyika. Inzila yokukwendeleja zwililijiko ilozwimila. IMHINGU 2

## Section 211

## Section 212

## Section 213

## Section 214

## Section 215

## Section 216

## Section 217

## Section 218 — Batabiliji Benyika

Batabiliji benyika. Unshingilo waBatabiliji beNyika. Kugajikwa kwBatabiliji beNyika mumatunhu alosiyanasiyana. Kumila kusengwa kwaBatabiliji beNyika kunoshingila kuzhe nabamili bezwamatongelo enyika. IGolokota lyenhabi yaBatabiliji beNyika. Kululamiswa kwaBatabiliji beNyika. Kutangwa nokushangana kweKomishini yemishingo yaBatabiliji beNyika. Mishingo yeKomishini yaBatabiliji beNyika. IMHINGU 3

## Section 219

## Section 220

## Section 221

## Section 222

## Section 223 — Unshingo Wamapolisa

Mapolisa nonshingo wawo. IGulukota lyenhabi inobona nezwokushinga kwaMapolisa. Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa. Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yaMapolisa. Unshingo weKomishini yaMapolisa. IMHINGU 4

## Section 224

## Section 225

## Section 226 — Mishingo Inolinga Nezwensiswa

Kumiswa kweMishingo inolinga nezwensiswa. IGulukota lyenhabi yezweMishingo inolinga nozwensiswa yenyika. Kululamisa kana kukwendelejwa kweMishingo inolinga nozwensiswa yenyika.

IMHINGU 5

## Section 227

## Section 228

## Section 229

## Section 230

## Section 231 — Majele Nokukololwa Kwabanota Milandu

Majele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu namashingilo awo. IGulukota lyenhabi yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. Komishina-Nkulugulu waMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. Mishingo yabomuMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. CHISIYANISO 12

Makapo Alozwimilila Oga Anobhancha Butongi Bwabanhu Banji IMHINGU 1

## Section 232

## Section 233

## Section 234

## Section 235

## Section 236

## Section 237 — Zwimwe Zwazo

MaKomishini alozwimila. Zwinangwa zwamaKomishini alozwimila. Bashingi bamaKomishini alozwimila. Lusungunuko lwamaKomishini. Begapo lyamaKomishini alozwimila banokwelela kusotola ibhazu kuna zwamatongelo enyika. Kungwina nokumiswa mishingo kwabegapo lyeKomishini ilozwimila. IMHINGU 2

## Section 238

## Section 239

## Section 240

## Section 241 — Ikomishini Yeshalujo Jomunyika yeZimbabwe

Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe. Mashingilo eKomishini yenshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe. Zwingakoneja kuti unhu engwine unshingo kuKomishini yezweshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe. IKomishini yeshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe inozibisa nezweshalujo nama referendamu. IMHINGU 3

## Section 242

## Section 243

## Section 244 — Ikomishini Yelulamilo Jabanhu bomuZimbabwe

Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe. Mashingilo eKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe. Zwizibiso kunda nokuva kuKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu. IMHINGU 4

## Section 245

## Section 246

## Section 247 — Ikomishini Yokukwelaniswa Kwabanhulume Nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe

Kugajikwa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe. Mashingilo eKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe. Zwizibiso zwilokwalwa zwinotiwa neKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe.

IMHINGU 5

## Section 248

## Section 249

## Section 250 — Ikomishini Yokushambajwa Kwendebo muZimbabwe

Kugajikwa nokumiswa kweKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo muZimbabwe. Mashingilo eKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo muZimbabwe. Zwizibiso zweKomishini yokushambajwa kwenyaya muZimbabwe zwilokwalwa. IMHINGU 6

## Section 251

## Section 252

## Section 253 — Ikomishini Yolunyalalo Nokulekejelana Munyika

Kugajikwa nokumiswa kweKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika. Kushinga kweKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika. Zwizibiso zwilokwalwa zweKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika. CHISIYANISO 13

Mabungano Okulwisa Buboli Nemilandu IMHINGU 1

## Section 254

## Section 255

## Section 256

## Section 257 — Ikomishini Inolwisa Buboli muZimbabwe

## Section 345 — Kupandulula

Mashingilo eMhingu 3. Zwimwe zwazwo zwingashingiswa pakupandulula iBumbilo. Imbhaunulo. Zwinotondekwa muZwisiyaniso, zwikamu nezwimwe. Mawhi aimo mubumwempela anoshanganisila mawhi aimo mubunji akale zwibwilulula alo. Zwitalwa nemisholo. Kuleba pezhulu ponshaji. Kuleba pezhulu pabashingi bezwibhula. Kuleba nozwePalamende. Kuchenjeja nokubhuzisisa. Kugajika unhu pechibhula. Kusiya chigalo nokuzwidila. Kutiwa kwemishingo nezwimwe. Kana unhu kuwhi aajilobhata unshingo wabanhu banji. Buwandi bwabanhu banobamba zwisungo nezwinobhuda pezhulu. Kunyenyejana pakati pamawhi omuBumbilo. ISHEJULU YOTANGA: Indembela yeNyika, Lwimbo lwenyika neMhande. ISHEJULU YABILI: Kujibilijwa kweLulamilo panshaji woMulawu wechimbichimbi muNyika. ISHEJULU YATATU: Ikowhe noKuvuma. ISHEJULU YECHINA: Zunodikana kuti unhu etubule kuvota. ISHEJULU YASHANU: Unkwala wamaBhili nejimwe iNyaya jomugota lyePalamende. ISHEJULU YASHANU NEMWEMPELA: Kutanga kushinga kweBumbilo, kusiyilana nokulondota.

INDEBO YOKUZHULA _________ Isu banhu beZimbabwe, Tikabhataniswa mukusiyana kwedu nechido chedu chimwempela cholusungunuko, bululami nokukwelana, akale nobugamba bwedu bwokulamba budyamiji, kushalulana zwikwendelana neganda nejimwe inzila jokusindamijya nobudyamiji, Titunhumwija nokukuja banhulume nabanhukaji bakabekazwimisila bakapila buoenyu bwabo muhondo yolusungunuko, Tilemekeja matategulu namamegulu nezwimwe zwizwalwa zwakashinga kasa kubuza imbhudililo munyika yedu, Tibona kuti panokwelela kusimiswa butongi bwabanji, bululami, kuta zwinhu nenzila ipo pachena akale nafulumende unotungamila zwinabululami nokutobeleja mulawu, Tisimatija kuzwipila kwedu mukutobeleja nokutabilija indulamo jabanhu jilokosha nolusungunuko lwabo, Tivuma kuti inyika yedu ilofuma nezwinowanika mwaili, Titunhumwija bubhodo bwamagalilo nobunhu bwedu, Tikazwipila kuzunda bukukutu bwose bunotabilija imbhudililo yedu, Tikoshesa lusungunuko, kukwelana kwabanhu, lunyalalo, kukwelaniswa konhu wose, kugajikana, kufuma nokuda inyika mukushaka imbhudililo impya nechinangwa chimwempela, Titunhumwija bukulugulu bwaMwali, unoba iye ulobhata bupenyu bwedu, Tinovumilana tishingisa milawu ilokwalwa mubumbilo yeli kuzwipila kubaka inyika ilobhatana, inobhata banhu bose zwibuyanana akale ilofuma, inokoshesa kuta zwinhu nenzila ipo pachena, kukwelana kwabanhu bose, lusungunuko, kutonga nokukobela zwilokwelana, bululami nokulemekejwa kokushinga nesimba, Akale, tikumbila kutungamilwa noMwali, tinobumba iBumbilo yeli akale tozwipila kwalili sondawu unkulwana weshango yeno inodikana.

CHISIYANISO 1 Zwisungo Zwilokosha 1

Inyika IZimbabwe inyika imwempela, inobutongi bwabanji akale ilozwimilila.


Bukulugulu bweBumbilo LyeMilawu

(1) Ibumbilo yeli ndiwo undawu unkulugulu weZimbabwe akale umwe undawu, kuta kana kuzwibhata kuloleyana nebumbilo akutoshinga zwikwendelana nokupambuka kwako pamulawu.

(2) Zwisungo zwilobigwa nebumbilo yeli zwinobhata unhu wose, unhu koga kana kuti wemilawu, kushanganisila inhabi jose jeNyika nenhabi jobutungamili bwenyika, inhabi inobumba milawu nenhabi yokubona kuti milawu yatobelejwa akale nenhabi jose jafulumende, akale zwinokwelela kuzhajikijwa najo. 3

Zwisungo zwilokosha

(1) IZimbabwe ilobumbwa pezhulu pokulemekejwa kwezwisungo zwinotobela—

bukulugulu bwebumbilo lyemilawu;

(c)

(g) kukwelana kwabechinhukaji nabechinhulume;

(h) butungamili bubuyanana; akale

(e)

kulemekejwa kwechisungo chokusiyaniswa kwamasimba;

Indembela, Lwimbo Lwenyika, Imhande jenyika

IZimbabwe inanendembela yenyika, lwimbo lwenyika, nemhande jenyika sezwilokwalwa mushejulu chokutanga. 5

Zwibabi zwafulumende Zwibabi zwafulumende weZimbabwe ngezwinotobela:

(a)

fulumende weshango yose;

(c)

butungamili bwamakuta nemisha, yezwi zwinoleba—

(i) makansili omumaguta, zwisinani namazina awo, anomilila nokubona unkwendeselo wobupenyu bwabanhu baimo mumakuta;

(ii) makansili okumisha, zwisinani namazina awo, anomilila nokubona unkwendeselo wobupenyu bwabanhu bokumisha nomubugalo bwomumatunhu.

(b) makansili omubugalo nomumakuta; no


Indimi

(1) Indimi jinotobela jinoti Chewa, ChiBarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda neXhosa indimi jinotambulwa zwipo pamulawu muZimbabwe.

(2) Mulawu wepalamende unotubula kusuma jimwe indimi sendimi jilotambulwa zwipo pomulawu akale unotubula kusuma lulimi lunoshingiswa mukukwala.

(3) INyika nejimwe inhabi jafulumende muzwibabi zwose jinokwelela:

(a)

kubona kuti indimi jose jilotambulwa zwipo pamulawu jinobhatwa zwinokwelana; akale,

(b) kulemekeja lulimi lwashalwa nabanhu balobhatikana nemilawu yafulumende.

(4) Inyika inokwelela kukutaija kushingiswa kwendimi jose jinoshingiswa muZimbabwe, kushanganisila lulimi lwokushingisa zwibabi zwombili akale inokwelela kubumba chimo chinovumila imbhudililo yendimi yeji. 7

Kukutaijwa kuzibwa kwezweBumbilo lyeMilawu kwabanhu bose

INyika inokwelela kukutaija kuti banhu bose bebe noluzibo lozweBumbilo lyeMilawu, kasa kasa—

(a)

kutolikila iBumbilo lyeMilawu kundimi jose jilotambulwa pamulawu akale nokuliswincha kubanhu banji kasa;

(b) kusuma kuti ibumbilo yeli lilayijwe muzwikolo akale libe chimwe chemhingu jezwilayijo chabashingi bokutabilijwa kwenyika, bashingi Iibafulumende nabashingi bamakapo anoshinga nabanhu;

(c)

kukutaija unhu wose namakapo ose, kushanganisila makapo anobona nozokugajikana kwabanhu kuti bebhanche mukulayija banhu bose nozwebumbilo yeli mubugalo bwose. CHISIYANISO 2 Zwibabalilo Zwenyika


Zwibabalilo zwichatungamila inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende

(1) Zwibabalilo zwilosumwa muchisiyaniso yechi zwinotungamilila inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende kumakapo ose mukubumbwa nokushingiswa kwemilawu ichatungamilila kubumbwa nokukutaijwa kokugajikana, lusungunuko nobutongi bwabanji kutila kuti banhu bose bewane imbhudililo, lushanto nobupenyu buzhele.

(2) Mukuwhisisa unshingo wenyika kutobelwa ibumbilo yeli kana kuti imwe milawu, zwibabalilo zwinotobela zwinokwelela kulemekejwa. 9

Butongi bulolulama

(1) INyika inokwelela kutobela nokushingisa milawu inokutaija kushinga nenzila ilokwelela akale ili iyo, kasa kasa inonyamula untolo zwipo pachena kulingwa munyaya jozwemali mumakapo ose afulumende.

(a)

kupiwa kwemishingo yokushingila banhu kunokwelela kutiwa kutobelejwa kutubula konhu;

(b) inzila jinokwelela kutiwa kuti kufumulwe, kutabilija kushingiswa kwenzila isili iyo nobubolo kwamasimba nayabo baipo pazwibhula zwamatongelo enyika kana kuti banoshingila banhu.

(2) INyika inolwelela kubona kuti inhabi jose jafulumende, kasa kasa maKomishini nejimwe inhabi jinoobumba kutobelwa ibumbilo yeli jinopiwa zwishingiso zwilokwelela kutila kuti etubule kuta mishingo yawo zubuyanana, zwilokwelela, zwinobulululami akale zwinandyo. 10

Kubhatana kwabanhu bose, lunyalalo nokugajikana

Inyika nonhu wose, kushanganisila bemilawu, nenhabi jose jafulumende banokwelela kukutaija kubhatana kwabanhu bose, lunyalalo nokugajikana. 11

Kusimiswa kwelulamilo jilokosha nolusungunuko lulokosha

Inyika inokwelela kuta zwose zwilokwelela kulondota ilulamilo nolusungunuko lulokosha zwilotulwa muChisiyaniso 4 akale ikutaije kushingiswa kwelulamilo nolusungunuko zwilopelela. 12

Zwisungo zwokushingilana nejimwe inyika/bokuzhe

(1) Zwisungo zokushungilana nejimwe inyika zwinokwelela kusendama pozunhu zunotobela—

(a)

kukutaija nokutabilija zwido zwenyika yeZimbabwe;

(c)

kugajikana zwinalunyalalo nejimwe inyika;

(b) kulemekeja milawu yopasi pose;

(d) kululamiswa kokukakabajana pakati penyika kushingiswa inzila jinalunyalalo.

(2) INyika inokwelela kusungwaja kulyijana namwe mashango omudunhu lyeAfrica, munyaya jechibanhu, jezwobufumi, kushingila pamwempela nomunyaya

jezwamatongelo akale kubhatikana munyaya jokubuza lunyalalo munyika jose jopasi nobunhu bwabo 13

Kubhudilila kwenyika

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kukweja kubuza imbhudililo nokukambija akale ilokwelela, akale ilokwelela kutola inzila—

(a)

jokukutaija banhu kuti bezwitangile zwinhu zwabo akale bezwibhanche boga;

(b) kukubusa imbhudililo munozokulima, zwokutengeselana, ma-indusitili, itekinoloji nesayensi;

(c)

(d)

Kukubusa imbhudililo yama-indisitalinokutengeselana kutila kuti banizi bomuZimbabwe bebenamasimba; akale kubuza imbhudililo inokwelana kubugalo bulosiyanasiyana muZimbabwe, kasa kasa kubuza imbhudililo inokwelanisa misha namaguta.

(2) Zwisungo zwatula muchibabi yechi zwinokwelela kukoka indangalilo jinova kubanhu mukubumba nokushinga kobulongwa bwembhudililo ilonangana nabo.

(3) Zwisungo zwilotulwa muchibabi yechi zwinokwelela kulondota nokukutaija indulamo jabanhu, kasa kasa banhukaji, kutila kuti banhu bose bewane chibaka chilkokwelela mumbhudililo.

(4) INyika inokwelela kubona kuti banizi bomumatunhu banowana bo kuva muzwinhu zwimo mubugalo bwabo. 14

Kupiwa kwamasimba nokubumbwa kwemishingo

(1) Inyika nenhabi jose jafulumendejinokwelela kukweja kukutaija akale kutola zwisungo zwokuti banhu, makapo namatunhu ose alosalila mumbhudililo epiwe masimba kushingiswa inzila jilokwelela, jipopachena, jinokwelanisa banhu akale jinolemekejwa mubugalo bwenyika yeZimbabwe.

(2) Unshaji uli wose, inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kubona kuti inzila jilokwelela akale jilokwana jinotolwa kubumba mishingo yezwizwalwa zwose zwomunyika yeZimbabwe, kasa kasa banhukaji nabachinokubuka. 15

Kulya kunji Inyika inokwelela—

(a)

kukutaija banhu kuti belime akale belondete kulya kunokwana;

(c)

kukutaija nokusimatija kulya kunobaka umbili akale kulokwana kushingiswa zwilayijo zwabanhu bose akale nejimwe inzila.

(b) kubambanya kulondotwa kokulya kokushingisa mumazhuba anoozha; akale


Magalilo

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutunhumwija nokulondota zwisungo zwamagalilo zwinokutaija kulemekejwa kugajikana nokukwelana kwezwizwalwa zomuZimbabwe.

(2)INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende nozuzwalwa zwose zomuZimbabwezwinokwelela kolondota magalilo enyika yeZimbabwe.

(3) Inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kubona kuti zwechibanhu zwinolondotejwa nokulemekejwa.

Kukwelaniswa kwabechinhukaji nabechinhulume

(1) iNyika inokwelela kukutaija kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji muZimbabwe, kasa kasa—

(a)

iNyika inokwelela kuktaija kubhatikana kwabanhukaji mu,apazu ose enyika yeZimbabwe kukalimgwa kuti banokwelana nabanhulume;

(b) iNyika inokwelela kushingisa inzila jose, kushanganisila kubumbwa kwemilawu, inodikana kubona kuti——

(i) banhukaji nabanhulume banomililwa zwilokwelana mumapazu nenhabi jafulumende; akale

(ii) banhukaji banobeli chikamu makuni mashanu muzana mumakomishini ose nezimwe zwibabi zwafulumende zwinaneminhu jinoshalwa kana kudomwa jinobumbwa kutobela iBumbilo yeli kana Mulawu wePalamende; akale

(c) iNyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutola inzila jose kuti banhukaji betubule kuwana zwibumbwa zwinoshanganisila ivhu, nenzila inokwelana nabanhulume.

(2) INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jinoboneka kukolola kushalujwa kwabanhukaji nabanhulume nokusokwelaniswa kwabo pezhulu pemilawu nezwitiko zwantolo. (2) Inyika inokwelela kutola inzila jinoboneka kukolola kushalujwa kwabanhukaji nabanhulume nokusokwelaniswa kwabo pezhulu pemilawu nezwitiko zwantolo. 18

Kumililwa kwamatunhu zwilokwelana

(1) INyika inokwelela kusungwaja kumililwa zwilokwelana kwamatunhu ose enyika yeZimbabwe munhabi jilosiyna jafulumende.

(2) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutola inzila jinoboneka kubona kuti matunhu ose anotubula kuwana zwibumbwa zwinowabhancha kubhudilila nenzila ilokwelela. 19

Bana

(1) Inyika inokwelela kutola zwisungo matano okusimisa kuti munyaya jilonangana nabana, kulemekejwe zwinokwendelana nabana.

(2) Inyika inokwelela kutola zwisungo nenzila, kukalingwa zushingiso zwipo, kubona kuti bana—

(a)

banowana kulondotwa nemhuli kana kuti bazwali, kana kuti kubhatwa zwibuyanana kana babhiswa mumuli jabo;

(b) banawana pokugala akale banowana kulya kulokwelela, bushanana bulokwelela akale nokubhatwa kubuyanana mubupenyu bwabo;

(c)

balotabilijwa mukubhatwa bubi, kulekelelwa kana kuti jimwe inzila jokushungulujwa; akale

(d) banotubula kuwana indayijo ilolulama.

(3) Inyika inokwelela kutola matano emilawu alokwelela nozumwe zwisungo—

(a)

kutabilija bana mukutishingiswa kusilolulama; akale

(b) kusimatija kuti bana abatokwelela akale abatovumilwa kuta mishingo—

(i) isolokwelela makole abo okuzwalwa; kana kuti

(ii) kubiga pengozi bupenyu bubuya bwabana, indayijo, busimu bwemibili yabo kana kuti indangalilo kana kuti umpepo, kana kuti bupenyu bwabo bwamazhuba ose. 20

Bachinokubuka

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutola matano alokwelela, kushanganisila alosendamila kunabachinokubuka kutila kuti bachinokubuka, kulebe banhu banamakalo apo pakati pegumi namashanu kunoswika makole makumi matatu anamashanu—

(a) banotubula kuwana indayijo ilokwelela;

(b) banotubula kuwana unshaji wokushangana akale nechokumililwa munezwamatongelo enyika, mukugajikana, munozwobufumi nozumwe zunanokuta nobupenyu;

(c)

banotubula kuwana unshaji kumishingo nezwimwe zwinobapa masimba obufumi;

(d) banotubula kuwana unshaji wokuzwibalayija akale nezwishingiso zwokuzwibalayija; akale

(e)

banotabilijwa kuzwitiko zwamagalilo zwinanengozi, kushingiswa kusulolulama nenzila jose jokushungulujwa.

(2) Mulawu wepalamende ungabiga chimwe chilongwa kana zwimwe zwilongwa zwabachinokubuka.

(3) Zwisungo nezwilongwa zwadomwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) na (2) zwinobhatanisa, zwisinolelekela ibhazu akale zwikalingana nenyika yose. 21

Bakwegula

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutola inzila jilokwelela, kushanganisila, kulondota kulemekejwa, kubhanchwa nokutabilijwa kwabakwegula akale kubatubusa kuti bebhatikane mubupenyu bwobugalo bwabo.

(2) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kukweja, kutobelwa zwishingiso zwipo—

(a) kusungwaja bakwegula kuti bebhatikane zwizhele muzwitiko zwomubugalo bwabo;

(b) kuwanisa zwishingiso zwinodikana, kulya nolumwe lubhancho lunodikana kubanhu bakwegula banolushaka;

(c)

kubumba bulongwa bunopa bakwegula chibaka chokubhatikana muzwitiko zwinobhancha zwinokwendalana nokutubula kwabo akale zwinokwendelana nezwido zwabo; akale

(d) kukutaija makapo anobona nezwokugajikana kwabanhu jilonangana nokubuza imbhudililo mubupenyu bunolalamwa nabakwegula. 22

Banhu banabukosha

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutola inzila jilokwelela, kushanganisila, kulondota kulemekejwa, kubhanchwa nokutabilijwa kwabakwegula akale kubatubusa kuti bebhatikane mubupenyu bwobugalo bwabo.

(2) Inyika nejimwe inhabi jafulumende, zwikwendelana nokutubula kwafulumende, inokwelela kubhancha banhu banabukosha bwombili kana kuti bwendangalilo kuti betubule kubhatikana nenzila ilopelela akale kuti kudelejwe bukukutu bwabanoshangana nabwo.

(3) Kasa kasa, inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela(a)

kubumba bulongwa bwabanhu banabukosha bwombili kana kuti bwendanganlilo, kasa kasa bulongwa bwemishingo bunokwendelana nokutubula kwabo akale yabanotubula kutambula kana kuti ingatambulwa nabamilili babo bezwemilawu;

(b) kulingisisa zwinodiwa nabanobukosha bwenduji jose sezumwe zwilokosha mubulongwa bwembudililo;

(c) kusungwaja nokubumba inzila jokulebelekisana jilolulamilabanhu banabukosha bwombili kana kuti bwendangalilo; akale

(d) kusungwaja makapo anoda kubhancha imbhudililo mubupenyu bwabanhu banobukosha bulosiyana.

(4) INyika inokwelela kutola zwisungo zwilokwelela kubona kuti zwibakwa nezwimwe zwishingiso zwilokosha zwinoshingiswa nabanhu banji zwinotubulika kuti zwishingiswe nabanobukosha. 23

Bakalwila iHondo yoLusungunuko

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende zwinokwelela kupa lulemekejo kumagamba ehondo yolusungunuko. Yezwi zwinoleba—

(a) yabo bakalwila ihondo yolusungunuko;

(b) yabo bakabhancha balwi behondo yolusungunuko; akale

(c) yabo bakasungwa, kuvalilwa kana kusungwa pezhulu penyaya jamatongelo enyika ponshaji wehondo yolusungunuko.

(2) INyika inokwelela kutola matano alolulama, kushanganisa matano emilawu, okubona kuti balwi behondo yolusununguka banowana bupenyu bubuya akale banowana masimba obufumi. 24

Mishingo nokulyijana kumishingo

(1) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutula zwisungo nenzila, kutobelwa kutubula kukwe, kutubusa unhu wose kuti ewane bo chibaka chokuwana unshingo muchitiko chaashala nokukwe, kutila kuti etubule kuzwibhancha iye nemhuli iye.

(2) INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kukweja kubona kuti—

(a) unshingo ulopelelela;

(b) kubhisa zwitabilijo zwinojibija zwinabudyamiji kana kuti zwinotabilija banhu kuti beshinge kana kuti bete zwimwe zwingababhancha munozobufumi;

indayijo yezwimishingo yabangashantila akale nokubumba bulongwa bwezwilayijo zwemishingo yamaboko yabangashantila, kushanganisila yobo bunobhancha banabukosha; akale

(d) kushingiswa kwezwisungo zwinanga zwokulondotwa kwemhuli zwinotubusa banhukaji kuti bewane chibaka chechokomende chokushinga.

(c)


Kutabilijwa kwemhuli

Inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende, sokutubula kwajo, jinokwelela kutabilija nokusungwaja kubhatana kubuyanana kwemhuli, akale itole zwisungo—

(a) zwokuwanisa lubhancho kumamai, matate nejimwe imhingu jemhuli jinolondota bana;

(b) kutabilija kushungulujwa kopasha. 26

Kulobolana INyika inokwelela kutola zwisungo zwokubona kuti—

(a)

apana kulobolana kunotikana pesina kuvumilana kunalusununguko akale kuzhele pakati pabamulobolana;

(b) bana abatopilwa kuti belobolwe;

(c)

kunakukwelana kwelulamilo mumishingo pakati pabalolobolana;

(d) pechitiko chokupalajika kokulobolana, pezhulu polufu kana kushimana, panokwelela kuba nezwinotikana kutabilija bana kana kuti unkaji kana undume. 27

Indayijo

(1) INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jose jinotubulika kusungwaja—

(a)

indayijo isinolipwa akale akale inopiwa mwana wose; akale

(b) indayijo yopezhulu pezwilayijo zwesekondali.

(2) INyika inokwelela kutola matano ose kubona kuti banhukaji banowana unshaji unokwelana nabanhulume mukuwana indayijo muzwibabi zwose. 28

Pokugala

INyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kutola zwisungo zwemilawo nezwimwe bo, mukutubula kwafulumende, kutubusa unhu wose kuti ewane pokugala palokwana. 29

Zwobuchilo

(1) INyika inokwelela kutola matano anotubulika okuwanisa bushanana bulokwela, bunowinikwa zwisna bukukutu akale bulokwana iZimbabwe yose.

(2) INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jilokwelela, jinokwelanisa unhu wose akale pasina unhu unojibiswa kuwana kulapwa kana awilwa nengozi inoda kulapwa nokukambija ungaba upi wawo unsha wobuchilo .

(3) INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jimo mumasimba ayo, jinoshanganisila kulayija nobulongwa bwokuzibisa banhu, kutabilija kutabukijwa kobulwaji. 30

Lubhancho kunabanolushaka

INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jose jinotubulika, akale itobeleja zwiwanikwa zwainotubula kushingisa, kupa lubhancho lwokulondota nolwobupenyu kuna yabo banolushaka. 31

Lubhancho penyaya jemilawu

INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jose jinotubulika, akale itobeleja zwiwanikwa zwainotubula kushingisa, kuwanisa bumilili bwezwemilawu munyaya jemilandu nejili jemilandu kubanhu banobushaka koga besinotubula kuzwiwanila bamilili bezwemilawu bokuzwishalila. 32 Mizano nobugalo bwokuzwibalaija INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jose jinotubulika kusungwaja mizano nokuzwibalayija, zwinoshanganisila kuwanisa zwishingiswa nezwibakwa zwemizano

nokuzwibalayija kubanhu bose. 33

Kulondotwa koluzibo lwechibanhu

INyika inokwelela kutola inzila jokulondota, kutabilija nokusungwaja luzibo lwechibanhu, kushanganisla luzibo lokulapa nozumwe bo zunanokuta nemhuka nemiti zwinowanikwa nananhu bomumatunhu. 34

Kutambulwa kwemilawu ilovumilwana nenyika jilosiyana

Inyika inokwelela kubona kuti milawu nezwivumilwana zose zwilovumilwana nenyika jinoshanganisila iZimbabwe inoshanganisilwa mumilawu yenyika yeno. CHISIYANISO 3 Bunizi 35

Bunizi muZimbabwe

(1) Banhu banizi muZimbabwe pezhulu pokuzwalwa, ibhatuko kana kuti kukwalisa.

(2) Bose banizi banokwelela kuwana ilulamiko, jinokwelana akale bananonshingo wabanizi unokwelana.

(3) Banizi bose bomuZimbabwe bananelulamiko yokuwana zunhu zunotobela, kushanganisila jimwe bo jabangapiwe nomulawu—

(a)

kutabilijwa nobutongi bwenyika kungaba kupi kwabanobeli;

(c)

iwhalo yokuzwalwa kana kuti chimwe chitupa chinopiwa nobutongi bwenyika.

(b) kuba nepasipoti kana imwe iwhalo jokukwendesa;

(4) Banizi bomuZimbabwe bananemishingo inotobela kushanganisila imwe yabangapiwe nemilawu—

(a)

kuzwipila kunyika yeZimbabwe;

(c)

kulemekeja indembela yeshango nolwimbo lweshango; noku

(b) kutobeleja iBumbilo lyeMilawu akale kulemekeja zwalinomilila

(d) mukutubula kwabo kopezhululuzhulu, kutabilija inyika yeZimbabwe nokuzwimilila kwayo. 36

Bunizi bwokuzwalwa

(1) Banhu banizi bomuZimbabwe nokuzwalwa kana bakazwalwa muZimbabwe akale ponshaji wokuzwalwa kwabo—

(a) tate kana amai bakabeli unizi womuZimbabwe;

(b) tategulu kana kuti amegulu wakabeli unizi womuZimbabwe nokuzwalwa kana kuti nebhatuko.

(2) Banhu bakazwalilwa kuzhe kwenyika yeZimbabwe banizi bomuZimbabwe kana, pabakazwalwa, umwe wabazwali wabo wakabeli unizi womuZimbabwe akale—

(a) unshaji unji wakabegala muZimbabwe;

(b) wakabeshingila inyika kana kuti igapo linoshanganisila inyika inji eli kuzhe kwenyika.

(3) Mwana unowanikwa muZimbabwe unamakole kana kuti unoboneka soti unamakole apo pasi pegumi anamashanu, akale usinozibikana kuti unizi wokupi

nabazwali babe besinozibikana, unotambulwa sonizi womuZimbabwe nokuzwalwa. 37

Bunizi bwebhatuko/bwechinde

Kutobelejwa chibabi 36(2), banhu bakazwalwa kuzhe kwenyika yeZimbabwe banizi bomuZimbabwe nozwebhatuko/ichinde kana ponshaji wokuzwalwa—

(a)

umwe wabazwali kana kuti tategulu kana amegulu babe bakabeli unizi womuZimbabwe nokuzwalwa kana kuti nebhatuko; kana

(b) umwe wabazwali wakabeli unizi womuZimbabwe nokukwalisa; akale kuzwalwa kulokwaliswa muZimbabwe kutobelejwa milawu ilonangana nokukwaliswa kokuzwalwa. 38

Bunizi bokukwalisa

(1) Unhu upi zwe ulolobola kana kulobolwa nonizi womuZimbabwe kwamakole mashanu kana kupinda, zwingaba ibumbilo yeli lisanu lyashinga kana kuti mushule mwokutanga kushinga kwalyo, akale ezhajikija zwinodiwa nomulawu wepalamende, unanelulamiko, mushule mwokukumbila, kukwalisa kuba unizi womuZimbabwe.

(2) Unhu upi zwe wabegala muZimbabwe zwipo pomulawu kwamakole igumi kana kupinda, kungaba ibumbilo yeli lisanu lyatanga kushinga kana kuti mushule mwokutanga kwalyo kushinga, akale ezhajikija zwinodiwa nomulawu wepalamende, unanelulamiko, mushule mwokukumbila, kukwalisa kuba unizi womuZimbabwe.

(3) Mwana usili unizi womuZimbabwe, koga eli atolwa akale elelwa nabazwali banizi muZimbabwe, zwingaba ibumbilo yeli lisanu lyatanga kushinga kana kuti mushule mwokutanga kwalyo kushinga, unanelulamiko, mushule mwokukumbila, kukwalisa kuba unizi womuZimbabwe. 39

Kutolelwa bunizi

(1) Bunizi bwomuZimbabwe bwokukwalisa bungatolelwa unhu kana—

(a)

kana unhu wadeni eli akawana bunizi nenzila jisilolulama, kuleba maniyepwa kana kuti kusisa zumwe zwinokwelela kuzibwa; kana

(b) ponshaji wehondo inoshanganisila iZimbabwe, unhu wadeni wakabetengeselana kana kulebelekisana nombengi kana kuti wakata kana kulyijana nemishingo yakata eziba kuti umutila kubhancha umbengi muhondo yeyo.

(2) Bunizi bwomuZimbabwe bwokuzwalwa bungatolelwa unhu kana—

(a)

kana bunizi bwadeni bwakawanwa nezila jisilolulama, kuleba maniyepwa kana kusisa zumwe zungada kuzibikanwa; kana

(b) penyaya yonhu walebwa muna 36(3), bunizi kana bazwali bonhu yoyu bakazibikanwa, akale zwikatondeja kuti unhu yoyu unizi weimwe inyika.

(3) Bunizi bwomuZimbabwe abutokwelela kutolelwa unhu kutobelejwa chibabi yechi kana kuli kuti unhu yoyo unoosala esina ishango. 40

Kundilila mbeli uli unizi mushule mwokulobola kana kulobolwa kana kupalajika kokulobolana

Bunizi bwomuZimbabwe abutolashika neyaya yokulobolana kana kupalajika kokulobalana. 41 Ibhodi yobunizi nokungwina kwabanhu munyika Mulawu wepalamende unokwelela kusuma kubumbwa kwebhodi yezobunizi nokungwina kwabanhu munyika, inoshanganisila ungalichibhula nejimwe imhingu imbili kana kupinda jinodomwa noNtungamili wenyika. Ibhodi yeyi inanshingo—

(a)

wokupa kana kutolela bunizi bwokukwalisa;

(c)

wokuta imwe mishingo ingapiwa ibhodi nomulawu wepalamende.

(b) wokuvumila banhu, basili banizi bomuZimbabwe, kugala nokushinga muZimbabwe akale nokusuma unshaji nezwisungo zwokugala nokushinga kwabo; akale


Masimba ePalamende penyaya jobunizi

Mulawu wePalamende ungabumba zwisungo, zwinokwendelana nechisiyaniso yechi unolungulula—

(a)

inzila jokutobeleja mukuwana bunizi bwokukwalisa;

(c)

inzila jokutobeleja mukutolela unhu bunizi bwamoZimbabwe bwokukwalisa;

(b) kuzwilambila woga bunizi bwomuZimbabwe;

(d) kubwilizila unhu bunizi bwomuZimbabwe;

(e)

(f) 43

kujibisa bunizi bwenyika imbili kubabanizi bebhatuko kana kuti bokukwalisa; akale kupa kuti chisiyaniso yechi chitange kushinga.

Kundlila mbeli kana kubwilizila bunizi bwakabelipo

(1) Unhu wose uli unizi womuZimbabwe mazhuba akalawao amu mushule mwelokutanga kushinga kwebumbilo yeli, unondilila mbeli eli unizi womuZimbabwe mushule mwokutanga kushinga kwebumbilo yeli.

(2) Unhu wose wakazwalwa muZimbabwe kusanu kwatanga kushinga ibumbilo yeli unizi muZimbabwe kana—

(a)

umwe kana kuti bose bazwali babe beli unizi munyika yakaba imhingu yenyika jokuChamhembe yakabumbwa nechivumilwano chakasainwa kuWindhoek munyika yeNamibia unsi wa17 iGotonga mugole lya1992; akale

(b) wakabegala muZimbabwe ponshaji wokutanga kushinga kwebumbilo yeli. CHISIYANISO 4 Kubhaunulwa Kwelulamilo IMHINGU 1 Kushinga Nokuwhisisa Chisiyaniso 4 44

Unshingo wokulemekeja ilulamilo nolusungunuko lukulugulu

Inyika nonhu wose, kushanganisila banhu banoshinga kubetekesa, nenhabi jose jafulumende jinokwelela kulemekeja, kutabilija, kusungwaja nokuzhajikija ilulamilo nolusungunuko lulodomwa muchisiyaniso yechi. 45 Mashingilo eChisiyaniso 4

(1) Chisiyaniso yechi chinosunga inyika nenhabi jose jafulumende jobutungamili bwenyika, jokubumbwa kwemilawu nejokutobelejwa kwemilawu.

(2) Chisiyaniso yechi chinosunga banhu koga nabemilawu zwikwendelana nokuti inoshingiswa kwabali, kulingisiswa chimo chendulamo kana lusungunuko lulonangwa akale nemishingo yalunobapa.

(3) Banhu banobetekesa milawu nabanhu koga balosungilwa kuwana ilulamilo nolusungunuko lulodomwa muchisiyaniso yechi zwikwendelana nokuti ilulamilo nolusungunuko yolo lunoswinchika zwibuyanana kwabali. 46

Kuwhisisa Chisiyaniso 4

(1) Mukuwhisisa chisiyaniso yechi, igota kana imwe inhabi—

(a)

inokwelela kubona kuti ilulamilo nolusungunuko zwimo muchisiyaniso yechi zwinotobelejwa zwizhele;

(b) inokwelela kusungwaja indangalilo nezwisungo zwinobumba ishango inabutongi bwabanji zwilosendama pakubhudila pachena, kubhatwa zwilolulama, kulemekeja bukulugulu bonhu, kukwelana kwabanhu nolusungunuko, akale kasa kasa ndangalilo nezwisungo zwatulwa muchibabi 3;

(c)

inokwelela kulangalila milawu inoshinga munyika inji nezwimwe zwisungo zwenyika inji zwinoshanganisila ishango yeZimbabwe;

(d) inokwelela etobeleje nenzila ilokwelela zwisungo zwebumbilo yeli, kasa zwibabalilo nezwinangwa zwilotulwa muchisiyaniso 2; akale

(e)

ingalangalila milawu yokunejimwe nyika inokwendelana nechisiyaniso yechi;

pezhulu pokulangalila zwimwe bo zinokwendelana nokuwhisisa ibumbilo lyemilawu.

(2) Mukuwhisisa milawu, akale mukuumbumba milawu inoshingiswa nabatongi neyebhatuko, makota ose nenhabi jose jokubetekesa milawu jinokwelela kusungwaja nokutungamilwa nezwishubo nezwibabalilo zwechisiyaniso yechi. 47

Chisiyaniso yechino achitojibija jimwe ilulamilo

Chisiyaniso yechi achitojibija jimwe ilulamilo nolumwe lusungunuko lungatambulwa kana kupiwa nondawu, nenzila inokwendelana neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli. IMHINGU 2 ILULAMILO NOLUSUNGUNUKO LUKULUGULU 48

Ilulamilo yobupenyu

(1) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yobupenyu.

(2) Mulawu ungavumila kuti untongo wolufu upiwe chete kana unhu eli awanikwa eli nondandu wokubulaya umwe elinechinangwa chokubulaya, akale—

(a)

mulawu unokwelela kuvumila igota kuti lizwiyeyele loga kuti lipe untongo yoyu kana kuti ne;

(b) untongo yoyu ungatiwa kutobelejwa untongo wopejeleja wapiwa negota lilolulama;

(c)

untongo yoyu autokwelela kupiwa unhu—

(i) wakabeli namakole aipo pasi pamakumi mabili

(ii) unamakole anopinda makumi mashanu anamakumi mabili;

(d) untongo yoyu autokwelela kupiwa unhu wechinhukaji; akale

(e)

unhu watongelwa lufu unanelulamilo yokukumbila kuNtungamili weNyika lulekejelo kana kuti epiwe untongo undelu.

(3) Mulawu wepalamende unokwelela kutabilija bupenyu bwabana basanu bazwalwa, akale mulawu yoyu unokwelela kusuma kuti imimba ingabhiswa kana kutobelejwa mulawu wakalewo chete. 49

Ilulamilo yolusungunuko lonhu

(1) Unhu wose unanendulamo yolusungunuko lulwe, kushanganisila ilulamiko—

(a) yokusobhatwa mubusungwa besinobeteswa; akale

(b) yokusotolelwa lusungunuko lwabo nesimba akale pesina chisoko jinoboneka.

(2) Apana unhu angavalilwa muntolongo pezhulu pokukonwa kuzhajikija chivumilano chaakata. 50

Ilulamilo jabanhu basungwa nabalobhatwa mubusungwa

(1) Unhu wasungwa—

(a)

unokwelela kubujwa ponshaji wokusungwa kukwe chisoko chokusungwa;

(b) unokwelela kuvumilwa, pesina kujilwa—

(i) kulebelekisana nondume kana unkaji kana igamu kana umilili wezwemilawu, kana umwe unhu waanoda kuzibisa kushingiswa inzila ichalipwa nafulumende; akale

(ii) kuwana lubhancho lwomulili wezwemilawu kana dokotela wanoda musiswa akale eli iye unsungwa uchalipa;

akale unokwelela kuzibiswa ilulamilo yeyi pakalepo;

(c) unokwelela kubhatwa sonhu akale kulemekejwa mubunhu bubwe;

(d) unokwelela kusunungulwa pesina zwitabilijo akale pezwisungo zwinotobelejeka, kumilila kubetekeswa, ado kuli nezwisoko zwinobhata nesimba kuti endilile mbeli eli mubusungwa; akale

(e) unokwelela nokukwe kuvumilwa kunyenyeja kululama kokusungwa kukwe mugota akale unokwelela kusunungulwa pakalepo kana kusungwa kukwe kusili pamulawu.

(2) Unhu wasungwa kana ulobhatwa mubusungwa—

(a)

nechinangwa chokuti esengwe kugota; kana

(b) nokuti umutamijwa undandu;

akale usanu asunungulwa unokwelela kusengwa kugota nokukambija akale kojoba zwikaba chini kusanu kwapinda ma awa makumi mana anamashanu namatatu mushule mwokuti kusungwa kana kubhatwa kukwe mubusungwa kwatanga, zwikwendelana nezwimutikana, zwisinani kuti unshaji yoyo unopela noMugibelo, neNsondo kana kuti pezhuba lyokunyetutukwa.

(3) Unhu upi azwe uchaleka kubuzwa kugota mukati mwama awa makumu mana anamashanu namatatu alebwa muna (2) pezhulu unokwelela kusunungulwa nokukambija kuzhe kokuti kubhatwa kukwe mubusungwa kuli kwavumijwa negota linolemekejwa.

(4) Unhu upi azwe wasungwa kana kuti ulobhatwa mubusungwa pezhulu pokutamijwa undandu unanelulamilo—

(a)

yokugala ekanyalala; akale

(c)

kusobhatwa nesimba kuzwifhija kana kuvuma undandu; akale

2013

(b) yokuzibiswa pakalepo—

(i) ilulamilo yiye yokugala ekanyalala; akale

(ii) zwingatobela kugala bekanyala kana kusonyalala kukwe;

(d) paanoma mbeli kwegota kwokutanga, kuzibiswa undandu waakumutamijwa kana kuzibiswa zwisoko zwokuti neni ekwelela kundilila mbeli ekabhatwa mubusungwa kana kusunungulwa.

(5) Unhu upi azwe ulobhatwa mubusungwa, kushanganisla basungwa balotongwa kale, unanelulamilo—

(a)

kuzibiswa pakalepo chisoko chokubhatwa mubusungwa kukwe;

(c)

kulebelikisana akale kushanyilwa—

(b) eshingisa imali jije, kulebelekisana musiswa nomilili wemilawu waazwishalila akale kuzibiswa ilulamilo yeyi pakalepo;

(i) nonkaji kana undume;

(ii) igamu;

(iii) umbhanchi wezwokudimbwa waazwishalila;

(iv) umilili wezwemilawu waazwishalila;

(v) undapi wokuzwishalila;

(vi) kutobelejwa zwisungo zwinowhisisika zwilobigwa kutila kuti majele nobugalo bwokulondota balobhatwa mubusungwa bushinge zwibuyanana, umwe unhu zwe waazwishalila;

(d) yokubhatwa nenzila yokubhatwa mubusungwa inokwendelana nokulemekeja bukulugulu bwobunhu, kushanganisila ushanji wokuta mizano inosimisa umbili akale, kushingiswa indipo yobutongi bwenyika, pokugala palokwana, zwimbuzi nezwishambilo, bushanana, kulya kubuya, iwhalo jilokwelela jokubala nokulapwa; akale

(e)

kunyenyeja kululama kokusungwa kukwe mugota akale, kana kusungwa kukwe kusili pomulawu, kusunungulwa pakalepo.

(6) Unhu upi azwe ulobhatwa mubusungwa ekamilila kubetekeswa undandu waakumutamijwa akale usanu abetekeswa mukati mwonshaji unotambulika unokwelela kusupulwa mubusungwa, zwizhele kana kuti kutobelwa zwisungo zwinotambulika kutila kuti mushule mwokusupulwa—

(a)

unozha kobetekeswa;

(c)

aatopama umwe undandu kubetekeswa kusanu kwatanga.

(a)

yokuti unhu ulobhatwa mubusungwa esupulwe, kana kuti ebuzwe kugota kuti kubonekwe kululama kokubhatwa kukwe mubusungwa, kana kuti kudombolwe pachena bugalo kwalobhatilwa mubusungwa; kana

(b) aatolundula busumikiji buchoopiwa pokubetekeswa; akale

(7) Kana pelinezwinotisa kuti pebe nokudimbwa kuti unhu ulobhatwa mubusungwa zwisipo pamulawu kana kuti azwijimutubulika kuti kuzibikane kuti unhu ulobhatwa mubusungwa uko kupi, unhu upi azwe unotubula kunda kuGota igulwana lye kunoshaka isumo—

(b) kusuma kuti kubhatwa mubusungwa yoku akupo pamulawu akale kusuma kuti unhu ulobhatwa mubusungwa esupulwe pakalepo;

akale iGota iGulwana lingatola chisungo chipi zwacho chilokwelela zwikwendelana nechimo.

(8) Kusunga kana kubhata mubusungwa kunokakabajana nechibabi yechi kana kuti kusinotobeleja zwisungo zwechibabi yechi akupo pomulawu.

(9) Unhu upi zwe wasungwa kana wabhatwa mubusungwa zwisipo pomulawu unanelulamilo yokulipwa nonhu yoyo wonsungisa kana kuti wombhatisa mubusungwa, koga undawu ungatabilija banhu banotobela kuti belipe kutobelejwa chibabi yechi—

(a)

bashingi bezwemilawu beta unshingo wabo wemilawu nenzila inabululami;

(b) unhu upi azwe unoshingila banji akale eta yezwi nenzila inabululami akale esina butela. 51

Ilulamilo yokulemekejwa sonhu

Unhu wose unanobunhu bunolemekejwa mwaali mubupenyu bubwe bwesiswa nayobo bunozibwa nonhu wose, akale ilulamilo yokuti bunhu yobu bulemekejwe akale butabilijwe. 52

Ilulamilo yokutabilijwa konhu

Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokulemekejwa kombili nendangalilo jije, kushanganisila—

(a)

kutabilijwa kunduji jilosiyana joluzha jingava mubunji bwabanhu kana musiswa;

(b) kutobelwa zwimwe zwisungo zweBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, kuzwiyeyela penyaya jokuzwala;

(c)


kusoshingiswa mushakisiso jokulapa kana jesayensi, kana kuti kutolwa kwenyama jombili kana kushingiswa kwajo iye esavuma eziba kuti ini zwimutikana.

Lusungunuko pokushungulujwa kana kubhatwa nenzila kana kulangwa nenzila inolelupisa bunhu

Apana unhu angabhatwa nenzila inoshunguluja umbili kana indangalilo kana kuti kubhatwa kana kulangwa nenzila inolelupisa bunhu bubwe kana isilokwelela unhu. 54

Lusungunuko kubutapwa Apana umhu angabatwa mubutapwa kana kuba untapwa.


Lusungunuko mukuta unshingo wokubhatwa nesimba Apana unhu unokwelela kuta unshingo wokubhatwa nesimba.


Kukwelana nokushalujwa

(1) Banhu bose banokwelana mbeli komulawu akale banelulamilo inokwelana yokutabilijwa nomulawu akale nokubhatwa zwinokwelana.

(2) Banhukaji nabanhulume banelulamilo yokubhatwa zwinokwelana, kushanganisila ilulamilo yokupiwa unshaji e zwinokwelana munazwamatongelo enyika, munozobufumi, zwamagalilo nobupenyu zwabo.

(3) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokusoshalujwa nenzila isilolulama kushingiswa zwinhu zwinanga bunizi, iganda, luji, bugalo bwokuzwalwa, kwaunova, lulimi mamililo munozobufumi, kudimbwa, zwamatongelo enyika zwaanotobeleja, miyeyo, magalilo, bunhulume kana bunhukaji, chimo chokulobolwa, makole,

(4) Unhu unowhi wabhatwa nenzila inoshaluja kutobelejwa (3) kana—

(a)

abhatwa nenzila ipo pachena kana ilosisika muchimo, chitabilijo kana bugosha nenzila isinotiwa kunabamwe banhu.

(b) bamwe banhu banopiwa zwipo pachena kana nenzila ilosisika iye esinopiwa.

(5) Kushalujana somwazulobhaunulwa muchibabi chechikamu (3) akujilolulama kuzhe kokuti kwaboneka kuti kushalujwa yoko kulolulama akale kulokwelela mushango yobutongi bwabanji ilosendama pokuta zwinhu nenzila ipo pachena, indulamo, bukulugulu bwobunhu, kukwelana kwabanhu nolusungunuko.

(6) Inyika inokwelela kutola zwisungo zwemilawu zwilokwelela nezwimwe bo kukutaija kukwelana kwabanhu akale kutabilija nokusundijila banhu kana kuti makapo abanhu babekasindamijwa nokushalujwa, akale—

(a)

zwisungo yezwo zwinokwelela kutolwa kutila kululamisa bukukutu bwechokomende;

(b) apana chisungo chakadai chingawhi achijilolulama kana kutobelejwa (3). 57

Ilulamilo yokuba nesiswa

Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokuba nesiswa, inoshanganisila kuba nelulamilo yokuti—

(a)

unzi, bugalo nezwibiya zwizwe azwijolokwelela kungwinwa pesawanikwa imvumo yomweni;

(b) apana chingashakwa pambili uwe, unzi, bugalo kana kuti zwilongo zwizwe;

(c)

yokusotolelwa zwibiya zwizwe;

(e)

chimo chobushanana bwabo achingadombolwe pachena.

(d) isiswa pakulebejana nabamwe alingasindamijwe;


Lusungunuko lokubungana nokulyijana

(1) Unhu wose unanolusungunuko lokubungana kana kulyijana nabamwe, akale nelulamilo yokusobungana kana kulyijana nababwe.

(2) Apana unhu angabhatwa nesimba kuti ebe imhingu yegapo kana kuti kunda kushangano kana igubungano. 59

Lusungunuko lokutondejela nokusenga zwililijiko

Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokutondejela kana kusenga zwililijiko, koga ilulamilo yeji jinokwelela kutiwa mulunyalalo. 60

Lusungunuko lokuzuyeyela zwaunoda

(1) Unhu unanelulamilo yokuzuyeyela inoshanganisila—

(a)

lusungunuko lokuyeya, lokuba nendangalilo, kudimbwa kana kuba noluvumilo; akale

(b) lusungunuko lokuta nokushambaja nokubhaunula zwaanoyeya, indangalilo, luvumilo kana kudimbwa, yezwi zwingatiwa mukati mwabanhu kana musiswa zwisinani kuti uli ega kana kuti unanabamwe banhu.

(2) Apana unhu angabhatwa nesimba kuti etole ikowhe lisinokakabajana

nokudimbwa kana luvumilo lulwe, kana kutola ikowhe nenzila inokakabajana nokudimbwa kana luvumilo lulwe.

(3) Bazwali nabalondoti babana batukutuku bananelulamilo yokushala, zwikwendelana nokudimbwa kwabo, inzila ilolulama yokukubusa bana noluvumilo lwabachotobejela, koga yezwi azwitokwelela kusindamija ilulamilo jabana jilosumwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yelino jinoshanganisila ilulamilo yezwilayijo, yobushanana, yokutabilijwa nobupenyu bubuya.

(4) Igapo lipi zwalyo lyezwokudimbwa linotubula kubumba inhabi inolayijwa zwokudimbwa, kojoba inhabi yeyo ikawana lubhancho kana imali yokubhancha kuva kuNyika. 61

Lusungunuko lokutula indangalilo nolusungunuko lwenzila jokukwendesa inyaya

(1) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokutula indangalilo, zwinoshanganisila—

(a)

lusungunuko lokushaka, kutambula nokuleba indangalilo kana lumwe luzibo;

(b) lusungunuko lwokubhisila pachena indangalilo kushingiswa zwemizano nokushakisisa nokutondeja bumhizha kushingiswa isayensi;

(c)

lusungunuko lwezwilayijo.

(2) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yenzila jokukwendesa luzibo kana inyaya, inoshanganisila kutabilijwa kwesiswa lyenhiba jabatoli bendebo.

(3) Lusungunuko lokuzhula ingumba jokushambaja mumpepo nezwimwe zwinoshingiswa pakushambaja inyaya, zwikwendelana chete nezwisungo zwinovumilwa nafulumende—

(a)

zwinodikana kuti kutubulwe kululamiswa masaisai nejimwe inzila jokuwakwendesa;

(b) zwilozwimila zwoga zwisinolulamiswa kushinga kwazo nafulumende kana bezwamatongelo enyika kana banoshaka imali.

(4) Ingumba jose jafulumende jokushambaja inyaya noluzibo jinokwelela

(a)

kusununguka kushala nokusuma jikazwimilila joga inzila jokupyeka zwajinoshambaja;

(b) ajitokwelela kutola ibhazu;

(c)

kupa unshaji ulokwelela wokutulwa kwendangalilo jilosiyana akale nejinonyunyuta.

(5) Lusungunuko lwokutula indangalilo nolusungunuko lwenzila jokukwendesa inyaya alutoshanganisila—

(a)

kukutaija kukakabajana kwokulwana;

(c)

kulwajisa nenzila isilokwelela bululami kana bunhu bomwe unhu;

(b) kusungwaja kubengana kana indebo jokubengana;

(d) kufukunyula nenzila isilolulama ilulamilo yonhu yesiswa lile. 62

Kutubula kuwana luzibo

(1) Unizi wose kana kuti unhu waba nemvumo yokugala muZimbabwe zwachose, kushanganisila bemilawu nabanoshambaja inyaya, unanelulamilo yokuwana luzibo lulolondotwa nenyika kana kuti inhabi ipi yafulumende, kana kuli kuti luzibo yolo lumudikana kutila kuti banhu bezibe zwikumutukana.

(2) Unhu wose, kushanganisila banotola indebo, unanelulamilo yokuwana luzibo/chinyewulilo chilolondotwa nomwe unhu, kushanganisila inyika, kana kuli kuti chinyeulilo yecho chimudikana kuti kutobelejwe kana kuti kutabilijwe ilulamilo.

(3) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokuti chinyeulilo pezhulu pape chilobhatwa nafulumende kana kuti imwe inhabi yafulumende chilulamise kana kuti chijimwe kana chili chokuniepa kana kuti chili nobulunda.

(4) Mulawu unokwelela kubumbwa kutila kuti ilulamilo yeyi ishinge, koga ungajibija kuwanwa kwechinyeulilo nechinangwa chokulondota inyika, lunyalalo mukati mwabanhu kana kuti isiswa lyonshingo koga chijibijo yechi chinokwelela kuba kalulama, chilichibuyanana, chikakwelela akale chilinendulamo neshango yobutongi bwabanji ilosendama mukubhudilana pachena, bululami, kulemekejwa kwobunhu, kukwelana kwabanhu nolusungunuko. 63

Lulimi namagalilo Unhu wose unanelulamilo—

(a)

yokushingisa lulimi lwaashala; noku

(b) yokubhatikana mubupenyu bwamagalilo bwaashala;

koga apana unhu mukushingisa ilulamilo yeji angata alo nenzila inokakabajana nechisiyaniso yechi chebumbilo lyemilawu. 64

Lusungunuko lwobumhizha kana unshingo

Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokushala nokuta bumhizha kana unshingo waanoda, koga kuta bumhizha kana unshingo kungalulamiswa nomulawu. 65

Ilulamilo jemishingo

(1) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokuwana zwisungo zwokushinga zwilolulama akale zwinotabilija ponshingo akale nokulipwa zwilokwelela akale zwilolulama.

(2) Unhu wose, kuzhe kwemhingu jinoshinga munezwokutabilijila inyika, unanelulamilo yokubumba kana kuba imhingu yamakubungano ezwabashingi nabashingilwa yaanoda, nokubhatikana muzwitiko zwamakubungano yaya zwipo pamulawu.

(3) Unshingi wose, kuzhe kwemhingu jinoshinga kutabilijila inyika, unanelulamilo yokubhatikana muzwitiko zwokubhtana nabambwe ponshiko zwinoshanganisila ilulamilo yokulamwa unshingo, kugala beliponshingo asi besinoshinga, kusoshinga kana kuti zwimwe bo zwabavumilana koga mulawu ungalulamisa kushingiswa kwelulamilo yeyi kutila kuti mishingo ilokosha indilile mbeli.

(4) Unshingi wose unanelulamilo yokuwana mashingilo mabuya, alokwelela akale alolulam.

(5) Kuzhe kwemhingu jinoshinga kutabilija inyika, bashingi, bashingilwa, makapo ezwokushingilana, makapo abashingi namakapo abashingilwa ose ananelulamilo—

(a)

yokulebelekisana panupamwempela naamwe makapo bevumilana;

(c)

yokubumba nokuba imhingu jamakapo abashingi kana bashingilwa;

(b) yokubambanyila;

(6) Banhukaji nabanhulume bananelulamilo yokuwana indipo inokwelana ponshingo unokwelana.

(7) Bashingi bechinhukaji bananelulamilo yokuwana unshaji wokunyetutuka wokuchila wemweji isimishoma kupinda mitatu akale bewana indipo yabo ilokwana. 66

Lusungunuko lokukwendenda nokugala

(1) Unizi wose womuZimbabwe—

(a) unanelulamilo yokungwina muZimbabwe;

(b) aangatatwe muZimbabwe; akale

(c) unanelulamilo yokuwana ipasipoti kana imwe iwhalo yokukwendesa.

(2) Unizi wose womuZimbabwe nonhu wose umo muZimbabwe zwipo pamulawu unanelulamilo—

(a)

yokukwendenda ekasununguka muZimbabwe;

(c)

yokubhuda muZimbabwe.

(b) yokugala kojoba munobupi bugalo muZimbabwe; noku


Ilulamilo jezwamatongelo enyika

(1) Unizi wose womuZimbabwe unanelulamilo—

(a)

yeshalujo jilosununguka, jilolulama kale jinotiwa noshaji ulokwelela pozugalo zwose zwilosumwa kutobelwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli kana umwe mulawu; noku

(b) yokushala zwaanoda munezwamatongelo enyika ekasununguka.

(2) Kutobelejwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, unizi wose womuZimbabwe unanelulamilo —

(a)

yokubumba, kuba imhingu kana kubhatikana muzwitiko zwegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika lyaashala;

(b) yokushambajila igapo kana zwibabalilo zwamatongelo enyika ekasununguka akale mulunyalalo;

(c)

kubhatikana muzwitiko zwamatongelo enyika zwinolunyalalo;

(d) kubhatikana, ega kana kuti pamwempela nabamwe, mumakubungano kana munejimwe inzila, muzwitiko zwokukutaija, kukakabaja kana kutondeja kushantila zwisungo zwafulumende kana kuti zwimwe zwazwo zwamatongelo enyika kana zwipi zwazwo.

(3) Kutobelejwa iBumbilo yeli, unizi upi zwe womuZimbabwe unamakole kana anopinda igumi linamashanu namatatu unanelulamilo —

(a)

yokushala mushalujo kana mareferendamu munoba munoshinga ibumbilo yeli kana kuti umwe mulawu akale kuta alo musiswa; akale

(b) yokuba umilili mushalujo akale, kana ashalwa, kushinga unshingo waashalilwa. 68

Ilulamilo yokuwana indulamo ilokwelela

(1) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokuwana indulamo ilokwelela ipo pamulawu, inotiwa nokukambija, inotondeja kukwelaniswa kwonhu wose.

(2) Unhu unanelulamilo, lusungunuko, chido kana kuti kulingilila kupo pamulawu kwasindamijwa nemishingo isinobekwendeswa zwanana, unanelulamilo yokupiwa zwisoko nokukambija akale zwikakwalwa zwokusindamijwa yoku.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa isimba lyokushinga komulawu yoyu, akale unokwelela—

(a)

kuvumila kuti igota, kana kuti kana zutubukana, nelimwe gapo lyemilawu

lilozwimilila akale lisina ibhazu, lilingisise unkwendeselo wezwinhu;

(b) kupa inyika unshingo wokubona kuti kuti ilulamilo jimo muzwibabi zwechikamu (1) na (2) jimutobelejwa; akale

(c) 69

kusungwajwa unkwendeselo wonshingo unandyo.

Ilulamilo yokubetekeswa kunabululami

(1) Unhu wose umutamijwa undandu unanelulamilo yokubetekeswa undandu uwe zwinabululami negota lilozwimilila loga akale lisinotola ibhazu akale zwipo pachena munshaji unotambulika.

(2) Mukusuma ilulamilo nemishingo, unhu wose unanelulamilo yokutongwa zwinabululami, nokukambija akale pachena mukati mwonshaji ulolulama mbeli kwegota kana imwe inhabi yabumbwa nomulawu lilozwimilila akale lisina ibhazu.

(3) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokuswika kumakota kana jimwe imhingu jilotalwa nomulawu eshaka kubhanchwa mukukolola kukabajana kupi zwako.

(4) Unhu wose unanelulamilo, ezwilipila ega, kushala nokumililwa nonhu unota zwemilawu mugota lipi zwalo, lilobumbilwa kulinga inyaya jonshaji upi zwawo kana limwe ibuta. 70

Ilulamilo jabanhu bamutamijwa

(1) Unhu upi zwe umutamijwa undandu unanelulamilo jinotobela—

(a)

kuyeyeselwa kuti aana undandu kuswika kwasumikijwa kuti unanondandu;

(c)

kupiwa unshaji ulokwana nezwishingiso kuti ebambanyile kuzwitabilija;

(e)

kumililwa nonhu wezwemilawu waapiwa neNyika akale kushingiswa imali yenyika, kana kuli kuti pangaba nokusindamijwa kukulwana;

(b) kuzibiswa nokukambija kuti umutamijwani, muchinyeulilo chilokwana kutila kuti etubule kushandula;

(d) kushala unhu wezwemilawu akale, ezwilipila ega, kumililwa nonhu wozwemilawu;

(f)

kuzibiswa nokukambija ilulamilo inopiwa mundima (d) na (e);

(g) kubaipo paanobebeteswa;

(h) kupa nokulamba busumikiji;

(i)

(j)

kunyalala nokusopa busumikiji nokusomanikijwa kupa busumikji bunonsungilila; kuti zwitiko zwokubetekeswa zwitolikilwe mululimi lwaanowhisisa;

(k) kusowanikwa eli nondandu pezhulu pechinhu chaakata kana chaakaleka kuta icho chisakabeli undandu ponshaji wachakatika;

(l)

kusowanikwa elinondandu pezhulu pechinhu chakabeli undandu chisichili undandu;

(m) kusobetekeswa undandu wechitiko kana kuti chinhu chaakaleka kuta icho eli akalekelelwa kana kuti akawanikwa esina ndandu kana kuti akawanikwa elinondandu;

(n) kupiwa untongo upo pasi panoyo ulotalwa kana yoyo untongo wondandu ulotalwa wakashandulilwa kuva ponshaji wokutiwa kwondandu nopanshaji wokupiwa untongo.

(2) Yopo chikamu yechi pachinoda kuti chinyeulilo chipiwe kunhu—

(a)

2013

chinyeulilo chinokwelela kupiwa mululimi unhu lwaanowhisisa; akale

(b) kana unhu esinotubula kubala kana kukwala, iwhalo ipi zwayo inanechinyeulilo yecho inokwelela kubhaunulwa nenzila yokuti unhu unoyiwhisisa.

(3) Mukubetekesa milandu, busumikiji bwawanikwa nenzila inokakabajana nezwisungo zweChisiyaniso yechi bunokwelela kusotambulwa kana kuli kuti kutambulwa kwobusumukiji yobo kuchata kuti kubetekeswa yoko kube kusilolulama kana kuti kulundule unkwendeselo wendulamo kana kuti zwido zwabanhu.

(4) Unhu upi azwe wabetekeswa undandu unanelulamilo, eli alipa imali ilokwelela ilotalwa nomulawu, yokupiwa lukwalo lulomuma makwendeselo okubetekeswa munshaji unowhisisika mushule mwokutulwa kontongo mukubetekeswa.

(5) Uhu upi azwe wabetekeswa akawanikwa elinondandu unanelulamilo, kutobelwa zwitabilijo zwinowhisiska zwilotalwa nomulawu—

(a)

wokuti inyaya yiye ilingululwe negota lipo pezhulu peelyo lyakapa untongo; kana

(b) kulilijika kugota lipo pezhulu pelo lakamba untongo kuti pezhulu pokuwanikwa nondandu nontongo. 71

Ilulamilo yechuma

(1) Muchikamu yechi— “indipo yomulyandiligele” zwinoleba ipencheni, imali inolipwa unhu igole negole mushule mwokusiya unshingo, imali inopiwa unshingi mushule mwokusiya unshingo ingalipwa—

(a)

unhu iva kuChikwama chaFulumende;

(c)

unhu walwala kana wakubajwa; kana

(b) unhu wakabeshingila umwe ;

(d) unhu wasiya unshingo nenyaya yamakole kana kulwala kana chimwe chisoko;

akale inoshanganisila kushandulwa kwepencheni yeyo, kana imali inolipwa unhu mushule mwegole mwokusiya unshingo, imali inopiwa unshingi mushule mwokusiya unshingo nemali inobwilizilwa unhu yaakabebhadalila ipencheni, imali inopiwa mushule mwegole, mushule mwokusiya unshingo kana indipilo; “ifuma” zwinoleba ifuma yolupi luji akale ilulamilo kana chibabalilo mufuma.

(2) Kutobelejwa chikamu 72, unhu wose unanelulamilo, mubugalo bupi zwabo bweZimbabwe, kuwana, kulondota, kushingisa, kupa umwe, kuzwipila kulipa imali, kucheneka kana kupambukana nenduji jose jefuma, ekazwimilila kana eli mugubungano nabamwe.

(3) Kutobelejwa chikamu yechino nechikamu 72, apana unhu angamanikijwa kutolelwa fuma chiche kuzhe kokuti zwisungo zwinotobela zwazhajikijwa

(a)

kutolelwa mukutobeleja mulawu unoshinga kunhu wose;

(b) kutolelwa kulokwelela kutila zwisoko zwinotobela—

(i) kutila kulondota kutabilija, kutabilija banhu, kugajikana kwabanhu, bululami mubanhu, bushanana mubanhu kanakulongwa kweguta kana inyika; kana

(ii) kutila kunancha kana kubuza imbhudililo nokushingisa ifuma yeyo kana chimwe nechinangwa chingabhancha banhu bose mubugalo;

(c)

2013

mulawu unoti imhingu inamasimba okutola ifuma—

(i) inokwelela kupa chizibiso chinowhisisika chechinangwa chokutola ifuma kunhu wose unechibabalilo kana ilulamilo ichabhatwa nokutola yoku;

(ii) kulipa indipo ilolulama akale ilokwana yokutola kusanu kwatolwa ifuma kana kuti mukati mwonshaji unowhisisika mushule mwokutola; akale

(iii) kana peli nokukakabajana pezhulu pokutola, kukwala kugota lilolulama kusanu kwatolwa ifuma, kana kuti kusanu kwapela mazhuba makumi matatu mushule mwokutola ifuma, kukumbilwa untongo unosimatija kutolwa kwefuma;

(d) mulawu unopa unhu upi azwe unanechuma chatolwa ilulamilo yokuti ekwale kugota lilolulama kuti chuma yecho chibwilile kwaali nokukambija kana igota lisasimatija kutolwa kwechuma chiche; akale

(e)

mulawu unopa unhu upi azwe unanelulamilo yokulipwa kuti ekumbile kugota lilolulama kuti litonge pezhulu—

(i) pokuba ipo, chimo nokuwanda kwebabalilo yabo mufuma yeyo;

(ii) pokuba pamulawu kwokutolwa kwefuma; akale

(iii) kuwanda bwendipo yaanokwelela kuwana;

akale kukwala kugota ekumbila kuti liluamise kuti epiwe indipo yiye nokukambija.

(4) Yopo unhu paananelulamilo yokulipwa imali yomulyandiligele, mulawu unolungulula kubhiswa kwelulamilo unoshingiswa,kutila chibabi chechikamu (3), somulawu unovumuja kutolwa kwefuma. 72

Ilulamilo yevu lyokulima

(1) Muchikamu yechi—

“ivu lyokulima” linoleba ivu linoshingiswa kana kuti lilokwelela kulimwa, zwileba kulimwa kwemilibo namaluba, kulimwa kwamagilepusi, zwamashango kana kulelwa kwehobe nechinangwa chipi zwacho chokulima nokulondota, kunoshanganisila—

(a) kulondota kana kukubusa chuma, imhuka jeshango, ihuku,imhuka kana inyuchi; kana

(b) kucheja chuma kana imhuka jeshango;

koga alitoshanganisila ivu lyokumisha kana kuti ivu limo mumiganu yamakansili omumaguta kana kuti mukati mweguta ilomiswa pasi pomulawu unolyijana nokulongwa kwamaguta nenyika kana kuti so mwakulopasanulwa nomulawu unolyijana nokushakisisa pezhulu pevu; “ivu” linoshanganisila chinhu chilobhatana zwachose nevu kana kuti chinomela pevu;

“chikamu chevu lyokulima” zwinoleba chikamu chevu lyokulima lilokwaliswa sechikamu chilosiyana chevu kuhofesi inolondota iwhalo jokuba mweni wefuma.

(2) Kana ivu lyokulima, kana kuti ilulamilo kana ibabalilo muvu yelo, lidikana kutila banhu banji, kushanganisila—

(a)

kugajika banhu kutila kulima kana chimwe chinangwa;

(b) kubambanya bupya kushingiswa kwevu, mashango, kulondota zwibumbwa

(c)

2013

kana kushingiswa kwemhuka jeshango kana zwimwe zwibumbwa; kana kugajikwa bupya kwabanhu bakatolelwa ivu kuti lishingiswe nechinangwa chalebwa mundima (a) na (b);

ivu, ilulamilo kana ibabalilo ingatolwa nenyika pesina ilulamilo yokulamba nokupa chizibiso muGazeti itondeka ivu, ilulamilo kana ibabalilo, akale pakalepo ivu, ilulamilo nebabalilo zwinova zwaba zwenyika kutangila pezhuba lyokubhiswa kwechizibiso.

(3) Yopo kana ivu lokulima, kana ilulamilo kana ibabalilo kuvu lyakadalo, lyatolwa pesina ilulamilo yokulamba nechinangwa chalebwa muchibabi (2)—

(a)

kutobelwa chikamu 295(1) na (2), apana indipo inolipwa pezhulu pokutolwa, kuzhe kwembhudililo yakatiwa palili lisanu lyatolwa;

(b) apana unhu angakumbila kugota lyezwemilawu kuti kusumwe indipo, kuzhe kwendipo yembhudililo yakatiwa pevu lisanu lyatolwa akale apana igota lyezwemilawu lingawhilila zwikumbilo zwakadai; akale

(c)

kutolwa akungagundujwe pokuti kunoshaluja zwinokakabajana nechikamu 56.

(4) Ivu lyose—

(a)

lilodombolwa muShejulu 7 cheBumbilo lyeMilawu lyakapinda; kana

(b) kusanu kwasika izhuba lyokushinga kwebumbilo yeli, lyakashalwa kutobelwa chikamu 16B(2)(a)(ii) kana (iii) webumbilo lyakapinda;

linondilila mbeli lili mumaboko enyika, akale apana indipo ingalipwa pezhulu pokutolwa kwalo kuzhe kokulipa imbhudililo yakatiwa palili lisanu lyatolwa.

(5) Nokukambija kunotubulika mushule mwokutolwa kwevu lyokulima pesina ilulamilo yokulamba nalo kutobelwa chibabi (2), unhu unanonshingo wokukwalisa ivu yelo unokwelela, esapa chimwe chizibiso, kutambula zwinobekakwelela pewhalo inotondeja kuba mweni wefuma kutila kumiswa zwimomulawu iwhalo inotondeja kuba mweni wefuma kana ivu uti kokwalisa ivu yeli pasi pafulumende somweni wevu.

(6) Mulawu wepalamende ungata kuti kube undandu kuti unhu upi azwe, esapiwa imvumo ipo pomulawu, kuba nevu kana kugala pevu lyalebwa muchikamu yechi kana kuti limwe ivu lyeNyika.

(7) Penyaya yokutolwa kwevu lyokulima pesina ilulamilo yokulamba nechinangwa chokugajika kutobelwa bulongwa bwokugajika bwenshanduko minozwevu, zwinhu zwinotobela zwinokelela kutolwa sezwilokosha zwikulugulu akale zwilopelela—

(a)

pasi pobutongi bobusindamiji banhu bomuZimbabwe bakatolelwa ivu nezwimwe zwibumbwa nenzila isilolulma pesina kulipwa;

(b) pezhulu pezwi banhu bakatola zwilwaniso kutila kuti betole ivu lyabo nokuzwitonga kwabo, akale yezwi zwakabuza Lusungunuko lweZimbabwe mugole lya1980;

(c)

Banhu bomuZimbabwe banokwelela kutubuswa kutondeja akale ilulamilo jabo akale bebwilile kuba beni bevu lyabo;

akale zwinotobela.

(i) butongi bwakabesindamija bunanondandu wokulipila ivu lyokulima lyatolwa pesina ilulamilo yokulamba, litolelwa kugajika banhu, bushingisa ihobhodo inanemali ilokwana yakabumbilwa chinangwa yechi; akale

(ii) kana butongi bwakabesindamija bwaakonwa kulipa bushingisa ihobhodo yakadalo, Fulumende weZimbabwe aana undandu wokulipila ivu lyokulima lyakatolwa pesina ilulamilo yokulamba, litolelwa kuti kugajikwe banhu.

(8) Chikamu yechi chinoshinga chisinolundula undandu wobutongi bwakabekasindamija kulipila ivu lyadomwa muchikamu yechi lyatolwa nechinangwa chokugajika banhu bupya. 73

Ilulamilo yezwilokombeleja

(1) Unhu wose unanelulamilo—

(a)

yokukombelejwa kusina igubajo kubushanana kana bupenyu bubuya bubwe; akale

(b) kuti zwilokombeleja zwitabilijwe kutila kuti zwibhanche zwizwalwa zwafeno nezwinotobela, kushingiswa milawu inowhisisika nezwimwe zwisungo zwino—

(i) tabilija kuswibha nokupalala kokulyijana kwezwibumbwa zwipenyu;

(ii) kutaija kulondota; noku

(iii) sungwaja imbhudililo inolondota kulyijana kwezwibumbwa zwipenyu nokushingiswa kwezwibumbwa nonshaji umwempela kusungwajwa imbhudililo yezwobufumi nokugajikana kwabanhu.

(2) INyika inokwelela kutola milawu inowhisisika nezwimwe zwisungo, kutobelwa kwezwishingiso zwafulumende, kuti ilulamilo imbuya jilokwalwa muchikamu yechi jiswinchwe. 74

Lusungunuko lwokusomanikijwa kutatwa

Apana unhu angatatwa ponzi uwe, kana kuti unzi uwe ukolomolwe, pesina ilulamiso inova kugota lyemilawu mushule mwokulingisisa zwinhu zwinokwendelana nenyaya. 75

Ilulamilo yendayijo

(1) Unizi wose nonhu unemvumo yokugala muZimbabwe zwachose unanelulamilo—

(a)

yendayijo ilokoshesesa inolipwa neNyika, kushanganisila indayijo yabakulwana ilokoshesesa; akale

(b) yendayijo inondilila mbeli inokwelela kupiwa akale nokutubuliswa nenyika ishingisa milawu nezwimwe zwisungo zwinowhisisika.

(2) Unhu wose unanelulamilo yokutanga nokulondota, eshingisa imali yiye, zwikolo zwilozwimilila zwilokwelela, koga pesina kushaluja kunolambijwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(3) Mulawu ungati zwikolo zwabumbwa kutobelwa chibabi (2) zwikwaliswe akale kuvalwa kwezwikolo zwakadalo kana zwisinozhajikija zwisungo zwilolulama zwilowhi zwinodikana kuti zwikwaliswe,

(4) INyika inokwelela kutola milawu nezwimwe zwisungo zwilolulama, zwikwendelana nemali nezwishingiso zwainanazo, kutubulisa kuwanwa kwelulamilo ilosumwa muchibabi (1).

2013

Ilulamilo yokuwana bushanana

(1) Unizi wose womuZimababwe nonhu unanemvumo yokugala muZimbabwe zwachose unanelulamilo yokuwana bushanana bulokoshesesa, kushanganisila bushanana bezwokuzwala.

(2) Unhu wose unolalama nobulwaji bunogala konshaji undefu busalapika unanelulamilo yokuwana zwobushanana zwilokoshesesa zwobulwaji yobo.

(3) Apana unhu angalambijwa kulapwa munachipi zwacho chibhadela kana eshaka kulapwa kokukimbija kunopiwa banobupenyu bumomungozi.

(4) INyika inokwelela kutola milawu ilokwelela nezwimwe zwisungo, zwikwendelana nemali nezwishingiso zwayinazo, kubona kuti ilulamilo jimo muchikamu yeji jinotubuswa. 77

Ilulamilo yokulya nevula Unhu wose unanelulamilo—

(a)

yevula ilotabilijika, ilochena akale inonyamulika; akale

(b) yokulya kulokwana;

akale iNyika inokwelela kutola milawu inowhisisika nezwimwe zwisungo, zwikwendelana nemali nezwishingiso zwainazo, kutubulisa kuwanikwa kwelulamilo yeyi. 78

Ilulamilo yokulobolana

(1) Unhu wose waswincha makole igumi linamashanu anamatatu unanelulamilo yokubumba imhuli. chiche.

(2) Apana unhu angamanikijwa kulobola kana kulobolwa kuzhe kwechido

(3) Banhulume boga boga kana banhukaji boga boaga abatovumilwa kulobolana pachabo. IMHINGU 3 Kupandululwa Kwejimwe Ilulamilo 79

Kushinga kweMhingu 3

(1) Chikamu yechi chinopandulula jimwe ilulamilo nolusungunuko kutila kuti zwibe pachena kushinga kwelulamilo nolusungunuko yolo kumakapo abanhu.

(2) Chikamu yechi achitokwelela kutolwa sokuti chikumujibija ilulamilo jilosumwa muMhingu 2. 80

Ilulamilo jabanhukaji

(1) Unhukaji wose unakulemekejwa kulopelela akale kunokwelana nokwabanhulume akale yezwi zwinoshanganisila unshaji unokwelana muzwitiko zwamatongelo enyika, zwobufumi nokugajikana nenduji jabanhu.

(2) Banhukaji bananelulamilo inokwelana neyabanhulume munezwokulondota nokukubusa bana, koga mulawu wepalamende ungalulamisa kuti ilulamilo yeyi inotobelejwa chini.

(3) Milawu yose namagalilo zwinosindamija ilulamilo jabanhukaji jabanopiwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli azwitoshinga zwikwendelana nobusindamiji bwadeni. 81

Ilulamilo jabana

(1) Mwana wose, kuleba unhulumyana wose kana unhukajana wose upo pasi pamakole igumi linamashanu anamatatu, unanelulamilo—

(a)

yokubhatwa zwinokwelana pomulawu, kushanganisila ilulamilo yokuwhikwa;

(b) yokupiwa izina nontupo wemhuli;

(c)

kana elimwana—

(i) wakazwalwa muZimbabwe; kana

(ii) wakazwala kuzhe kweZimbabwe akale eli unizi webhatuko muZimbabwe;

kuwana lukwalo lokuzwalwa nokukambija;

(d) yemhuli nokulondotwa nabazwali, kana kubhtwa nenzila ilolulama kana abhiswa mubugalo bwemhuli;

(e)

(f)

kutabilijwa mukudyamijwa munozobufumi kana zopabulo, kushingiswa kwabana, nokusobhtwa zwibuyanana, kulekelelwa kana kuti jimwe inzila jokushunguluja bana; kundayijo, bushanana, kulya kulolulamila umbili nopokugala;

(g) kusongwina mubalwi behondo kana kubhatikana munozkukabajana kwehondo;

(h) kusomanikijwa kuta zwipi zwazo zwitiko zwamatongelo enyika; akale

(i)

(i)

kusobhatwa mubusungwa kuzhe kokuti kana kuli inzila yokupejeleja akale, kana babhatwa mubusungwa— kubhatwa mubusungwa konshaji untukutuku ulokwelela;

(ii) kulondotwa bekapambulwa kubanhu bamo mubusungwa banamakole apo pezhulu pegumi linamashanu namatatu; akale

(iii) kubhatwa nenzila, akale nokulondotwa mubugalo, bunolinga kuti mwana zukwendelana namakole omwana. nabana.

(2) Zwibabalilo zwabana zilokosha zwikulugulu munyay jose jilonanokuta

(3) Bana bananelulamilo yokutabilijwa namakota, kasa kasa iGota lyopezhulu sondondoti wabo unkulwana. 82

Ilulamilo jabakwegula

Banhu banamakole aipo pezhulu pamakumi mashanu anamakumi mabili bananelulamilo—

(a)

yokuwana lubhancho lulolulama kuva kumhuli jabo neNyika;

(c)

yokuwana lubhancho lwemali kushingiswa inzila jinolondota banhu nokubapa lubhancho;

(b) yokuwana lubhancho lwobushanana nokulapwa kuva kuNyika; akale

akale iNyika inokwelela kuta milawu ilolulama nozwimwe zwisungo, kukalingwa kutubula kwayo, kubona kuti ilulamilo yeyi inowanwa.

2013

Ilulamilo jabanhu banabukosha

INyika inokwelela kutola zwisungo zwilolulama, kukalingwa kutubula kwayo, kubona kuti banhu banabukosha banoswincha kutubula kwabo kulopelela kwendangalilo nokombili, kushanganisila zwisungo—

(a)

zwinobabhancha kuti betubule kuzwililitila;

(c)

zwinobatabilija kunduji jose jokushingiswa kunabusindamiji nokushungulujwa;

(b) zwinobatubuslia kuti begale nemhuli jabo akale bebhatikane muzwitiko zwamagalilo abanhu, zwobumhizha nozwokuzwibalaija;

(d) yokuwaniswa miti yokulapwa bulwaji, kulapwa mukulwala indangalilo nokulapwa kuti bebe banhu banozwishingila;

(e)

(f) 84

zwokuwanisa zwibakwa zwilolulama zwndayijo yabo;

zwokuwanisa indayijo inolipwa neNyika yopo painodikana.

Ilulamilo jabalwi behondo yolusungunuko

(1) Balwi behondo yolusungunuko, yezwi zwileba—

(a)

yabo bakalwa muHondo yoLusungunuko;

(c)

yabo bakabasungwa, bakabhatwa mubusungwa nabakalambijwa kukwendenda pezhulu penyaya jamatongelo enyika nonshaji wehondo yolusungunuko;

(b) yabo bakabhancha balwi muHondo yoLusungunuko;

balokwelela kulangalilwa pezhulu pemishimgo yabakata pakusungunula inyika yeZimbabwe, akale balokwelela kuwaniswa lubhancho lulokwelela lunanga ipencheni nokuwana bushanana bulokosha.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa kubalwi behondo yolusungunuko yezwo zwilobakwelela zwilodomwa muchibabi (1). IMHINGU 4 Kusimatijwa Kwelulamilo Nolusungunuko Lukulugulu 85

Kusimatijwa kwelulamilo nolusungunuko lukulugulu

(1) Upi azwe wabanhu banotobela, banoti—

(a)

unhu upi azwe etila zwido zwizwe;

(c)

unhu upi azwe umuma semhingu yegapo, kana kuti etobela zwibabalilo zwegapo lyabanhu;

(b) unhu upi azwe emilila umwe unhu usunotubula kuzwitila ega;

(d) unhu upi zwe umumilila banhu banji;

(e)

igapo lipi zwalyo lilomilila zwibabalilo zwemhingu jalyo;

unanelulamilo yokunda kugota, eleba kuti ilulamilo nolusungunuko lukulugulu lulosumwa muChisiyaniso yechi javunwa, jimuvunwa kana kuti zwingatikana kuti jichavunwa, akale igota lingapa lubhancho lulokwelela, lunoshanganisila kusumwa kwelulamilo nokusuma kuti unhu elipwe.

(2) Kuti unhu wavuna mulawu akutonjibija kuti eswike kugota ekumbila
