lubhancho kugota somwazwasumwa muchibabi chechikamu (1).

(3) Milawu yamakota ose inokwelela kusuma inzila jichatobelwa kana kushakwa lubhancho somwazwasumwa muchibabi chechikamu (1), akale milawu yeyo inokwelela kubona kuti:

(a)

ilulamilo yokuswika kugota kutobelwa chibabi (1) inozhajikijwa zwilopelela;

(b) zwitiko zwokwendeselo, kushanganisila kutanga kwazo, zwinokwelela kuba zwishoma;

(c)

zwitiko zwonkwendeselo, kushanganisila kutanga kwazo, zwinokwelela kuba zwishoma;

(d) unhu unanobumwe bumhizha, eli avumilwa negota, angasumikija sonkwaina wegota.

(4) Kusoba ipo kwemilawu yalebwa muchibabi chechikamu (3) akutotabilija ilulamilo yokutanga kwezwitiko sokulebwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) nokuwhiwha kwenyaya nokutongwa kwayo mugota. IMHINGU 5 Kujibilijwa Kwelulamilo Nolusungunuko Lukulugulu 86

Kujibilijwa kwelulamilo nolusungunuko

(1) Ilulamilo nolusungunuko lukulugulu lulosumwa muChisiyaniso yechi jinokwelela kushingiswa nenzila imbuyanana akale kulingisiswa ilulamilo nolusungunuko lwabamwe banhu.

(2) Ilulamilo nolusungunuko lukulugulu lulosumwa muChisiyaniso yechi zwingajibilijwa chete kana kutobelwa mulawu unoshinga kunhu wose akale kana kuli kuti kujibilija yoku kulokwelela, kulolulama akale kunowhisisika munyika inolusungunuko ilosendama pokubhudila pachena, indulamo, kulemekejwa konhu, kukwelana kwabanhu nolusungunuko, kulingisiswa zwose zwilokwelela zwinoshanganisila—

(a)

umbumbilo welulamilo kana lusungunuko lumulebwa;

(c)

chimo nokumila kokujibilija;

(f)

kojoba kuli nenzila jingakoneja chinangwa chokujibilija yoku.

(a)

ilulamilo yobupenyu, kuzhe kana kutobelwa chikamu 48;

(c)

ilulamilo yokusolangwa kunolwajisa kana kubhatwa nenzila inoshunguluja, kubhatwa kana kulangwa nenzila isilokwelela unhu kana

(b) chinangwa chokujibilija, kasa kasa kuti chilokwelela kukalingwa zwibabalilo zwokutabilija inyika, kutabilija banhu, kugajikana kwabanhu, bululami bwabanhu banji, bushanana bwabanji kulongwa kwamatunhu namaguta kana kuti zwibabalilo zwabanji;

(d) kusimatija kuti kuwana ilulamilo nolusungunuko konhu upi azwe akutotabilija kuwanwa kwelulamilo nolusungunuko lwabamwe;

(e) kulyijana pakati pelulamilo yajibiswa nechinangwa chokujibisa, kasa kasa kuti inojibija ilulamilo kana lusungunuko yolo zwilopindilija yozo zwinodikana kuti kuswinchwe chinangwa;

(3) Apana mulawu ungajibija ilulamilo jinotobela jilosumwa muChisiyaniso yechi, akale apana unhu angajivuna—

(b) ilulamilo yokulemekejwa kwobunhu;

inodeleja chimilo;

(d) ilulamilo yokusobhatwa mubuzike kana mubutapwa;

(e)

(f)


ilulamilo yokubetekeswa zwilokwelela;

ilulamilo yokuwana untongo wokuzha mbeli kwegota koshakisisa pezhulu pobululami bwokubhata unhu mubusungwa somwakulokwala muchikamu 50(7)(a).

Ijibilijo ponshaji wegubajo linowila banhu banji

(1) Kupamhija pejibilijo jilosumwa muchikamu 86, ilulamilo nolusungunuko lulokosha zwilodombolwa muChisiyaniso yechi zwingajibilijwa zwilondilila mbeli nomulawu ulokwalwa unosuma zwisungo zwokulingana nezwitiko zwabuzwa negubaji linowila banji, koga kuswika yopo panovumijwa nechikamu yechi muShejulu yabili.

(2) Mulawu ulokwala wasumwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) nezwimwe zwisungo zwatolwa kutobelwa mulawu yoyu zwinokwelela kubandwa muGazeti.

(3) Ijibilijo ipi zwayo, inoba mulawu ulokwalwa wabandwa muchibabi chechikamu (1), inojibija ilulamilo kana lusungunuko lukulugulu lulobandwa muBumbilo yeli aitokwelela kuba ihulwana kupinda yezwo zwinodikana ponshaji wegubaji.

(4) Apana mulawu unolebeleka pezhulu pokuzibiswa kwechimo chegubanjo lyabanji, akale apana zwisungo zwemilawu kana zwimwe zwisungo zwinotolwa kutobelejwa chizibiso chakadalo, unga—

(a)

tabilija kubetekeswa, kana ungavumila kutabilija kubetekeswa kwafulumende kana kuti makapo nenhabi jose jafulumende, kana umwe unhu zwe, penyaya jezwitiko zwisili pamulawu; kana

(b) kujibilija ilulamilo ipi zwayo ilodomwa muchikamu 86(3), kana kuvumila kuti ipi zwayo imwe yendulamo yejo ivunwe. CHISIYANISO 5 Butongi IMHINGU 1 Masimba obutongi 88

Masimba obutongi

(1) Masimba obutongi anova kubanhu beZimbabwe akale anokwelela kushingiswa kutobelwa iBumbilo lyoMilawu yeli.

(2) Masimba obutongi bweZimbabwe aimo mumaboko oNtungamili weNyika iye unowashingisa, etobeleja iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, eshingisa iGota lyaMakulukota. IMHINGU 2 Untungamili Wenyika Nabatobeli Bontungamili Wenyika 89

UNtungamili weNyika UNtungamili weNyika ndiye uNsholo weNyika nonkulwana wabatabaliji benyika.

2013

Mishingo youngamili wenyika

(1) UNtungamili wenyika unokwelela kutunhumwija, kutabilija, kutobeleja nokulemekeja iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli somulawu unkulugulu wenyika akale unokwelela kubona kuti iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli nemimwe milawu inotobelwa zwilobimbika.

(2) UNtungamili unokwelela—

(a)

kusungwaja kubhatana nolunyalalo munyika kutila kubhancha nokugajikana kwabanhu bose bomuZimbabwe;

(b) kutambula nokulemekeja zwibabalilo nokukosha kwehondo yolusungunuko;

(c)

kubona kuti ilulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko lwabo lukulugulu nokutonga komulawu zwinotobelejwa;

(d) kulemekeja kusiyana siyana kwabanhu namakapo abanhu bomuZimbabwe. 91

Unkwendeselo wokuvumilwa kushalwa soNtungamili kana kuti uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika

(1) Unhu unotubula kushalwa soNtungamili weNyika kana kuti unTobeli woNtungamilu weNyika kana—

(a)

eli unizi womuZimbabwe nokuzwalwa kana nebhatuko;

(c)

egala azwe muZimbabwe; akale

(b) eli aswincha makole makumi mana okuzwalwa;

(d) ekakwalisa kushala kana kuvota.

(2) Unhu aangatubule kushalwa soNtungamili weNyika kana kuti uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika kana akababa uNtungamili weNyika kana kuti uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika pasi peBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli kwezwikamu zwibili, zwisinani kuti zwikamu yezwi zwakabetobelana kana kuti mane akale kulingwa chibabi yechi makole matatu kana kupinda anotolwa sechikamu chilopelela. 92

Kushalwa koNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika

(1) Kushalwa koNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika babili kunokwelela kutiwa pakati ponshaji ulotalwa muchikamu 158.

(2) Wose ungunduji mukushalwa koNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kudoma banhu babili banoma naye mushalujo saBatobeli babe, akale unokwelela kudoma umwe wabanhu yabo soNtobeli uwe wokutanga akale umwe wadeni soNtobeli wabili.

(3) UNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika banoshalwa panupamwempela zomene nabashali balokwalisa muZimbabwe yose, akale inzila yokushalwa kwabo ilobandwa muMulawu weShalujo.

(4) Kutubula kukwalisa sonshali nokushala peshalujo joNtungamili weNyika kana kuti jaBatobeli baBatungamili beNyika jilobandwa muShejulu yechina.

(5) Kushalwa koNtungamili weNyika kana Batobeli baBatungamili beNyika kunokwelela kutiwa panu pamwempela nejose ishalujo jabamilili bomupalamende, mumakansili amatunhu namakansili omubugalo. 93

Kunyenyeja inshalujo jontungamili wenyika

(1) Kutobelwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, ungunduji upi azwe umunyunyuta anganyenyeja buchokomende bwokushalwa koNtungamili kana Batobeli boNtungamili

nokukwala lukwalo lokunyunyuta kugota lyezweBumbilo lyeNyika mukati mwamazhuba mashanu anamabili mushule mwokuzibizwa kwezwabhuda mushalujo.

(2) Kushalwa koNtungamili wenyika kunganyenyejwa kana kuli kuti washalwa ajilokwelela kushalwa.

(3) Igota lyezweBumbilo lyeMilawu linokwelela kuwhilila nokupa untongo pokunyenyeja kulotalwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) mumazhuba makumi anamana mushule mwokuswinchwa kolukwalo lwokunyenyeja, akale untongo wegota yeli ndiwo wokupejeleja.

(4) Mukupa untongo pokunyenyeja pasi pechibabi chechikamu (1), iGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu linga—

(a)

dombola wazunda;

(c)

kupa umwe untongo walinobona ukalulama akale ukakwelela.

(a)

iGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu likati ishalujo joNtungamili weNyika ajijilokwelela, kushalwa kwaBatobeli babili boNtungamili naiko kunova kwamiswa kumbali;

(b) suma kuti ishalujo ajijakakwenda nenzila ilolulama, akale kana zwadai imshalujo impya jinokwelela mukati mwamazhuba makumi mashanu anegumi mushule mwokupa untongo; kana

(5) Kana, mulukwalo lwokunyunyuta pasi pechibabi chechikamu (1)—

(b) iGota linolinga nozweBumbilo lyeMilawu likamisa kumbali kushalwa komwe uNtobeli woNtungamili kana kuti bose Batobeli boNtungamili, nokukambija uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kudoma unhu ulokwelela kana kuti banhu balokwelela, zwikwendelana nozumutikana, kuba uNtobeli woNtungamili kana Batobeli boNtungamili weNyika. 94

Kutanga unshingo Ntungamili wenyika nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika

(1) Banhu bashalwa soNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika banotanga unshingo kana bakata, mbeli kontongi unkulwana, uNtongi unkulugulu, kana kuti umwe untongi unkulwana unotobela wipo, ikowhe lyoNtungamili weNyika nelyoNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika zwilokwelela nenzila ilokwalwa muchipamhijo chatatu, iwo makowhe bekwelela kuwata—

(a)

nezhuba lyashanu namana mushule mwokudomwa kuti bashalwa; kana

(b) kana kuli kuti bululami bweshalujo bwebenyenyejwa, mukati mwama awa makumi mana namashanu anamatatu mushule mwokuti iGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu lyabadoma saibo bazunda.

(2) UNtungamili wipo unondilila mbeli nonshingo kuswika uNtungamuli washalwa atola ikowhe kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (1).

(3) UNtobeli woNtungamili unotola chibhula choNtungamili mushule mwokufa koNtungamili weNyika, kusiya unshingo kana kubhiswa pechibhula unotanga unshingo kana akatola, mbeli kontongi unkulwana UNtongi unkulugulu, kana kuti umwe untongi unkulwana unotobela, ikowhe lyoNtungamili weNyika lilokwalwa muShejulu Chatatu, iyo ikowhe yanokwelela kutola nokukambija akale kojaba kwata chini mukati mwama awa makumi mana anamashanu namatatu mushule mwokuba chibhula choNtungamili chasala chisina unhu.

2013

Chikamu chokushinga koNtungamili weNyika nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika

(1) Chikamu chokushinga koNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili chinotanga izhuba lyanoponchwa akale akatanga unshingo kutobelwa chikamu 94(1)

(a) kana kuti 94(3).

(2) Chikamu chokushinga kwoNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili chinondilila mbeli kuswika—

(a) akasiya unshingo kana kuti akabhiswa unshingo; kana

(b) mushule mweshalujo, wadomwa kuti washalwa bupya kana kuti uNtungamili weNyika umpya wadomwa kuti washalwa; akale, kuzhe kwezo zwilovumilwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, chikamu chonshingo wabo makole mashanu akale anokwendelana nechikamu chobupenyu bwePalamende. 96

Kusiya unshingo kwoNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika

(1) UNtungamili weNyika angasiya unshingo uwe nokukwalila uNdebelekeli, iye unokwelela kupa chizibiso chokusiya unshingo kulunji lwabanhu nokukambija akale kojoba zwikaba chini mukati mwama awa makumi mabili namana.

(2) UnTobeli wonTungamili weNyika angasiya unshingo nokukwalila unTungamili weNyika, iye unokwelela kupa chizibiso chokusiya unshingo kulunji lwabanhu nokukambija akale kojoba zwikaba chini mukati mwama awa makumi mabili namana. 97

Kubhiswa koNtungamili weNyika kana unTobeli wonTungamili weNyika ponshingo

(1) ISeneti neGota lyabaMilili, nokuvumilana panupamwempela kwechilabo chemhingu jabo jipo pakati nopakati, bangavumilana kuti umbhuzo wokuti uNtungamili weNyika kana kuti uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika ebhiswe pechibhula nenyaya yo—

(a) kuzwibhata bubi zwikulugulu;

(b) kukonelwa kutobela, kulondota kana kutabilijila iBumbilo lyeMilawu;

(c)

kuvuna iBumbilo lyeMilawo nomwaza;

(d) kukonelwa kuta mishingo yehofesi yiye pezhulu pokusotubula kwombili kana indangalilo;

kupoteseswe kutobelwa zwisungo zwechikamu yechi.

(2) Mushule mwokuta chivumilano kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (1), ikomithi yemilawu nezwisungo inokwelela kudoma ikomithi iloshangana imhingu jeSeneti neGota lyabamilili inanemhingu ishanu nena itobeleja chimo chobumilili bwezwamatongelo enyika muPalamende, kupoteseswa kubhiswa pechibhula koNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika, sezwa zwimutikana.

(3) Kana—

(a)

ikomithi inoshanganisila imhingu jengumba imbili yadomwa kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (2) ikakutaija kubhiswa pechibhula koNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika; akale

(b) iSeneti neGota lyabaMilili, nokuvumilana nobunji bwabanhu babili mubatatu bwemhingu jose, bakasuma kuti uNtungamili kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, sezwo zumutikana, ebhiswe pechibhula;

uNtungamili weNyika kana kuti uNtobeli woNtungamili unova ajiba pakalepo kubhata chibhula.

2013

Kusosungwa kwoNtungamili weNyika

(1) Echili pechibhula, uNtungamili weNyika aangamiswe mbeli kwegota pezhulu penyaya jokusowhanana mukugajikana kana jondandu jakatikana muzina lilye.

(2) Wakabeli uNtungamili weNyika angabetekeswa milandu yenyaya jokusowhanana nejobugebengu yakata kana yakaleka kuta esanhu aba uNtungamili weNyika kana kuti aba unNtungamili weNyika.

(3) Chikwelete choNtungamili chinobhutunuka esanhu agala pechuibhula yechi kana eli pechibhula chinomiswa kana engondila mbeli eli ponshingo.

(4) Mumakwendeselo api zwawo okubetekesa wakabeli uNtungamili weNyika, wakabeli uNtungamili unotubula kuzwitabilija nokuti zwinhu zwose wakazwita nechinangwa chibuya. 99

Mishingo yaBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika

Batobeli baNtungamili wenyika banobhancha uNtungamili weNyika mukuta mishingo iye nokuta imwe mishingo yabangapiwe noNtungamili weNyika, inoshanganisila kubona unkwendeselo weNhabi, chibabi kana Mulawu wePalamende. 100 Unowana ekabhata chibhula choNtungamili weNyika

(1) Pose yopo kana uNtungamili weNyika esimo munyika kana kuti kana esinotubula kuta mishingo iye yopomulawu pezhulu pokulwala kana chimwe chisoko, mishingo yeyo inokwelela kutolwa akale itiwe—

(a)

noNtobeli wokutanga woNtungamili weNyika;

(b) kana uNtobeli wokutanga woNtungamili weNyika esinotubula kuta mishingo yeyo, inotiwa noNtobeli wabili woNtungamili weNyika; kana

(c)

kana pesina uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika unotubula kuta mishingo yeyi, inotiwa neGulukota—

(i) lyadomwelwa chimo chakadali noNtungamili weNyika; kana kuti

(ii) lyadomwa neGota lyaMakulukota, kana kuli kuti apana iGulukota lyadomwa noNtungamili weNyika kutobelwa chibabi chendima (i).

(2) Kuzhe kwokuti kutobelwa chivumilwano chatiwa nabanji kupinda bamwe pakati pemhingu jose jeGota lyaMakulukota, unhu umuta mishingo yoNtungamili weNyika sokubhaunulwa kwatiwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) pezhulu aatokwelela kushingisa masimba oNtungamili weNyika—

(a)

kutuma Batabiliji beNyika kunobhata nshingo;

(b) kungwina muzwivumilano nenyika inji;

(c)

kugajika kana kubhisa pechibhula uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota kana kuti uNtobeli weGulukota; kana kuti

(d) kupa mishingo kana kushandula mishingo yapiwa kuNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota, kushanganisila, kana kuli uNtobeli weNyika kana iGulukota, unshingo wonkwendeselo woMulawu wePalamende kana iNhabi kana Chibabi, kana kumisa mishingo yeyi yapiwa. 101 Kutolwa kwechibhula mushule mwolufu, kusiya unshingo kana kusotubula kwoNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika

(1) Kana uNtungamili engofa, kusiya unshingo kana kuti akabhiswa pechibhula—

(a)

2013

uNtobeli wokutanga woNtungamili weNyika unotola chibhula kuswika unshaji wokupela kwechikamu chayoyo wakabeli uNtungamili;

(b) uNtobeli wechibili woNtungamili weNyika unotola chibhula soNtobeli wokutanga kuswika unshaji wokupela kwechikamu chayoyo wakabeli uNtungamili weNyika;

(c)

mushule mwokutanga unshingo soNtungamili weNyika, yoyo wakabeli uNtobeli wotanga woNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kudoma unhu ulokwelela kuti ebe uNtobeli wabili woNtungamili weNyika kuswika unshaji wokupela kwechikamu chayoyo wakabeli uNtungamili weNyika.

(2) Kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika akafa, akasiya unshingo kana kuti akabhiswa pechibhula—

(a)

uNtobeli wabili woNtungamili weNyika unotola chibhula choNtobeli wotanga kuswika kupela konshaji wechikamu chokushinga choNtobeli wokutanga; akale

(b) uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela nokukambija kudoma unhu ulokwelela kuti ebe uNtobeli wabili woNtungamili weNyika kuswika unshaji wokupela kwechikamu chokushinga chayoyo wakabeli uNtobeli wokutanga. 102 Indipo yoNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli baBatungamili beNyika

(1) UNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika bananelulamo yokuwana indipilo, imali yomulyandiligele nezwimwe zwilotalwa muMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Imali nama nejimwe indipilo joNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtobeli weNyika jinoshakwa akale jinokwelela kubhuda kuChikwama chaFulumende.

(3) Unhu wajiba kuba uNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika unanelulamilo yokuwana—

(a)

Mulyandiligele unokwelana nemali inoholwa noNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili, somwazulokwelela kubeli; akale

(b) jimwe imali jokubhancha nezwimwe bo zwinobekatalwa muMulawu wePalamende. 103 UNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika nayabo bakababa pezuwibhula yezwi abotokwelela kutola zwimwe zwibhula kana kungwina mishingo UNtungamili weNyika naBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika, akale unhu upi azwe wakababa uNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, abatokwelela, zwipo pachena kana nenzila isipo pachena, kutola chipi zwacho chibhula chokushingila banji kana kungwina unshingo kumwe bechili bakabhata chibhula kana kuti kana belimukutambula ipencheni kuva kunyika soNtungamili kana uNtobeli woNtungamili wakapinda, somwazulokwelela. IMHINGU 3 Makolokota, Batobeli Bamakolokota Negota Lyamakolokota 104 Kudomwa kwaMakolokota naBatobeli baMakolokota

(1) UNtungamili weNyika unodoma Makulukota akale etukobapa unshingo, unoshanganisila kubona unkwendeselo woMulawu wePalamende upi zwawo kana

iNhabi kana igapo, koga uNtungamili angazwilondotela iye nokukwe unshingo wokubona unkwendeselo woMulawu, iNhabi kana iGapo.

(2) UNtungamili wenyika angadoma Batobeli baMakulukota kuti bebhanche iGulukota lipi zwalo mukuta unshingo walyo.

(3) Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota banodomwa kuva kumaSeneta kana kuti iMhingu jeGota lyabaMilili, koga jinoswika inshanu, jashalilwa bumhizha bwemishingo nokutubula kwajo, jingadomwa kuva kuzhe kwePalamende.

(4) Mukudoma Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota, uNtungamili wenyika unokwelela kutungamilwa nokulangalila kukwelaniswa kwamatunhu nabechinhulume nabechinhukaji.

(5) Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota basili imhingu jePalamende bangagala akale belebeleke, koga abatoshala, muSeneti kana kuti Mugota lyabaMilili muPalamende.

(6) Asanhu atanga unshingo, unhu wadomwa seGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota unokwelela kutola ikowhe yeGulukota ilokwelela mbeli koNtungamili weNyika nenzila ilotalwa muShejulu yaTatu. 105 IGota lyaMakolokota

(1) Pana neGota linoshanganisila uNtungamili weNyika, sontungamili weGota, Batobeli boNtungamili weNyika naMakulukota yawo angadomwa noNtungamili weNyika kuba muGota lyaMakulukota.

(2) Mishangano yeGota lyaMakulukota inotungamilwa noNtungamili weNyika kana kuti, iye esipo, noNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika kana kuti, kana besipo, iGulukota lalebwa muchibabi 100(1)(c). 106 Kuzwibhata kwaBatobeli boNtungamili, Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota

(1) Wose uNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota naBatobeli boNtungamili banokwelela kutobela iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(2) Batobeli babaTungamili beNyika, Makulukota, naBatobeli baMakulukota abangatubule, nonshaji bechili pozwibhula—

(a)

pachena kana zwisili pachena, kubhata zwimwe zwibhula zwokushingila banji kana kuta unshingo wokuhola;

(b) kuzwibhata neipi inzila zwayo isinokwendelana nezwibhula zwabo, kana kuzwibiga muchimo chipi zwacho chinoshanganisila ingozi yokunyenyejana pezwibabalilo zwabo zwonshingo wabo nozwabo bekazwimilila boga;

(c) kushingisa zwibhula zwabo, kana chimwe chinyeulilo chabalobhatiswa muchibimbo, kuzwifumisa ibo pachabo kana kubhancha umwe unhu nenzila isilolulama.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kusuma inzila jokuzwibhata kwaBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika, Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota. 107 Undandu waBatobeli boNtungamili weNyika, Makulukota nabaTobeli boNtungamili weNyika

(1) Kutobelwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, wose uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota nonTobeli weGulukota bananondandu, bekazwimilila akale panupamwempela, kuNtungamili weNyika penyaya jokuta unshingo wabo.

(2) Wose uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota nonTobeli weGulukota unokwelela kuswika kuPalamende nokumakomithi epalamende kunoshandula mibhuzo ilonangana nenyaya jaanolinga eli ega kana kuti janolinga panupamwempela nabamwe. 108 Chikamu chokushinga kwaMakulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota

(1) Chibhula cheGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota chinozhulika—

(a)

kana uNtungamili weNyika akoombhisa pechibhula;

(b) kana akasiya unshingo nechizibiso chilokwalwa kuNtungamili weNyika;

(c) panotanga uNtungamili weNyika umpya unshingo uwe.

(2) Kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (4), iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota lyakabeli iMhingu yePalamende pakagajikwa seGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota unosiya chibhula paanojiba kuba iMhingu yabamilili muPalamende.

(3) Kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (4), iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota usakabeli iMhingu yePalamende paakagajikwa kuba iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota unosiya chibhula chiche kana kukaba nechimo chokuti chibhula chiche chasala chikashama.

(4) Kutobelwa iBumbilo yeli, kana zwikatikana kuti iGota lyabaMilili likazakunulwa, Makulukota naBatobeli babaTungamili banondilila mbeli beli pozwibhula zwabo kuswika unshaji uNtungamili weNyika umpya washalwa atola chibhula chiche mushule mweshalujo. 109 Ishalujo jokushayiwa chibimbo munaFulumende

(1) ISeneti neGota lyabaMilili muPalamende, beshingisa chivumilano chatiwa panupamwempela nabanhu babili mubatatu bemhingu jose, zwingashala kusuma kushayiwa chibimbo munaFulumende.

(2) Kusundijila chivumilwano chenshalujo jokusuma kushayiwa chibimbo kungatiwa chete kana—

(a)

chizibiso chamazhuba mashanu anamabili kana kupinda chapiwa kundebelekeli; akale

(b) chizibiso chokusundijila chasainwa nechilabo chemhingu chipo pakati nopakati babaMilili bose beGota lyabaMilili muPalamende.

(3) Kusundijila ishalujo jokokushayiwa chibimbo—

(a)

kunokwelelwa kunyenyejwa munshangano weNgumba jePalamende imbili panupamwempela mukati mwamazhuba makumi mabili nelimwempela mushule mwokuba uNdebelekeli atambula chizibiso chokusundijila; akale

(b) kunokwelela kushalujwa mukati mwemishangano mishanu nemibili inotobelana mushule mwokusundijila; zwikasotikana yezwi kusundijila kunotolwa sokwazundwa.

(4) Kana iPalamende ikavuma kuti ayichina chibimbo munaFulumende, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela, mukati mwamazhuba igumi namana mushule mweshalujo—

(a)

kubhisa makulukota ose naBatobeli bose baMakulukota pezwibhula, kuzhe kokuti basiya kale unshingo pezhulu yechivumilano, etukodoma bamwe banhu pobugalo bwabo; kana

(b) kuzakunula iPalamende akale, mukati mwamazhuba makumi mashanu anamakumi mana, eshobe inshalujo ihulwana.

(5) Kana uNtungamili weNyika akasota sozwalebwa muchibabi chechikamu

(4) mukati mwamazhuba igumi linamana mushule mwokuvumilana kushayiwa kwechibimbo munaFulumende, iPalamende inova yazakulunika. IMHINGU 4 Mishingo Yobutongi Bwenyika 110 Mishingo yobutongi yoNtungamili naMakulukota

(1) UNtungamili weNyika unamasimba aanopiwa neBumbilo yeli noMulawu upi zwawo wePalamende kana umwe mulawu, kushanganisila yeyo ilokwelela kuti ete mishingo yoNsholo weNyika.

(2) Kutobelwa iBumbilo yeli uNtungamili unanemishingo yo—

(a) kuvuma nokusaina maBhili;

(b) kuswincha Mulawu uchinofhulwa kuGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu kushakwa kululamiswa kuti Mulawu uchinofhulwa yeyo unokwendelana neBumbilo;

(c)

kushoba iGota lyabaMilili, iSeneti kana iPalamende kunshangano unkulugulu kota unshingo ulosiyana namazhuba ose;

(d) kudoma banhu pezwibhula yezwo zwinowhi neBumbilo lyeMilawu kana nomulawu uNtungamili edome;

(e)

(f)

kushoba ishalujo kutobelwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli;

kushoba ireferendamu penyaya ipi zwayo kutobelwa mulawu;

(g) kutuma Batabiliji beNyika;

(h) kupa lulemekejo nendipilo;

(i) kudoma bamilili kunejimwe inyika, banopiwa masimba, banobona bulyijani nabashingi mumahofisi anomilila inyika kuzhe;

(j) nokutambula nokulingisisa bamilili bokunejimwe inyika yomuno nabanoshinga mumahofisi ejimwe inyika yomuno;

(3) Kutobelwa iBumbilo yeli iGota lyaMakulukota linanonshingo wo—

(a)

kululamisa kushinga kwaFulumende;

(c)

kubambanyila, kupalula nokubona kuti milawu yenyika imushinga;

(e)

kupa mano kuNtungamili wenyika.

(b) kuta unshingo waFulumende muPalamende;

(d) kubumba nokubona kuti zwisungo zwenyika zwimushinga;

(4) Kutobelwa iBumbilo yeli uNtungamili wenyika angata chivumilano kana kuta zwimo muzwivumilano nejimwe inyika nabafulumende namakapo enyika inji.

(5) Chisungo choNtungamili weNyika chinokwelela kube kakwalwa kana chatulwa kutobelwa Mulawu.

(6) Mukuta mishingo yiye yobutongi, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kushinga etobela maano eGota lyaMakulukota, kuzhe kana payopo paanobeta mishingo sokubhaunulwa muchibabi chechikamu (2) pezhulu. 111 IHondo nolunyalalo

(1) UNtungamili weNyika unamasimba okusuma ihondo nawokusuma lunyalalo, akale unokwelela kuzibisa iSeneti neGota lyabaMilili muPalamende mukati mwamazhuba mashanu namabili okuta mishangano mushule mwokushingisa masimba akadali.

(2) ISeneti neGota lyabaMilili muPalamende, nechivumilano chatiwa nemhingu imbili munhatu jemhingu jose jePalamende, zwingavumilana kuti isumo yehondo imiswe.

(3) Yopo kana iPalamende yavumilana kuti isumo yehondo imiswe, uNtungamili unokwelela kutola jose inzila jinotubulika kubhuda muhondo yeyo, elangalila kulondoteka kwabanhu beZimbabwe nezwishingiso zwenyika. 112 Masimba okulekejela

(1) UNtungamili weNyika, mushule mwokukwejisa neGota lyaMakolkota, angashingisa masimba okulekejela, yezwi zwileba kuti anga—

(a)

pa lulekejelo kunhu upi zwe ulobhatikana kana kuti wakawanikwa elinondandu pamulawu;

(b) misa kutiwa kontongo wondandu upi zwawo konshaji undefu zwilojibilijwa kana konshaji ulojibilijwa;

(c)

suma undandu undelu panooyo wasumwa pandandu upi zwawo; kana

(d) biga kumbali kwonshaji ulodomwa kana kubwiliza kuGota undandu wose kana chikamu chondandu upi zwawo kuti ulingisiswe kana kutolelwa ifuma nenyaya yondandu upi zwawo;

akale angasuma zwinokwelela kutobelwa polulekejelo, lunyetutuko, kubwilizila kana kubigwa kumbali.

(2) Kana unhu unogala muZimbabwe akawanikwa eli nondandu muneimwe inyika kulingwa milawu inoshinga munyika yakaleyo, uNtungamili angasuma kuti kuwanikwa elinondandu yoku akungatambulwe sokuwanikwa elinondandu mukushingiswa kweBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli kana umwe mulawu umushinga muZimbabwe.

(3) Kulekejelwa kana kubigwa kumbali kontongo kunokwelela kubhiswa muGazeti. 113 Unshaji wegubajo kubanji

(1) UNtungamili weNyika eshingisa chizibiso muGazeti angasuma kuti pananechimo chinobiga banji muZimbabwe kana kunelimwe ibhazu lyenyika zwalyo mugubaji.

(2) Isumo yechimo chinobiga banji mugubajo inopela kushinga mushule mwamazhuba igumi linamana kutanga nezhuba lyokuzibiswa kwesumo yeyo muGazeti kuzhe kwokuti, kusanu kwapela unshaji yoyo, isumo yeyo ivumwe nechikamu imbili munhatu kana kupinda chabamilili bose bePalamende ponshangano unotiwa neSeneti neGota lyabamili panupamwempela.

(3) Kana iPalamende likazakunyulwa mumazhuba igumi namana mushule mwokusumwa kwechimo chegubajo kubanji, isumo yeyo inopela kushinga mushule mwamazhuba makumi mabili nelimwempela, kutanga pezhuba lyokubhaunulwa kwesumo muGazeti, kuzhe kwokuti mukati mwonshaji yoyo isumo yeyo inovumwa nabanji bemhingu jose jePalamende impya munshangano weSeneti neGota lyabamilili panupamwempela.

(4) Isumo yechimo chegubajo kubanji yavumwa kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu

(2) kana (3) inondilila mbeli ishinga kwemweji mitatu kuvila izhuba chizibiso pachakabhaunulwa muGazeti kuzhe kokuti yakabalambwa kana kupela kushinga sokupandululwa kumo muchikamu yechi.

(5) Kana isumo yechimo chegubajo kubanji chisavumwa mushule

mwokukwejisa kwePalamende, kana kuti pezhulu pechisoko chipi zwacho isakwejiswa nePalamende mukati mwonshaji ulolebwa muchikamu yechi, uNtungamili unokwelela, mukati mwamazhuba mashanu namabili, nokupa chizibiso muGazeti, kubiga kumbali isumo yeyo.

(6) Kana, kutobelwa chivumilano chatiwa nabanji bemhingu jimo munshangano weSeneti neGota lyabamilili panupamwempela, iPalamende ikasunga kuti isumo yechimo chegubaji kubanji—

(a)

indilile mbeli kwonshaji usinopinda mweji mitatu, pesina kujilwa uNtungamili unokwelela, nokuzibisa muGazeti, kupamhija wesumo kwonshaji wakalewo;

(b) inokwelela kumiswa kana kuti inokwelela kushinga mubugalo butukutuku, pesina kujilwa uNtungamili wenyika unokwelela, nokuzibisa muGazeti kumisa isumo yeyo kana kubhaunula kuti isumo yeyo ishinge mubugalo butukutuku.

(7) IGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu, umwe unhu unanebabalilo eli akumbila, lingatonga kutambulika—

(a)

kwesumo yechimo chegubajo kubanji;

(b) kupamhijwa konshaji wesumo yechimo chegubajo kubanji.

(8) Igota lipi zwalyo lingatonga kutambulika komulawu upi zwawo wafhulwa, kana chitiko chatolwa, kutobelwa isumo yechimo chegubajo kubanji. IMHINGU 5 Igweta Igulwana 114 IGweta igulwana

(1) PananeGweta igulwana linodomwa noNtungamili wenyika.

(2) Unhu wadomwa seGweta igulwana unotanga unshingo mushule mwokutola, mbeli koNtungamili wenyika, kana umwe unhu wapiwa imvuma noNtungamili wenyika, ikowhe lyokuzwipila nonshingo nenzila ilotalwa muShejulu yaTatu.

(3) Unhu unotubula kudomwa segweta igulwana kana etubula kudomwa sontongi weGota igulwana.

(4) Mishingo yeGweta igulwana ngeinoti—

(a)

kuba umpi wamano unkulugulu penyaya jemilawu kunaFulumende;

(c)

kufhula milawu likamilila Fulumende;

(e)

kuta imwe mishingo ingapiwa iGweta igulwana lyaFulumende noMulawu wePalamende;

(b) kumilila Fulumende munkwendeselo wenyaya jokukakabajana zwimo mumulawu;

(d) kukutaija, kutabilija nokutobeleja kutonga komulawu nokutabilija zwibabalilo zwabanhu;

akale iGweta igulwana lyaFulumende lingashingisa mishingo yeyi nokwalo kana kuti naBatobeli balo beshinga pasi ponkwala nezwilulamiso zwinova kuGweta igulwana lyaFulumende.

(5) IGweta igulwana lyaFulumende—

(a)

lingangwina mumishangano yeGota lyaMakulukota, koga alitoshala;

(b) lingangwina nokulebeleka muSeneti nomuGota lyabaMilili muPalamende, koga alitoshala; akale

(c)

lyapiwa imvumo negota lilokwelela, kuma mbeli kwegota sonkwaina wegota penyaya jipi zwajo jisinani naFulumende.

115 Kubhiswa unshingo kweGweta igulwana lyaFulumende UNtungamili weNyika angabhisa iGweta igulwana lyaFulumende unshingo noumpi zwawo unshaji. CHISIYANISO 6 Zwokubumba Milawu IMHINGU 1 Masimba Okubumba Milawu 116 Babumbi bemilawu Inhabi yezokubumbwa kwemilawu muZimbabwe inobumbwa nePalamende noNtungamili betobeleja Chisiyaniso chakalechi. 117 Chimo namasimba enhabi inobumba milawu

(1) Masimba okubumba milawu muZimbabwe anova kubanhu akale alopiwa akale anoshingiswa nenhabi yokubumbwa kwemilawu kutobelwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(2) Masimba okubumba milawu anopa inhabi yokubumbwa kwemilawu isimba—

(a)

lyokushandula mulawu kutobelejwa chikamu 328;

(c)

nokupa masimba opasi kunaejimwe inhabi kutobelwa chikamu 134.

(b) lyokubumba milawu yolunyalalo, kugajikana nokutongwa kunabululami kweZimbabwe; IMHINGU 2 IPalamende 118 IPalamende IPalamende linobumbwa neSeneti neGota lyabamilili muPalamende. 119 Mishingo yePalamende

(1) IPalamende inokwelela kutabilija Ibumbilo lyeMilawu yeli nokukutaija kutonga kubuyanana kunotobeleja zwido zwabanhu banji.

(2) IPalamende inanamasimba okubona kuti zwisungo zweBumbilo lyeMilawu zwinotobelwa nokuti iNyika nenhabi jose namakapo ose afulumende jinota zwinhu jitobela iBumbilo nezwibabalilo zwenyika.

(3) Kutila chibabi chechikamu (2), inhabi jose namakapo ose eNyika nafulumende anobhaunula unshingilo wawo kuPalamende.

IMHINGU 3 ISeneti 120 Imhingu jeSeneti

(1) ISeneti inobumbwa namaSeneta makumi mashanu namakumi matatu, banotobela—

(a)

bashanu nomwempela banoshalwa kuva kudunhu limwe nelimwe lyomuZimbabwe, kushingiswa inzila yokumilila kunokwendelana nechilabo chabanhu bashalwa zwikwendelana nechibabi chechikamu (2);

(b) igumi nabashanu nomwempela maShe, babili babo banoshalwa negota lyamatunhu lyamaShe kuva kudunhu limwe nelimwe, kuzhe kwamatunhu amaguta makulwana, linopasanula iZimbabwe;

(c)

uNtungamili noNtobeli woNtungamili weGota lyenyika lyamaShe;

(d) babili banoshalwa nenzila ilobhaunulwa muMulawu weNshalujo kumilila banhu banabukosha.

(2) Ishalujo jamaSeneta jinokwelela kutiwa kutobelwa Mulawu weShalujo, iwo unokwelela kubona kuti maSeneta abhaunulwa muchibabi chechikamu (1)(a) anoshalwa kuvamumazina amakapo kutobelwa inzila yokuti bumilili bunokwendelana nabashali—

(a)

ilosendama pamavoti awanwa nabagunduji balomilila makapo ezwamatongelo enyika mudunhu limwe nelimwe munshalujo jikulwana jabaMilili muGota lyabaMilili;

(b) banhulume nabanhukaji balodomwa zwilipesaniswa betobelana, undungwe wose utanga nongunduji unhukaji. 121 Kuvumilwa nokusovumilwa kushalwa saSeneta

(1) Unhu unotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa saSeneta wadomwa muchikamu 120(1)(a) kana (d) kana—

(a)

ekakwalisa kuba unshali; akale

(b) elinamakole makumi mana kana kupinda;

kuzhe kokuti esinotubula kuvumilwa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (4) kana (5).

(2) Unhu unotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa saSeneta wamaShe wabhaunulwa muchikamu 120(1)(b) kana—

(a)

eli iShe; akale

(b) kana ekakwaliwa sonshali;

kuzhe kwokuti esinotubula kuvumilwa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (4) kana (5).

(3) Unhu unotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa saSeneta walebwa muchikamu 120(1)

(d) kana eli unhu unabukosha sokubhaunulwa muMulawu weShalujo, kuzhe kwokuti ejibiswa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (4) kana (5).

(4) Unhu aatotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa saSeneta kana—

(a)

ejibiswa pasi peShejulu yeChina kukwalisa sonshali; kana kuti

(b) mukati mwamakolekusanu kwasika inshalujo, wakasiya chibhula chiche jomuSeneti kana chomuGota lyabamilili pasi pechikamu 129(1)(i) nokuwanikwa elinondandu.

(5) Unhu aatotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa musnhalujo jokuzhajikija chibhula chomuSeneti kana eli iMhingu yeGota lyabamilili muPalamende. 122 UNtungamili weSeneti

(1) Munshangano wayo wokutanga mushule mweshalujo ihulwana akale kusanu kwendilila mbeli noupi zwawo unshingo, iSeneti inokwelela kushala unobona nezwonkwendeselo iye unooshobwa ewhi uNtungamili weSeneti.

(2) Pose pakunoba nebanga pechibhula choNtungamili weSeneti, iSeneti inokwelela kushala unhu kuti etole chibhula yechi nokukambija.

(3) Unhu unotubula kushalwa soNtungamili weSeneti kana eli kana kuti akababa Seneta kana kuti unotubula kushalwa muSeneti.

(4) Ishalujo joNtungamili weSeneti jinokwelela kutiwa noNkwali wePalamende jilulamiswa neKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, musiswa kutobelwa milawu yepalamende, akale zwabhuda zwinokwelela kuzibiswa nokukambija.

(5) Esanu atanga unshingo, uNtungamili weSeneti unokwelela kutola ikowhe mbeli kwoNtongi unkulugulu, kana untongi unkulwana eta ikowhe lyokuzwipila nokushinga somwazwilotalwa muShejulu yatatu.

(6) Seneta unoshalwa kuba uNtungamili weSeneti uojiba kuba iSeneta, akale chibhula chazhulika chinokwelela kuzhajikijwa kutobelejwa Milawu yeShalujo.

(7) UNtungamili weSeneti angasiya chibhula nokuzibisa iSeneti nokukwe kana kuti, kana iSeneti isina mishangano, nokukwalila uNkwali wePalamende.

(8) UNtungamili weSeneti unokwelela kubhuda pechibhula—

(a)

pezhuba iSeneti painoshangana kwokutanga mushule mweshalujo ihulwana;

(b) paanovuma kutola chimwe chibhula chokushingila banji kana kuti panongwina umwe unshingo kumwe unhu upi zwe;

(c)

paanoba imhingu yePalamende kana kuti uNdebelekeli;

(e)

kana kukamuka chimo chalumbhata nesimba kuti esiye chibhula chiche ngana kuli kuti wabeli Seneta; kana

(d) paanoba uNtobeli woNtungamili, iGulukota kana kuti uNtobeli weGulukota;

(f)

kana chivumilwano chokumbisa pechibhula chavumilwanwa nechikamu chababili mubatatu kana kupinda chemhingu jose jeSeneti.

123 UNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti

(1) Nokukambija kunotubulika mushule mwokushala uNtungamili weSeneti kutobela ishalujo ihulwana, iGota iSeneti inokwelela Seneta kuba uNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti.

(2) Pose yopo pakunoba nechibhula chisina unhu choNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti, iSeneti pesina kujilwa inokwelela kushala unhu uchatola chibhula yecho.

(3) Ishalujo joNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti jinokwelela kutiwa noNkwali wePalamende elulamiswa neKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, musiswa kutobela milawu yePalamende, akale zwabhuda zwinokwelela kuzibiswa pesina kujilwa.

(4) Esanu atanga mishingo iye, uNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti unokwelela kutola ikowhe lyokuzwipila nelonshingo mbeli kwoNtongi unkulugulu, kana kuti umwe umbetekesi unkulwana unotobela unowanikwa somwakulotalwa muShejulu yaTatu.

(5) UNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti angasiya unshingo nokupa chizibiso iye nokukwe kuGota lyeSeneti kana, kana iGota lyeSeneti isinoshangana, nokupa chizibiso kuNtungamili weSeneti kana, uNtungamili weGota lyeSleneti esipo, kuzibisa uNkwali wePalamende. kana—

(6) UNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti unokwelela kusiya chibhula chiche

(a)paanojiba kuba iSeneta;

(b) paanoba uNdebelekeli;

(c)

paanoba uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota;

(d) kana chivumilwano chokuti ebhiswe chikavumwa nechikamu chababili mubatatu bemhingu jose jeGota lyeSeneti. IMHINGU 4 Igota Lyabamilili 124 Chimo cheGota lyabaMilili muPalamende

(1) Igota lyabamilili linobumbwa—

(a)

nemhingu mazana mabili anegumi jinoshalwa musiswa kuva kumatunhu mazana mabili anegumi munoba imo Zimbabwe mwayilopasanulwa; akale

(b) kwonshaji wamaPalamende mabili okutanga mushule mwokutanga kushinga, kunoamhijwa imhingu jechinhukaji makumu mashanu anegumi, bashanu nomwempela kuva kudunhu limwe nelimwe.

(2) Ishalujo jabaMilili muGota lyabaMilili muPalamende jinokwelela kutiwa kutobelwa Milawu yeshalujo.

(3) Kutubula kushalwa sonshali nokushala mushalujo jabaMilili muGota lyabamilili kulotalwa muShejulu yeChina. 125 Kuvumilwa nokusovumilwa kushalwa kuba imhingu yeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende

(1) Unhu unotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa seMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende kana—

(a)

ekakwalisa kuba unshali mushalujo; akale

(b) kana eli namakole makumi mabili nelimwempela kana kupinda;

kuzhe kokuti esinotubula kuvumilwa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (2) kana (3).

(2) Unhu aatotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa seMhingu muGota lyabamilili muPalamende kana—

(a) esinovumilwa pasi peShejulu yeChina kukwalisa sonshali mushalujo; kana

(b) mukati mwamakole mashanu pasanu patiwa ishalujo wakasiya chibhula chiche muSeneti kana kuti muGota lyabaMilili kutobelejwa chikamu 129(1)(i) eli ata undandu.

(3) Unhu aatotobula kuvumilwa kushalwa kushalujo jipi zwajo jokuzhajikija chibhula muGota lyabaMilili muPalamende.

126 Undebelekeli weGota lyabaMilili muPalamende

(1) Munshangano wokutanga mushule mweshalujo ihulwana, akale kusanu kwandililwa mbeli nomwe unshingo, iGota lyabaMilili linokwelela kushala unobona nezwonkwendeselo unooshobwa ewhi UNdebelekeli.

(2) Pose pakunoba nonshingo pechibhula choNdebelekeli, iGota lyabaMilili muPalamende linokwelela pesina kujilwa kushala unhu uchazhajikija chibhula yecho.

(3) Unhu unotubula kuvumilwa kushalwa soNdebeleki kana akababa iMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili kana kuti kana etubula kuvumilwa kushalwa seMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili.

(4) Ishalujo joNdebelekeli jinokwelela kutiwa noNkwali wePalamende elulamiswa neKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, musiswa kutobelwa Milawu yomuPalamende, akale zwabhuda zwinokelela kuzibiswa pakalepo mushule mweshalujo.

(5) Esanu atanga mishingo yiye, uNdebelekeli unokwelela kutola ikowhe mbeli kwoNtongi unkulugulu, kana umwe untongi unkulwna unotobela upo ikowhe lyokuzwipila nelyonshingo nenzila ilokwalwa muShejulu yaTatu.

(6) IMhingu yeGota lyabMilili muPalamende yashalwa kuba uNdebelekeli uojiba kuba iMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili, akale chibhula chazhulika chinokwelela kuzhajikijwa kutobelwa Mulawu weShalujo.

(7) UNdebelekeli angasiya chibhula nokuzibisa kusiya kukwe kuGota lyabaMilili muPalamende kana kuti, kana iGota lyabaMilili muPalamende lisikumishangana, kushingisa chizibiso chilokwalwa kuNkwali wePalamende.

(8) UNdebelekeli unokwelela kusiya chibhula chiche—

(a)

pezhuba iGota lyabaMilili pabanoshangana pokutanga mushule mwenshalujo ihulwana;

(b) mushule mwokutambula kushingila imwe ihofisi inota nozwabanji kana kuti paanongwina unshingo kunoumwe unhu zwe;

(c)

paanoba iMhingu yePalamende kana kuti uNtungamili weSeneti;

(e)

kukamuka chimo chalumbhata nesimba kuti esiye chibhula, ngana wabeli iMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende;

(d) paanoba uNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika, iGulukota kana kuti uNtobeli weGulukota;

(f)

kana chivumilano chokuti ebhiswe pechibhula chatiwa nabanhu zwikamu babili mubatatu bemhingu jose jeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende.

127 UNtobeli woNdebelekeli weGota lyabaMilili muPalamende

(1) Nokukambija kunotubulika mushule mwokushala uNdebelekeli kutobela ishalujo ihulwana, iGota lybaMilili muPalamende linokwelela kushala imwe iMhingu yalo kuti libe uNtobeli woNdebelekeli.

(2) Pose pakunoba nonshingo pechibhula choNtobeli woNdebelekeli, iGota lyabaMilili muPlamende linokwelela pesina kujilwa kushala unhu uchazhajikija chibhula.

(3) Ishalujo joNtobeli woNdebelekeli jinokwelela kutiwa noNkwali wePalamende elipasi pokululamiswa kweKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, musiswa kutobelwa Milawu yePalamende, akale zwabhuda zwinokwelela kuzibiswa pakalepo mushule mweshalujo yejo.

(4) Esanu atanga mishingo iye, uNtobeli woNdebelekeli wePalamende unokwelela kuta mbeli kwoNtongi unkulwana, kana umwe uMbetekesi unkulwana unotobela unoba elipo, ikowhe yokuzwipila neyonshingo somwakulotalwa muShejulu yaTatu.

(5) UNtobeli woNdebelekeli angasiya unshingo nokusuma kusiya nokukwe kuGota lyabMilili kana, kana iGota lyabaMilili lisinoshangana, kupa chizibiso chilokwala kuNdebelekeli kana, kana pesina uNdebelekeli, kuNkwali wePalamende.

(6) UNtobeli woNdebelekeli unokwelela kusiya chibhula—

(a)

paanojiba kuba iMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende;

(c)

paanoba uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota;

(b) paanoba uNtungamili weSeneti;

(d) kana chivumilwano chokubhiswa kukwe pechibhula kwatambulwa nechikamu chababili mubatatu kana kupinda jemhingu jose jeGota lyabaMilili. IMHINGU 5 Bulefu Bwonshaji Wokushinga Kwabamilili 128 Ikowhe lyoMilili muPalamende

(1) UMilili muGota lyePalamende usanu atola bugalo bubwe muPalamende unokwelela kuta ikowhe lyeMhingu yePalamende somwakulotalwa muShejulu yechiTatu.

(2) Ikowhe imulebwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) inokwelela kutolwa mbeli koNkwali wePalamende. 129 Bulefu bwonshaji wokushinga kwoMilili muPalamende

(1) Chibhula choMilili wePalamende chinozhulika—

(a)

pakuzakunulwa kwepalamende;

(c)

uMilili muPalamende paanoba uNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika;

(b) uMilili muPalamende paanosiya unshingo nechizibiso chilokwalwa kuNtungamili weGota lyeSeneti kana uNdebelekeli wePalamende, zwikwendelana nokuti imo muneipi ingumba;

(d) uMilili muPalamende paanoba uNtungamili weSeneti kana uNdebelekeli kana iMhingu yeyimwe iNgumba;

(e)

(f)

kana uMilili muPalamende paajiba kutubula kuvumilwa kukwalisa sonshali;

kana, pesina imvumo kuva kuNdebelekeli kana uNtungamili weSeneti, zwikwendelana nokuti umo muneyipi ingumba, uMilili muPalamende akasozha kungumba kwaali iMhingu kwamazhuba makumi mabili nelimwempela ingumba yeyo painoshangana, akale ingumba yakaleyo ikavumilana nokushala kwemhingu chikamu chomwempela mubabili kana kupinda chemhingu jose chibhula yecho chinozhulika;

(g) kana umilili muPalamende akavuma kushinga muhofisi yabanji kana kuti muhofisi semhingu yegapo lilobumbwa nomulawu, igapo limolulamiswa nafulumende ikansili yedunhu, yomuguta kana kuti yeomubugalo;

(h) kana uMilili muPalamende abeli unhu unoshingila banji kana kuti unshingi wegapo lilobumbwa nomulawu, igapo linolulamiswa nafulumende, ikansili yedunhu kana iguta kana kuti yomubugalo pezhuba lyasumwa semhingu, akale kova ekonwa kusiya chibhula yecho, kana kuba imhingu kana unshingo mukati mwamazhuba makumi matatu mushule mwezhuba yelo;

(i)

kana uMilili muPalamende awanikwa—

(i) muZimbabwe elinondandu unoshanganisila wokuvunwa kwechibimbo, kusobimbika kana kulwana sezwilokosha mundandu yoyo;

(ii) kuzhe kweZimbabwe ilinokuzwibhata kokuti kana kwatiwa muZimbabwe kwaluba undandu unoshanganisila kuvunwa kwechibimbo, kusobimbika kana kulwana sezwilokosha mundandu yoyo;

akale etukutongelwa kugala mujele kwemyeji mishanu nomwempela kana kupinda pesina kuvumilwa kubhadala ifaini kana kuti pesina chibaka chokupiwa umwe untongo wokusovalilwa, kuzhe kokuti mushule mwokulilijika untongo unobigwa kumbali kana kuti untongo wokuvalilwa unoshomopajwa kumyeji ipo pasi pemishanu nomwempela kana kuti kwabigwa umwe untongo kuzhe kowokuvalilwa pobugalo bwontongo wokuvalilwa;

(j)

kana iMhingu yasumwa kuti aichina imali inotubula kulipa zwikwelete zwayo kutobelwa mulawu umushinga muZimbabwe akale aajanu akabhuda muchimo yecho kana kuwanikwa ekakwelela kuwanikwa abhuda muchimo yecho, kana kuti kana iMhingu yata chivumilwano chakadalo kutobelwa nooyo wakaicheneka kutobelwa mulawu wakadalo akale chivumilwano yecho chisanu chabigwa kumbali;

(k) uMilili muPalamende wajiba kuba iMhingu yegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika lyaakabeli iMhingu paakashalwa kuba muPalamende akale igapo lyezwamatongelo enyika yelo, nechizibiso chilokwalila uNdebelekeli kana uNtungamili weGota lyeSeneti, somwakulokwelela, lyasuma kuti iMhingu yeyo aichino mugapo yelo;

(l)

kana iMhingu isebeli imhingu yegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika payakashalwa kuba muPalamende, ikaba imhingu yegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika;

(m) kana iMhingu ikawanikwa kuti inolwala indangalilo kana kuti kuyeya kwayo akujilopelela kutobelwa mulawu upi zwawo womuZimbabwe;

(n) kana iMhingu yawanikwa nondandu pasi poMulawu weShalujo akale iGota lyopezhulu lililasuma kuti imhingu yeyo aitotubula kuvumilwa kukwalisa sonshali munejipi zwajo ishalujo.

(2) Imhingu yadomwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) (i) unobata chililijiko pezhulu pontongo uwe, angendila mbeli kuba umilili nokuwana indipo semhingu, kuzhe kwokuti kana igota lyapa untongo wokuti egale mubusungwa kukamililwa zwinobhuda mushule mwechililijiko chiche.

(3) IMhingu yePalamende inoba ungalichibhula wekansili yedunhu unosiya chigalo chaabekabhata esanu atanga unshingo wongalichibhula.

IMHINGU 6 Masimba Okubumba Mulawu Naamwe 130 Masimba nemishingo yeSeneti neGota lyabaMilili

(1) Kuzhe somwakulovumilwa muShejulu yaShanu, mukuta mishingo yayo yemilawu zwose iSeneti neGota lyabaMilili zwinanesimba lyokusungula, kubambanyila, kulingisisa nokulamba kutambula mulawu upi zwawo.

(2) Kupamhija pezhulu pemishingo yayo ilodomwa muBumbilo lyemilawu yeli, iSeneti neGota lyabaMilili zwingata imwe mishingo yazwinopiwa kana yatulwa pazwili pasi pomulawu upi zwawo. 131 Milawu yePalamende nenzila jokuyibumba

(1) Masimba ePalamende ezwemilawu anoshinga nokubumbwa kweMilawu yePalamende.

(2) Mulawu uchinofhulwa wa—

(a)

tulwa ukavumwa najose iNgumba jePalamende; akale

(b) vumwa ukasainwa noNtungamili weNyika;

zwinokwendelana neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(3) Mawhi okubumba muMulawu wePalamende anoti “Wakabumbwa nePalamende akale noNtungamili weZimbabwe”, kana kuti mawhi anoleba zwinokwendelana nozwakalezo.

(4) Unkwendeselo unootobelwa neGota lyabaMilili neSeneti penyaya jamaBhili ulotalwa muShejulu yashanu.

(5) Mushule mwokuti Mulawu uchinofhulwa yavumwa neNgumba jose imbili somwakulotalwa muShejulu yashanu, uNtungamili weSeneti kana uNdebelekeli, somwakulokwelela, unokwelela pesina kujilwa—

(a)

kuta kuti itulwe kuNtungamili weNyika kuti eyivume akale eyisaine, panupamwempela nechitupa chipi zwacho chinodiwa neBumbilo yeli kuti chikwende panupamwempela noMulawu uchinofhulwa;

(b) kupa chizibiso kubanji chezhuba Mulawu uchinofhulwa payakasengwa kuNtungamili; akale

(6) Kana Mulawu uchinofhulwa ukatulwa kuNtungamili weNyika kuti elitambule zwipo pomulawu nokuusaina, unokwelela mumazhuba makumi mabili nelimwempela kuta chimwempela pakati pezwinotobela—

(a)

kuntambula zwipo pomulawu nokuusaina, etukota kuti ushambajwe muGazeti pesina kujilwa;

(b) kana eyeya kuti autotobela iBumbilo lyoMulawu kana kuti elinokusugunchikana nawo, kubwiliza Mulawu uchinofhulwa kuPalamende eshingisa uNkwali wePalamende, panupamwempela nezwisoko zwilonika zwilokwala zokusokutikana yoko akale nechikumbilo chokuti Mulawu uchinofhulwa yoyo ulingisiswe bupya.

(7) Yopo Mulawu uchinofhulwa paunobilizwa muPalamende kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (6)(b), uNdebelekeli unokwelela, pesina kujilwa, kushoba unshangano weGota lyabaMilili muPalamende linokwelela—

(a) kulingisisa Mulawu uchinofhulwa litukobona kuti zwabebuza kusogutikana koNtungamili zwalulamiswa;

(b) kuvuma Mulawu uchinofhulwa, peli kana pesina inshandulo, nabanhu chikamu chababili mubatatu kana kupina pakati pemhingu jose jeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende; akale muchimo chipi zwacho pakati pezwibili yezwi uNdebelekeli unokwelela kuta kuti iBhili liswinchwe kunTungamili weNyika pesina kujilwa kuti elitambule zwipo pomulawu nokulisainaakale etukupa chizibiso kubanji chezhuba iBhili palakasengwa kunTungamili weNyika.

(8) Kana Mulawu uchinofhulwa watulwa kuNtungamili weNyika kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (7) lyatambula zwizhele zwabebuza kusogutikana koNtunagmili weNyika, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kulitambula zwipo pomulawu akale elisaine mukati mwamazhuba makumi mabili nelimwempela akale etukota kuti lishambajwe muGazeti pesina kujilwa, koga kana uNtungamili echilinokusokutikana noMulawu uchinofhulwa yoyo unokweleala, mukati mwonshaji yoyo—

(a)

kutambula Mulawu uchinofhulwa zwipo pomulawu akale etukousaina, zwisinani nokusogutikana kukwe;

(b) kuswincha Mulawu uchinofhulwa kuGota lyezweMilawu lishaka kululamiswa pokutambulika kwMulawu uchinofhulwa yoyo muBumbilo lyeMilawu.

(9) Kana mushule mwokutambula Mulawu uchinofhulwa somwakulotalwa muchibabi chechikamu (8) Igota lyezweMilawu likazibisa kuti Mulawu uchinofhulwa yoyo unovumilana neBumbilo lyeMilawu, uNtungamili unokwelela kuuntambula zwipo pamulawu akale etukousaina pakalepo akale etukota kuti ushambajwe muGazeti pesina kujilwa.

(10) Kana Mulawu uchinofhulwa watulwa kuNtungamili weNyika kuti euntambile zwipo pamulawu akale nokuusaina akale usapelekejwa nechitupa chinodiwa nechisungo chipi zwacho cheBumbilo lyeMilawu, uNtungamili weNyika aatokwela kuuntambula zwipo pamulawu kana kuunsaina kuswikila chitupa chili chezha koga unokwelela kuta kuti uNkwali wePalamende ezibiswe, pakalepo akale nenzila ilokwalwa, kuti chitupa achijakezha noMulawu uchinofhulwa. 132 Kutanga kushinga koMulawu wePalamende Mulawu wePalamende unotanga kushinga pokutanga kwezhuba pawashambajwa muGazeti, kana pokutanga kwezhuba lingabe likadomwa muMulawu kana imwe isumo yomulawu. 133 Kukwaliswa koMulawu wePalamende

(1) Kana uNtungamili atambula zwipo pomulawu nokusaina Mulawu wePalamende, uNkwali wePalamende unokwelela kuswincha imwe iwhalo imbuya yawo, inotondeja buchokomende bwayo nemhande yoNtungamili wenyika nemhande yenyika yeZimbabwe, kuti ikwaliswe muhofisi yoNkwali weGota lyopezhulu, akale iwhalo yeyo busumikiji buzhele bwezwisungo zwomuMulawu yoyu kuzhe kokuti Mulawu wabwilululwa pasi pomulawu wePalamende walebwa muchibabi chechikamu

(2).

(2) Mulawu wePalamende ungavumila kuti mulawu wabumbwa nePalamende, kana chimwe chibabi chawo, ushambajwe uli wabwilululwa akale ungavumila kuti—

(a)

pokushambajwa, ibwilululo ndiyo iwhalo yechokomende yemilawu imo mukati;

(b) imwe iwhalo yebwilululo inokwelela kuswinchwa kuhofisi yonkwali weGota lyopezhulu; akale

(c)

2013

iwhalo yaswinchwa kuNkwali weGota lyopezhulu busumikiji bulopelela bwezwisungo zwemilawu yabumbwa nePalamende zwilomumwa.

(3) Kutambulika koMulawu wePalamende kana ibwilululo yomulawu wabumbwa nepalamende akujilosendama pokukwaliswa kana kuswinchwa kwawo pasi pechibabi yechi. 134 Milawu ipo pasi peMilawu yePalamende IPalamende, muMulawu wePalamende, ungapa masimba okubumba milawu inokwendelana akale nechinangwa chilosumwa muMulawu yoyo, koga—

(a)

masimba makulugulu okubumba milawu aatokwelela kupiwa imwe imhingu;

(b) milawu ipo pasi pemilawu yepalamende aitokwelela kukuvuna kana kushomopaja ilulamilo ipi zwayo kana lusungunuko lupo pasi pakuBhaunulwa kweLulamilo;

(c)

milawu ipo pasi pemilawu yepalamende inokwelela kukwendelana neMilawu yePalamende yaili pasi payo;

(d) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kusuma pachena kujibilijwa kwamasimba, chimo chemilawu ingabumbwa, nezwilokosha nezwisungo zwingashinga pamulawu upo pasi pemilawu;

(e)

(f)

milawu ipo pasi peMilawu yePalamende aitoshinga kuzhe kwokuti yashambajwa muGazeti; akale milawu ipo pasi peMilawu yePalamende inokwelela kutulwa mbeli kweGota lyabaMilili kutobelwa milawu yokushinga lwePalamende akale iswinchwe kuKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu kuti utokolojwe. IMHINGU 7 Unkwendeselo Wonshingo MuPalamende

135 UNtungamili wePalamende

(1) UNdebelekeli ndiye untungamili wePalamende koga unokwelela kuta unshingo uwe etobela milawu yokushinga kwePalamende.

(2) Kutobelwa milawu yokushinga kwePalamende, uNtungamili weGota lyeSeneti ndiye uNtobeli wontungamili wePalamende akale ndiye unobhata chibhula chintungamili pose kana uNdebelekeli esinotubula kuta alo pezhulu pechisoko chipi zwacho. 136 Banhu banotungamila muPalamende

(1) Unhu unotungamila ponshangano upi zwawo weSeneti unokwelela kuba—

(a)

uNtungamili weSeneti kana kuti, kana uNtungamili esipo, uNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti; kana

(b) kana uNtungamili weSeneti noNtobeli besipo, Seneta washalilwa chinangwa yecho neSeneti, koga Seneta yoyo aatokwelela kuba iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota.

(2) Unhu unotungamila ponshangano upi zwawo weGota lyabaMilili unokwelela kuba—

(a)

uNdebelekeli kana kuti, kana esipo, uNtobeli woNdebelekeli; kana

(b) kana uNdebelekeli noNtobeli woNdebelekeli besipo, iMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili yashalilwa chinangwa yecho neGota lyabaMilili, koga iMhingu yeyo aitokwelela kubeli iGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota.

(3) UNdebelekeli kana kuti, kana esipo, uNtungamili weSeneti unokwelela kutungamila unshangano unobhatanisa iGota lyabaMilili muPalamende neGota lyeSeneti panupamwempela. 137 Kuwanda kwabanhu banodikana kuti unshingo utiwe muPalamende

Iseneti neGota lyabaMilili zwinokwelela kusuma muMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende chilabo cheMhingu jinokwelela kubelipo kuti unshingo utiwe. 138 Kushala nelulamilo yokuwhikwa muPalamende

(1) Kuzhe payopo iBumbilo yeli palinovumila kuti kutiwe neimwe inzila—

(a)

mibhuzo yose inoshaka kuti kutolwe zwisungo muneipi iNgumba yePalamende ipi zwayo inopasanulwa nokushala kwamavoti manji eMhingu jeNgumba yakaleyo jipo akale jinovumilwa kushala;

(b) unhu umutungamila iNgumba yePalamende ipi zwayo aana ibhazu lyaanoshala kana eliMungumba yePalamende;

(c)

kana mavoti omuNgumba yePalamende ipi zwayo ekakobwa zwilokwelana, indangalilo yeyo yabeshalwa yazundwa;

(2) Batobeli baBatungamili banovumilwa kugala nokulebeleka, koga abatoshala, muNgumba jePalamende imbili.

(3) Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota bananelulamo yokugala nokulebeleka muNgumba imbili koga abana ilulamilo yokushala muNgumba yabasili iMhingu.

(4) IMhingu yeKomithi yePalamende yeMilawu neimwe ipi zwayo ikomithi inoshanganisila imhingu jengumba imbili bananelulamilo yokugala nokulebeleka muneipi zwayo iNgumba yePalamende pakati pembili nechinangwa chokusungula kana kukwejisa isumo yeKomiti yeyo imo muNgumba, koga abana ilulamilo yokushala muNgumba yabasili imhingu.

(5) Yopo kana iMhingu yePalamende, kuzhe kweGulukota kana uNtobeli weGulukota, yasungula Mulawu uchinofhulwa muNgumba mwaili iMhingu, akale iNgumba yeyo yavuma Mulawu uchinofhulwa yelo, iMhingu yeyo kana kuti, kana isipo, imwe iMhingu yeNgumba yeyo inanelulamilo yokugala nokulebeleka muneimwe iNgumba nechinangwa chokuta unshingo woMulawu uchinofhulwa yoyo muNgumba yeyo, koga aina ilulamilo yokushala muneimwe iNgumba. 139 Milawu yokushinga kwePalamende

(1) Unkwendeselo womuSeneti nowomuGota lyabaMilili unolulamiswa nezwisungo zwemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende, zwinobumbwa neNgumba yeji imwe neimwe ikazwimilila kana kuti panupamwempela kutobelwa zwilulamiso zwapiwa neKomiti yeMilawu yePalamende.

(2) Milawu yokushinga kwePalamende ingalulamisa—

(a)

kuvumila iBhili;

(c)

inzila, masimba, nozwanopiwa nokutabilijwa neNgumba mwangashingiswe kana kutobelejwa;

(b) kubumba makomiti nokusuma mishingo yawo nokuwapa mishingo;

(d) kubhuza Makulukota naBatobeli baMakulukota bebhuzwa neMhingu jePalamende;

(e)

(f)

inzila yokuzwibhata kweMhingu jePalamende;

kushinga kwelulamilo yabanji yokulilijika kuPalamende; akale

(g) kululamisa nokutiwa kwonshingo nonkwendeselo nenzila ilolongeka muNgumba nopakati peNgumba imbili.

(3) Unkwendeselo nontilo wePalamende namaKomiti ayo, somwakulovumilwa mumilawu yokushinga kwePalamende, zwinokwelela kusungwaja kutiwa kwezwinhu pachena, zwinokwelela kukutaija kubhatikana kwamakapo ose ezwamatongelo enyika amo muPalamende nabanhu zwabo, akale zwinokwelela kubeli nobululami nobubuya.

(4) Ikomiti ipi zwayo yabumbwa kana kuti ipo pasi pemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende inokwelela kutondeja, umbumbilo wePalamende peMhingu jamatongelo enyika kana kuti pobunhukaji nobunhulume kana kuti ingumba kunoshinga milawu yePalamende yeyo. 140 Kulebeleka koNtungamili weNyika mupalamende neNdebo yiye kuPalamende

(1) UNtungamili weNyika angalebeleka panaupi unshaji zwawo muNgumba ipi zwayo pakati pembili kana kuti unshangano unoshanganisa iNgumba imbili.

(2) UNtungamili angatumila indebo yiye kuNgumba ipi zwayo pakati peNgumba imbili, akale indebo yakadalo ipi zwayo inokwelela kubalwa noNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika kana iGulukota kana unhu umutungamila ingumba yakalelayo yatambula indebo yeyo.

(3) UNtungamili weNyika angenda kuPalamende kunoshandula mibhuzo penyaya ipi zwayo somwakulotalwa mumilawu yokushinga kwePalamende.

(4) UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela unokwelela kutula indebo munshangano unoshanganisila iNgumba jepalamende imbili pechimo chenyika, akale uNdebelekeli noNtungamili weSeneti banokwelela kuta bulongwa bulokwela bwokuti iPalamende itambule indebo yakadalo. 141 Kuswika nokubhatikana kwabanji muPalamende IPalamende inokwelela—

(a)

kutubulisa banji kuti bebhatikane muzwitiko zwayo zwemilawu nezwimwe zwitiko nezwitiko zwamaKomiti ayo;

(b) kubona kuti makapo ananebabalilo anobhuzwa bo pezhulu paMilawu ichinofhulwa akumupasanulwa nePalamende, kuzhe kokuti kubhuzwa yoku kusakakwelela kana kuti kusinotubulika; akale

(c)

kuta unshingo wayo nenzila ipo pachena akale kuta mishangano yayo, neeyo yamakomti ayo, panotubulwa kubonwa nabanji, koga zwisungo zwinokwelela kutolwa—

(i) kulondota kulongeka mumakwendeselo emishingo yePalamende;

(ii) kululamisa kubona kwabanhu, nokwabatoli bendebo, zwitiko zwePalamende kana maKomiti ayo;

(iii) kujibisa banhu, kushanganisila batoli bendebo, kumishangano yamaKomiti; akale

(iv) kuvumila kushaka zwipo ponhu akale, palokwelela, kulambija unhu kungwina muPalamende kana kubhiswa konhu upi zwe muPalamende;

koga zwisungo yezwi zwinokwelela kubekalulama, zwiwhisisika akale zwitambulika mubugalo munabutongi bwabanji bulosendama pokuta zwinhu zwipo pachena, indulamo, kulemekejwa konhu, kukwelana nolusungunuko. 142 Kutambulika kwezwitiko zwomuPalamende

(1) Kuba nechigalo chisina umilili muSeneti kana muGota lyabaMilili, kana kumiswa unshingo kweMhingu yePalamende, akutojibisa iSeneti kana kuti iGota lyabaMilili kuta unshingo.

(2) Kuti apana unhu usina ilulamilo yokuta alo wagala akashala muSeneti kana kuti muGota lyabaMilili kana kuti wabhatikana muzwitiko zweSeneti kana iGota lyabaMilili azwitoleba kuti zwitiko zwomuPalamende azwitotambulika. IMHINGU 8 Bulefu Bwonshaji Nokuzakunulwa KwePalamende 143 Bulefu bwonshaji nokuzakunulwa kwePalamende

(1) IPalamende iloshalwa kushinga konshaji wamakole mashanu unotanga izhuba uNtungamili weNyika washalwa paanotola ikowhe etukotanga unshingo somwakulotalwa muchikamu 94(1)(a), akale iPalamende inozakunulwa pakati pobusiku bwezhuba lopejeleja kusanu kwatiwa ishalujo ihulwana jinotobela akale jashobwa kutobelwa chikamu 144.

(2) UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuzakunula iPalamende nokuzibisa banhu zwipo pomulawu kana iSeneti neGota lyabaMilili, mimishangano ilosiyana, namavoti zwikamu zwibili mubanhu batatu zwemhingu jose mungumba imwe neimwe zwavumilana kuzakunula.

(3) UNtungamili angazakunula iPalamende eshingisa chizibiso kubanji chipo pomulawu kana iGota lyabaMilili lyalamba zwisinowhisisika kuvuma Mulawu uchinofhulwa lyomulawu wokukobelwa kwemali ilotalwa muchikamu 305.

(4) Chisungo chokuzakunula iPalamende somwakulotalwa muchibabi chechikamu (3), kana imwe iMhingu yalilijika kugota, chingamiswa kumbali mushule mwokulingisiswa neGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu.

(5) Chililijiko chokuti kuzakunulwa kwePalamende kulingisiswe chinokwelela kuswinchwa kuGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu mukati mwamazhuba mashanu namabili mushule mwokutulwa kwechizibiso akale—

(a)

iGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu linokwelela kutula untongo walo mukati mwamazhuba igumi namana mushule mwokutambula chililijiko;

(b) kukamililwa untongo weGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu, chisungo chokuzakunulwa kwePalamende chinobigwa kumbali. 144 Inshalujo ihulwana jinobuzwa nokuzakunulwa kwePalamende

(1) Yopo iPalamende kana isaba iyo yatanga kuba nechivumilano chokuzakunula kutobelwa chikamu 143(2), uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kupa chizibiso chokushoba ishalujo akale nokusuma mazhuba eshalujo ihulwana jichatiwa mukati mwonshaji ulosuma muchikamu 158.

(2) Yopo—

(a)

iPalamende yavumilana kuzakunula kutobelwa chikamu 143(2);

(b) unTungamili weNyika azakunula iPalamende kutobelwa chikamu 143(3);

(c)

2013

uNtungamili azakunula iPalamende zwitobela ishalujo jokushayiwa chibimbo kutobelwa chikamu 109(4); kana

(d) iPalamende lazakunulika kutobelwa inshalujo jokushayiwa chibimbo kutobelwa chikamu 109(5);

uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kupa chizibiso chokushoba nokusuma mazhuba eshalujo ihulwana jichatiwa kusanu kwapinda mazhuba makumi mashanu anamakumi mana mushule mwokuti iPalamende yavumilana kuzakunula kana kuti uNtungamili weNyika eli azakunula iPalamende kana kuti iPalamende lazakunulika, sozwilokwelela.

(3) Mazhuba eshalujo ihulwana jashobwa kutobela chibabi chechikamu (1) kana (2) anokwelela kusumwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mwokulebelekisana neKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo. 145 Unshangano wokutanga wePalamende mushule mwenshalujo ihulwana

(1) Unshangano wokutanga wePalamende mushule mweshalujo unokwelela kutiwa nonshaji nezhuba lyasumwa noNtungamili weNyika, koga izhuba alitokwelela kupinda mazhuba makumi matatu mushule mwokuti uNtungamili weNyika washalwa atanga unshingo kutobelwa zwisungo zwechikamu 94.

(2) Kujine kushalwe uNtungamili weSeneti kana kuti uNdebelekeli, somwakulokwelela, unshangano wokutanga weNgumba yePalamende unokwelela kutungamilwa noNkwali wePalamende. 146 Mishangano nonshaji wokunyetutukwa INgumba imwe neyimwe inozwisumila unshaji nobulefu bwemishangano, kuzhe kwonshangano wokutanga, akale nonshaji wokunyetutukwa, koga—

(a)

unTungamili weNyika angashoba iPalamende panoupi unshaji zwawo kota unshingo uloshalika;

(b) akungapinde mazhuba izana linamakumi mashanu anamakumi matatu pakati pemishangano. 147 Kupela kushinga kwaMilawu ichinofhulwa iSundijilo, zwilijiko neimwe mishingo yePalamende Pakuzakunulwa kwePalamende, zwitiko zwose zwabesanu zwapela noyo unshaji zwinopela, akale Milawu ichinofhulwa yose, isundijilo, zwililijiko neimwe mishingo inopela. IMHINGU 9 Zwimwe Zwilosiyanasiyana Zwinanokuta NePalamende 148 Zwinowanika nezwinotabilijika mugota lyePalamende

(1) UNtungamili weSeneti, uNdebelekeli neMhingu jePalamende bananolusungunuko lwendebo muPalamende nomumaKomiti ose ePalamende akale, kojoba bekwelela kutobeleja milawu nobulongwa bweNgumba yejo, abangabeteswe undandu, kusungwa, kubhatwa mubusungwa kupiwa undandu wokubuza igubaji pezhulu pozo zwabaleba, zwabatondeja kana zwabaswincha kuPalamende kana ipi zwayo iKomiti yePalamende.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende—

(a)

2013

ungasuma isimba linoshingiswa nezwinotabilija imhingu jePalamende nabashingi bayo;

(b) ungapandulula kuzwibhata kunotondeja kusolemekeja iPalamende, zwisinani kuti zwatiwa neMhingu yePalamende kana kuti bamwe banhu; akale

(c)

unopa imvumo yokushandula, kushingiswa uNdebelekeli kana uNtungamili weSeneti, somwakulokwelela, kubanhu balwajiswa zwisilolulama nozwalebwa pezhulu pabo muPalamende;

koga apana Mulawu wakadalo ungavumija iPalamende kana iMhingu jayo kana bashingi bayo kupa chilango chondandu, kuzhe kokulipisa imali, kana kwavunwa isimba linopiwa negota yeli kana kusalemekejwa iPalamende. 149 Ilulamilo yokusenga chililijiko kuPalamende

(1) Unizi wose nonhu unanemvumo yokugala muZimbabwe zwachose unanelulamilo yokuswincha chililijiko chiche kuPalamende kuti ilingisise inyaya ipi zwayo imo mumasimba ayo, kushanganisila kusumwa, kushandulwa nokulambwa kwemilawu;

(2) Inzila yokuswincha zwililijiko kuPalamende, nezwinokwelela kutiwa nePalamende panoswinchwa chililijiko, zwinokwelela kusumwa muMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende. 150 Bugalo bwokutila mishangano yePalamende IPalamende lingashangana kunabumwe bugalo kuzhe kwayopo kunowaja kutilwa mishangano yePalamende, koga yezwi zwingatiwa chete penyaya yezwibabalilo zwabanhu, kutabilijika kana kuti unshingo ukwende zwibuyanana. 151 IKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende

(1) IPalamende inokwelela kudoma iKomiti ichazibwa seKomiti yemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende nechinangwa cho—

(a)

kululamisa kushinga kwePalamende;

(c)

kulingisisa nokutola zwisungo penyaya jose jinanokuta nePalamende; noku

(b) kubumba milawu yokushinga kwePalamende;

(d) kuta imwe mishingo ingapiwa iKomiti neBumbilo yeli kana Milawu yokushinga kwePalamende kana umwe mulawu.

(2) IKomiti yemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende inokwelela kuba noNdebelekeli noNtungamili weSeneti neMhingu jePalamende jinotobela—

(a)

uNtobeli woNdebelekeli;

(c)

iGulukota lyezwemali namamwe makulukota mabili adomwa noNtungamili weNyika;

(b) uNtobeli woNtungamili weSeneti;

(d) uNtungamili wemishingo yaFulumende muNgumba imwe neimwe;

(e)

(f)

uNtungamili wegapo linonyenyeja muNgumba imwe neimwe;

undulamisi unkulwana wamakapo ose alomililwa muNgumba imwe neimwe;

(g) uNtungamili weKansili yenyika yamaShe;

(h) iMhingu imbili jisili Makulukota kana Batobeli bamakulukota, umwempela eliSenata wakadomwa kuKomiti noNtungamili weSeneti akale umwe eli iMhingu yeGota lyabaMilili muPalamende yadomwa noNdebelekeli; akale

(i)

iMhingu inshanu nenhatu jisili Makulukota kana Batobeli baMakulukota, banobashalwa neSeneti kuti bebe mukomithi nabamwe bana bashalwa neGota lyabaMilili muPalamende kuti bebe muKomiti yeyi.

(3) IMhingu jinokwelela kushalwa kana kudomwa kuba muKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende nokukambija mushule mwokutanga kwechikamu chokutanga chePalamende imwe neimwe, akale jinokwelela kushalwa nenzila yokuti iKomiti inotondeja zwilopelela chimo chobumili bwamakapo amatongelo enyika nobunhulume nobunhukaji bweNgumba jePalamende panupamwembela.

(4) IKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende inodomwa kuti ishinge kwonshaji unokwelana nowepalamende imwe neimwe.

(5) IKomiti yemilawu yokushinga kwepalamende inotungamilwa noNdebelekeli kana kuti, kana uNdebelekeli esipo, uNtungamili weSeneti.

(6) Unkwendeselo unootobelwa neKomiti yemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende unokwelela kubhaunulwa muMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende.

(7) Pose pakunoba nechibhula chazhulika muKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende, iMhingu inokwelela kushalwa kana kudomwa, somwakulokwelela, nokukambija kutila kuzhajikija chibhula yecho. 152 IKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu

(1) Nokukambija kunotubulika mushule mwokutanga kwechikamu chokutanga che Palamende imwe neimwe, iKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende inokwelela kudoma iKomiti inoozibwa nokuti iKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu, inoshanganisila iMhingu jePalamende inhatu kana kupinda jisili Makulukota kana Batobeli baMakulukota.

(2) Banji bemhimgu jeKomoti yePalamende yeMilawu banokwelela kuba banotubula kuvumilwa kushinga sabanhu bemilawu kuzhe kokuti kube nabanhu basinokwana bakadalo banotubula kudomwa kuKomiti yeyi.

(3) IKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu inokwelela kupenyengula—

(a)

mabhili ose, kuzhe kweBhili lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu, isanu yawana mavoti okupejeleja muSeneti kana muGota lyabaMilili;

(b) iBhili ipi zwayo yashandulwa mushule mwokutokolojwa neKomiti, iBhili yeyo isanu yawana mavoti ayo okupejeleja muSeneti kana muGota lyabaMilili;

(c)

milawu yose ipo pasi pemilawu yepalamende yatulwa muGazeti;

(d) bwose bulongwa bwoMulawu uchinofhulwa bwaswinchwa kuKomiti noNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika kana iGulukota; ne

(e)

bwose bulongwa bwemilawu ipo pasi pemilawu yePalamende bwaswinchwa kuKomiti nenhabi inamasimba okubumba mulawu yoyo; akale inokwelela kusuma kuPalamende kana kuNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, iGulukota kana inhabi, somwakulokwelela, kuti inoyeya na kuti pangaba nechisungo muBhili, mulawu upo pasi pemilawu yePalamende kana bulongwa bwomulawu, bwokuti bukatobelejwa bungakakabajana nezwisungo zweBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(4) Mushule mwokupenyengula mulawu upi zwawo upo pasi pomulawu wePalamende kana kuti bulongwa bwomulawu upo pasi pomulawu wePalamende,

iKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu inokwelela kusuma kuPalamende kana kuNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika kana kuti inhabi ilokwelela kana iyeya kuti pangaba nechisungo mumulawu upo pasi pemilawu wepalamende chokuti chikatobelwa chinobeli kuzhe kwamasimba oMulawu wePalamende unolulamiasa mulawu yoyo.

(5) Mulawu wePalamende kana Bulongwa bwokushinga kwePalamende bungapa imwe mishingo kuKomiti yePalamende yeMilawu. 153 Imali jinoholwa noNtungamili weSeneti, uNdebelekeli neMhingu jepalamende

(1) Imali jinoholwa noNdebelekeli noNtungamili weSeneti—

(a)

jinokwelela kusumwa muMulawu wePalamende akale jinova kuChikwama chaFulumende;

(b) aitokwelela kudelejwa bechili bakabhata zwibhula zwabo; akale

(c)

banokwelela kundilila mbeli behola mushule mwokuzakunulwa kwePalamende kuswikila bajiba kubhata zwigalo zwabo.

(2) Imali inoholwa neMhingu jePalamende jinokwelela kusumwa muMulawu wePalamende. 154 UNkwali wePalamende nabamwe bashingi

(1) IKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwPalamende, ili yavumilwa neGota lyabaMilili, inokwelela kudoma unhu uchazibwa soNkwali wePalamende, kuti ebe iye unobona nezwokushinga kwePalamende izhuba nezhuba, koga etobela milawu yokushinga kwePalamende akale elulamiswa noNdebelekeli.

(2) UNkwali wePalamende unodomwa kwechikamu chamakole mashanu nelimwempela, akale angadomwa kabili kwechimwe chikamu chakadalo.

(3) UNkwali wePalamende unokwelela kusiya chibhula chiche—

(a)

kana, mushule mwokunyeulilwa neKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende, iMhingu jeGota lyabaMilili jinopinda chikamu chimwempela mubabili bemhingu jose jikavumilan kuti uNkwali ebhiswe;

(b) kana kwakakwelela kuti uNkwali esiye chibhula chiche ngana abeli iMhingu yePalamende; kana

(c) kojoba kwata chini, mushule mwokubhata chibhula kwamakole igumi linamabili.

(4) IKomiti yemilawu yokushinga kwePalamende inokwelela kudoma zwimwe zwishingi zwePalamende zwainobona kuti zwinodikana.

(5) UNkwali wePalamende nabamwe bashing bepalamende—

(a)

banodomwa kuti beshinge betobela zwisungo zwokushinga zwilovumwa neKomiti yeMilawu yokushinga kwePalamende nonshaji nonshaji; akale

(b) banhu banoshingila banji koga abatoba chibabi cheZwishingi zwaFulumende.

CHISIYANISO 7 Inshalujo IMHINGU 1 Bulongwa Nezwitiko Zwenshalujo 155 Zwisungo zwobulongwa bwenshalujo

(1) Inshalujo, ijo jinokwelela kutiwa nonshaji nonshaji, namareferundamu, anotiwa pasi peBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli jinokwelela—

(a) kubeli nolunyalalo, lusungunuko nokukwelana;

(b) banoshala nenzila yokushala musiswa;

(c)

kusendama pokuvumila bose banhu bakulwana kushala nokukwelana kwamavoti; akale

(d) ajitokwelela kuba nenyonga nyonga kana zwimwe zwitiko zwokushala zwisilolulama.

(2) Inyika inokwelela kutola zwose zwisungo zwilokwelela, kushanganisila zwisungo zwemilawu, kubona kuti zwisungo zwilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) akale, kasakasa, inokwelela—

(a)

kuba nechokomende kuti banizi bose banovumilwa kushala, yezwi zwileba banizi bose balovumilwa pasi peShejulu yeChina, banokwalisa kuba bashali;

(b) kuba nechokomende kuti banizi bose banovumilwa kushala mushalujo kana ireferendamu banowana unshaji wokushala, akale inokwelela kubona kuti inotubusa banabukosha kana zwimwe zwinodikana kushala;

(c)

kuba nechokomende kuti makapo ose ezwamatongelo enyika nabamilili bamukwikwija mushalujo kana balobhatikana mureferendamu banotubula kuwana zwishingiso zwose noluzibo lose lulokwelela kutila kuti bebhatikane zwinandyo;

(d) kuwanisa makapo ose ezwamatongelo enyika nabamilili banokwikwijana mushalujo kana bamubhatikana mureferendamu unshaji ulokwelela akale unokwelana kushambaja mujingumba jokushambaja jemichina nejilodindwa, jose jabanji nejilozwimilila; akale

(e)

kuba nechokomende kuti kukakabajana kwenyaya jenshalujo kunolulamiswa nokukambija.

156 Kutiwa kwenshalujo namareferendamu Panotiwa inshalujo jose namareferendamu, iKomishini inolinga nozwenshalujo inokwelela kuba nechokomende kuti—

(a)

inzila ipi zwayo yashingiswa indelu, inobhisa zwinhu somwazwilokwelela, inotubulika kushakisisa panokulunduka kana palolulama, ilosima akale ipo pachena;

(b) zwabhuda munshalujo kana mureferendamu zwinozibiswa nokukambija kunotubulika mushule mwokukonywa kwenshalujo; akale

(c)

bulongwa nezwishingiso zwilokwelela zwinobigwa kutila—

(i) kutabilija zwilopelela inyonganyoga inanokuta nenshalujo nezwimwe zwitiko zwenshalujo zwisilolulama;

(ii) kulondoteka kubuyanana kwezwishingiso zwenshalujo.

157 Milawu yeShalujo

(1) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kusuma inzila jokubhata ishalujo namareferendamu anotiwa pasi peBumbilo yeli, akale kasakasa inyaya jinotobela—

(a)

kuganula matunhu namawadi nonshaji nonshaji kutobelwa chikamu 161;

(c)

inzila jokuzwibhata jamakapo ezwamatongelo enyika, bakwikwiji nabamwe banhu banobhatika mushalujo kana mureferendamu;

(b) (b) kukwalisa kwabashali, nezwinodikana kuti unhu ekwalise pewhalo yokukwalisa kuvota;

(d) inzila yobumilili bunokwendelana namavoti mushalujo jabanhu kuzwibhula zweSeneti zwimulebwa muchikamu 120(1)(a) nozwibhula zwilolondotelwa bechinhukaji muGota lyabaMilili zwilolebwa muchikamu 124(1)(b), nenzila yokuzhajikija mabanga muzwibhula yezwi, akale mabanga yayo anokwelela kuzhajikijwa nonhu—

(i) (i) unova kugapo lyamatongelo enyika imwempela noyo wakabekabhata chibhula yecho mushule;

(ii) wechinhukaji unobwilizwa nowechinhukaji kuti wechinhulume unobwilizwa wechinhulume.

(e)

(f)

kushalwa kwabamilili babanhu baabeli pechibhulao;

untilo wenshalujo jamakansili omumatunhu nomumaguta nomunalaunda;

(g) kusenga chililijiko pezhulu pezwabhuda munshalujo.

(2) Inzila yobumilili bunokwendelana namavoti ilosumwa zwikwendelana nechibabi (1)(d) inokwelela kuba nechokomende kuti panobumilili bulokwelana bwabechnhukaji mukati mwamaSeneta zwilolebwa muchikamu 120(1) (a).

(3) Mulawu weShalujo unolwelela kuvumila kudomwa kwabagunduji mushalujo jipi zwajo kuti kutikane pechili namazhuba igumi linamana kana kupinda mushule mwokushambajwa kwechizibiso chokushoba ishalujo yejo. Ishalujo jinokwelela kutiwa mushule mwamazhuba makumi matatu kana kupinda mushule mwokudomwa kwabagunduji.

(4) Apana inshandulo ingatiwa poMulawu weShalujo, kana mulawu wakabumbwa pasi poMulawu weShalujo, kuzhe kwokuti iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo yabhuzwa akale indangalilo jatiwa neKomoshini jalingisiswa zwilokwelela.

(5) Mushule mwokuti inshalujo jashobwa, apana inshanduko poMulawu weNshalujo kana umwe mulawu ulonangana nenshalujo unooshinga kukalingwa inshalujo yejo. IMHINGU 2 Unshaji Wokutiwa Kwenshalujo 158 Unshaji wokutiwa kwenshalujo

(1) Ishalujo ihulwana jinokwelela kutiwa nenzila yokuti jinotikana kusanu kwapinda—

(a)

mazhuba makumi matatu unshaji wokupela kwamakole mashanu wadomwa muchikamu 241 usanu wapela;

(b) kana iPalamende yavumilana kuzwizakunula kutobelwa chikamu 143(2), mazhuba makumi mashanu anamakumi mana mushule mwokuta chivumilano chakadalo; kana

(c)

kana iPalamende yazakunulwa kutobelwa chikamu 109(4) kana (5) mushule mweShalujo yokushayiwa chibimbo, mazhuba makumi mashanu anamakumi mana mushule mwokuzakunula.

(2) Ishalujo ihulwana jamakansili jinokwelela kutiwa panupamwempela neshalujo joNtungamili wenyika nejepalamende.

(3) Kushala mushalujo jokuzhajikija zwibhula zwomuPalamende kana makansili kunokwelela kutiwa mukati mwamazhuba makumi mashanu namakumi mana mushule mwokuti zwibhula yezwo zwazhuluka kuzhe kokuti zwibhula yozo zwazhulika mukati mwemyeji mishanu nemina kusanu kwatiwa ishalujo ihulwana, akale kana zwadalo zwibhula yezwo zwinogala zwisinq bqnhu kuswika ishalujo jatiwa. 159 Kuzhajikijwa kwezwibhula zwenshalujo Pose pakunoba nechigalo chazhulika muhofisi inogwinwa nokushalwa akale ilobumbwa kutobelwa iBumbilo yeli, kuzhe kwehofisi inoba inolingwa nechikamu 158, inhabi ilopiwa masimba okubambanyila ishalujo jehofisi yeyo jinokwelela kuta kuti ishalujo jitiwe mukati mwamazhuba makumi mashanu anamakumi mana kuzhajikija chigalo yecho. IMHINGU 3 Kupambulanywa Kwemiganu Yenshalujo 160 Kuwanda kwemiganu inoshingiswa peshalujo namawadi

(1) Nechinangwa chokushala Bamilili bePalamende, ,iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kuganula inyika mumiganu yeshalujo mazana mabili anegumi.

(2) Nechinangwa cheshalujo jamakansili, iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kuganula makansili kuti ebe mawadi zwikwendelana nemhingu jinooshalwa kuti jibe mumakansili alokwelela 161 Kupambulanywa kwemiganu yenshalujo

(1) Kamwempela mumakole igumi, pezhuba kana kuti mukati mwonshaji wabigwa neKomishini kutila kuti ubeipo mushule unotobela kubalwa kwabanhu munyika, iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kupambulanya miganu yeshalujo inopambulanya inyika yeZimbabwe.

(2) Kana kupambulanywa kwemiganu yeshalujo kukapela kwasala myeji mishoma panemishanu nomwempela kusanu kwasika izhuba lyokushala mushalujo ihulwana, miganu yapambulanywa aitoshinga mushalujo yejo, koga miganu yakabelipo kusanu kwatanga kupambulanya yoku ndiyo inoshinga.

(3) Miganu yeshalujo inokwelela kuba nenzila yokuti, ponshaji wokupambulanya buwandi bwab abashali balokwalisa mubugalo bozweshalujo imwe neimwe bunokwelana nenzila inotubulika.

(4) Miganu yamawadi inokwelela kuba nenzila yokuti, ponshaji wokupambulanywa buwandi bwabanhu balokwalisa muwadi imwe neyimwe yekansili bunokwelana nenzila inotubulika.

(5) Pokupambulanya—

(a)

miganu yamawadi, iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, inokwelela kuba nechokomende kuti apana iwadi inopambulanywa pakati pamakansili mabili kana kupinda;

(b) miganu yobugalo bwozweshalujo, iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kuba nechokomende kuti apana iwadi ilopambulanywa pakati pemiganu yeshalujo mibili kana kupinda.

(6) Mukupambulanya iZimbabwe mumawadi nemiganu inoshingiswa penshalujo, iKomishini inolinga nozwenshalujo inokwelela, mubugalo bupi zwabo, kulingisisa zwilokwelela—

(a)

umbumbilo wobugalo bwadeni;

(c)

inzila banhu yabagele nayo munyika;

(e)

mukupambulanya miganu mushule mwokupambulanywa kokutanga kwakabatiwa, miganu yenshalujo ipo; akale

(b) inzila jokulebelekisana jimo mubugalo;

(d) bugalo bupi zwabo bwabanhu banovota;

(f)

buwandi bwabagali;

akale beshingise zwinhu yezwi. IKomishini ingasiyana nokuti kunodikana kuti miganu inoshingiswa peshalujo namawadi ose anokwelela kuba nobuwandi bwabanhu bunokwelana, koga apana unganu weshalujo kana iwadi wakadalo ungaba nabashalibalokwalisa banji kana bashoma nechikamu chinopinda makumi mabili muzana kana kukwelaniswa neimwe miganu inoshingiswa peshalujo kana mawadi.

(7) Mushule mwokupambulanya mawadi nemiganu inoshingiswa peshalujo, iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kuswincha kuNtungamili weNyika lukwalo lokutanga lulomuma—

(a)

undungwe wamawadi nemiganu inoshingiswa peshalujo, namazina apiwa imwe neimwe akale nokupandulula miganu yayo;

(b) imepu kana mamepu anotondeja mawadi nemiganu inoshingiswa penshalujo; akale

(c)

lumwe luzibo iKomishini lwainobona lukakwelela;

akale uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuti lukwalo lokutanga yolu lunotulwa mbeli kwePalamende mukati mwamazhuba mashanu namabili.

(8) Mukati mwamazhuba igumi namana mushule mwokuti lukwalo lokutanga lwatulwa mbeli kwePalamende—

(a)

uNtungamili weNyika angabwiliza lukwalo yolo kuKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo kuti ilulingisise akale imwe inyaya ipi zwayo;
