(b) imwe yeNgumba ingavumilana kuti lukwalo lubwilizwe kuKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, kuti ilulingisise akale imwe inyaya ipi zwayo, akale kana zwadalo uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kubwiliza lukwalo kuKomishini kutila kuti kulingisiswa yoku kutiwe.

(9) Yopo lukwalo lwokutanga lwabwilizwa kuKomishini kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (8). IKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kulingisisa inyaya inyaya yadeni, koga zwichalebwa neKomishini ndizo zopejeleja.

(10) Nokukambija kunotubulika mushule mwokutobeleja chibabi chechikamu

(7) na (9), iKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo inokwelela kuswincha lukwalo lwokuganula lwokupecheleja kuNtungamili weNyika.

(11) Mushule mwamazhuba igumi namana mwokutambula lukwalo lwokupejeleja lweKomishini inolinga nozweshalujo, uNtungamili wenyika unokwelela kushambaja chizibiso muGazeti chibanda mazina nemiganu yamawadi nemiganu yeshalujo somwazwapejeleja kutalwa neKomishini.

(12) Kana peli nonsiyano pakati ponganu wewadi kana unganu wobugalo bozwenshalujo ipi zwayo ulopandululwa nonganu ulotalwa pemepu kana pamamepu akabambanyilwa neKomishini, kunotobelejwa zwilopandululwa.

CHISIYANISO 8 Ijudishiyali Namakota IMHINGU 1 Undonganyilo Wamakota 162 Masimba eJudishuyali anova kubanhu beZimbabwe akale alopiwa makota, anoti—

(a)

iGota lyezweBumbilo lyeMilawu;

(c)

iGota lyopezhulu;

(e)

iGota lyezwobutungamili;

(b) iGota lyopezhuluzhulu;

(d) iGota lyezokushinga;

(f)

makota opasi;

(g) makota echibanhu;

(h) namwe makota anobumbwa noMulawu kana kuti pasi poMulawu wePalamende. 163 IJudishiyali

(1) IJudishiyali yeZimbabwe inobumbwa—

(a)

uNtongi uNkulugulu, uNtobeli woNtongi uNkulugulu nabamwe batongi beGota lyeBumbilo lyemilawu;

(b) Babetekesi beGota lyopezhuluzhulu;

(c)

uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyoPezhulu nabamwe babetekesi begota yelo;

(d) uMbetekesi unkulugulu beGota lyezokushinga nabamwe babetekesi begota yelo;

(e)

(f)

uMbetekesi unkulugulu beGota lyezwobutungamili nabamwe batbetekesi begota yeli;

nabanhu banotungamila unkwendeselo kumakota amamajisitilati, makota echibanhu kana api zwawo makota alobumbwa pasi poMulawu wePalamende.

(2) UNtongi uNkulugulu ndiye unsholo wejudishiyali akale ndiye unobona unkwendeselo wemishingo yeGota lyeMilawu neGota lyopezhuluzhulu.

(3) uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyopezhulu ndiye unobona unkwendeselo wonshingo mugota yelo.

(4) uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyezokushinga ndiye unobona unkwendeselo wonshingo mugota yelo.

(5) uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyezwobutongi ndiye unobona unkwendeselo wonshingo mugota yelo. 164 Kuzwimilila kwejudishiyali

(1) Makota anozwimilila akale anolulamiswa chete neBumbilo yeli nomilawu, zwaanokwelela kushingisa pesina kutola ibhazu, nokukambija pesina lutyo, kana kutondejela kulelekela kunelimwe ibhazu kana kujongwa munyaya jawo.

(2) Kuzwimilila, kusotola ibhazu nokuta unshingo zwinandyo kwamakota ndizwo zwikulugulu mukutobelejwa kwemilawu nobutongi bwabanji, nokudalo

(a)

iNyika kana imwe inhabi yafulumende ipi zwayo kana unhu upi zwe aangajongane nokushinga kwamakota;

(b) iNyika, ishingisa zwisungo zwemilawu nezwime bo, inokwelela kubhancha nokutabilija makota kutila kuti kubanechokomende kuti alozwimilila, aatotola ibhazu, anolemekejeka, anoswikika nabanhu akale anoshinga zwinandyo akale kuba nechokomende kuti anotobeleja zwisungo zwilotulwa muchikamu 165.

(3) Untongo kana chisungo chegota chinosunga iNyika nabanhu bose nenhabi jose jafulumende jinobhatwa nontongo kana chisungo yecho, akale zwinokwelela kutobeleja zwisungo yezwo.

(4) Apana chinhu muchikamu yechi chinokwelela kubonwa sokuti chinotabilija Mulawu wePalamende kupa mishingo kuzhe kweyokutonga kumhingu yejudishiyali, koga kuta mishingo yeyo akutokwelela kujonga kuzwimilila kwabashingi bejudishiyali bakalabo mukuta mishingo yabo yejudishiyali akale akutojonga kuzwimilila kwejudishiyali zwayo. 165 Zwisungo zwinolulamisa ijudishiyali

(1) Mukushingisa masimba ejudishiyali, imhingu jejudishiyali jinokwelela kutungamilwa nezwisungo zwinotobela—

(a)

indulamo inokwelela kutiwa kunhu wose, zwisinani nechimo;

(c)

mishingo yamakota makulugulu mukulondota ilulamilo nolusungunuko lwabanhu nokutobelwa komulawu.

(b) indulamo aitokwelela kujilwa, nokudalo imhingu jejudishiyali jinokwelela kuta mishingo yajo yejudishiyali zwinandyo akale nokukambija kunoboneka;

(2) Imhingu jejudishiyali, umwe nomwe kana kuti panupamwempela, jinokwelela kulemekeja nokubhata zwinobubuya mishingo yabo yejudishiyali sechibimbo chabapiwa nabanji akale banokwelela kubabalila kusungwaja kuzwimilila kwawo kutila kulondota chibimbo chabanji mubulongwa bwejudishiyali.

(3) Mukuta zwisungo zwejudishiyali, imhingu yejudishiyali inokwelela kuita ikasungunuka akale pesina kujongwa kusilolulama.

(4) Imhingu yejudishiyali ajitokwelela—

(a) kuta zwamatongelo enyika;

(b) kubhata mahofisi kana kuba imhingu jegapo lipi zwalyo lezwamatongelo enyika;

(c) kushaka imali kana kubhisa imali yokupa lipi igapo zwalyo lyezwamatongelo enyika;

(d) kunda kugubungano lyezwamatongelo enyika.

(5) Imhingu jejudishiyali ajitokwelela kushaka kana kutambula chipo, imali kana chuma chinokobanwa mushule mwedilwe, chikweleti kana chimwe chikumbilo chingaba noluboko mukuta unshingo wabo wejudishiyali kana kupa chitondejelo jokuta unshingo wejudishiyali zwisina bululami.

(6) Imhingu jejudishiyali jinokwelela kukoshesa unshingo wajo wejudishiyali kupinda zwimwe zwitiko, akale ajitokwelela kuta zwimwe zwitiko zwingajonga kana kukanganisa mishingo yajo yejudishiyali.

(7) Imhingu jejudishiyali jinokwelela kutola zwisungo zwilokwelela kulondota nokukubusa luzibo lwabo lwonshingo, bumhizha nokuzwibhata kwabo akale kasakasa banokwelela kugala beziba inshanduko mumilawu yenyika neyopasi lyose. 166 IGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu

(1) IGota lyeBumbilo igota lyopezhuluzhulu linolondota makwalo azwose zwitiko akale linobumbwa—

(a)

uNtongi uNkulugulu noNtobeli woNtongi uNkulugulu; akale

(b) babetekesi bamwe bashanu beGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu.

(2) Kana kudikana unhu uchabata unshingo sontongi muGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu konshaji umfupi, uNtongi uNkulugulu angadoma untongi kana untongi wakasiya unshingo kuti ewane ekabhata chibhula muGota lyezweBumbilo lyeMilawu konshaji yoyo.

(3) Inyaya jikombeli kweGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu—

(a)

jilonangana nokuvunwa kwelulamilo jabanhu jilokosha kana lusungunuko lomuChisiyaniso 4, kana kuti jilonangana nokushalwa koNtungamili weNyika kana uNtobeli woNtungamili weNyika, jinokwelela kuwhililwa nababetekesi bose beGota;

(b) kuzhe kwenyaya jimulebwa mundima (a), jinokwelela kuwhililwa nabatongi beGota batatu kana kupinda;

koga Mulawu wePalamende kana weGota ungavumila kuti inyaya jisanu japelela jiwhikwe nontongi umwempela kana batongi banji beGota.

(4) Batongi kana kuti Batongi bakasiya unshingo badomwa kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (2) bangandilila mbeli sabatongi beGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu mushule mwokupela kwonshaji wokudomwa kwabo, nechinangwa chokundilila mbeli nosnhingo wabakatanga bechili bakabhata zwibhula zwobutongi. 167 Masimba eGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu

(1) IGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu—

(a) ndilo igota lyopejeleja penyaya jose jebumbilo lyemilawu, akale untongo wayo penyaya yeji unosunga amwe ose makota;

(b) linotonga inyaya jebumbilo lyemilawu nenyaya jilonangana nomitongo yenyaya jinanokuta nezwebumbilo lyemilawu, kasa kasa kudomwa nokushingiswa kwechikamu 131(8)(b) nendima 9(2) yeShejulu yashanu; akale

(c) linopa untongo wokupejeleja pokuti inyaya yomuBumbilo lyeMilawu kana kuti inyaya inokamujana nontongo penyaya jebumbilo lyemilawu.

(2) Kutobelejwa iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, iGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu chete ndilo loga linga—

(a)

pa maano okutambulika muBumbilo lyeMilawu kwomulawu umuda kubigwa, koga lingata alo kana mulawu wakaleyu uli waswinchwa kuGota yeli kutobelwa zwisungo zweBumbilo yeli;

(b) wha nokutonga kukakabajana kulonangana neshalujo johofisi yoNtungamili wenyika;

(c)

wha nokutonga kukakabajana kwenyaya jokuti unhu angatubula kuvumilwa kana aatovumilwa kubhata ihofisi yoNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika; kana

(d) tonga kuti iPalamende kana kuti uNtungamili wenyika wakonelwa kuzhajikija mishingo yabo yeBumbilo lyeMilawu.

(3) IGota lyeBumbilo linopa untongo wokupejeleja pokuti Mulawu wePalamende kana kuta koNtungamili wenyika kana iPalamende kunotambulika peBumbilo lyeMilawu kana kuti ne, akale linokwelela kusimatija untongo wokusotambulika peBumbilo kwatiwa nelimwe igota untongo yoyo usanu watobelejwa.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende ungavumila kushinga kwamasimba eGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu akale nokudalo ungapa masimba okubumba milawu yegota.

(5) (5) Milawu yeGota lyeBumbilo inokwelela kuvumila unhu, kana zwilimuchibabalilo chendulamo akale eli koja esina imvumo yeGota lyeBumbilo lyeMilawu—

(a)

kubuza inyaya inanokuta nebumbilo kuGota lyeBumbilo esatanga apinda kunelimwe igota;

(b) kulilijika kuGota lyeBumbili eva kunelimwe gota akale esatanga apinda kunelimwe;

(c)

kuma mugota sonkwaina wegota.

168 Igota lyopezhuluzhulu

(1) IGota lyopezhuluzhulu igota lyopezhulu linolondota makwalo azwose zwitiko akale linobumbwa—

(a)

uNtongi unkulugulu noNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu ;

(c)

bamwe babetekesi bapamhijwa badomwa pasi pezwisungo zwimo muchibabi chechikamu (2).

(b) Babetekesi basili bashoma kupinda babili beGota lyoPezhuluzhulu;

(2) Kana unshingo wababetekesi bokupamijila udikana kuGota lyopezhuluzhulu konshaji umfupi, uNtongi uNkulugulu angadoma untongi weGota lyoPezhulu, kana untongi wakasiya unshingo kuti ewane ekabhata sontongi weGota lyoPezhuluzhulu konshaji wakalewo.

(3) Batongi kana kuti batongi bakasiya unshingobadomwa kuti bewane bakabhata unshingo pasi pechibabi chechikamu (2) bangandilila mbeli beshinga sabatongi beGota lyopezhuluzhulu mushule mwokupela konshaji wokudomwa kwabo, nechinangwa chokuta unshingo upo mbeli kwabo wakatanga bechili bakabhata zwibhula. 169 Unganu wamasimba eGota lyopezhuluzhulu

(1) IGota lyopezhuluzhulu ndilyo lyopejeleja kunosengwa zwililijiko ,kuzhe kwenyaya chinolingwa neGota Linolinga nozweBumbilo.

(2) Zwinda nezwimo muchibabi chechikamu (1), Mulawu wePalamende ungapa masimba kuGota lyopeZhuluzhulu.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende ungapa isimba kuGota lyopeZhuluzhulu kubumba milawu yegota.

(4) Milawu yegota ingapa masimba kuNkwali weGota lyopeZhuluzhulu, api zwawo masimba munyaya jemilandu isina bugebenga—

(a)

kubuja zwokuta munyaya jisina zwililijiko, kuzhe kwenyaya jinanechokuta nokulondotwa nokulelwa kwabana;

(b) kubiga inyaya jimwe jinosomuka mukati mwejimwe inyaya, kushanganganisila kukwalila unkwala wokutobeleja, asi jisili inyaya jinolundula lusungunuko lwani azwe;

asi koga mulawu ulokwelela kupa yoyo unhu azwe ulokubajwa nechisungo chonkwali imvumo yokulingululwa nontongi weGota lyopeZhuluzhulu, lingasumikija, kukolola kana kubiga kumbali kana kupa chimwe chisungo sokuyeya kukwe. 170 Igota lyopezhulu

IGota lyopezhulu ndilyo igulwana pamakota ose pezhulu pezwinokwalwa akale linoshanganisila—

(a)

uNtongi unkulugulu, uNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu, noMbetekesi unkulugulu, weGota lyopezhulu; akale

(b) kana bapi zwabo babetekesi beGota lyopezhulu banobagajwa unshaji nonshaji. 171 Unganu weGota lyopezhulu

(1) IGota lyopezhulu—

(a) linamasimba ose pezhulu pemilandu isina bugebenga, nemilandu inabugebenga, munyika yose yeZimbabwe;

(b) linamasimba okulinga unshingilo wamakota opasi naamwe makota aipo pasi akale anolingulula zwisungo zwinobatulwa;

(c) lingapa luyeyo pezhulu penyaya jinanechokuta nozwebumbilo kuzhe kwenyaya chinolingululwa neGota lyeBumbilo chete; akale

(d) lina isimba lyokushandula untongo unobatulwa namwe makota sezwazwinolingililwa kutiwa noMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende ungapa masimba okulingulula unganu weGota lyopezhulu, nokudalo lingapa masimba kupa untongo wegota.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende ungabiga kuti iGota lyopezhulu lipambulanywe muzwikamu zwilonangana, koga chose chikamu yechi chilokwelela kutobeleja masimba eGota lyopezhulu, pezhulu penyaya jinobuzwa kwalili.

(4) Mulawu wegota ungapa masimba kuna uNkwali weGota lyopezhulu munyaya jemilandu isina bugebenga—

(a)

kukolola inyaya jili jinonyenyejana, kuzhe kwokulolala inyaya jinanechokuta namamililo kana kulelwa kana kulingwa kwabana;

(b) kupa untongo wokutanga penyaya muna imwe inyaya kushanganisila kupa chitondejelo/kutondeka, kuzhe kwenyaya jinanechokuta nolusungunuko lwonhu;

koga milawu ilokwelela kupa yoyo unhu watongwa pezhulu pechitondeko kana chisungo chatulwa wegota, ilulamilo yokuti untongo yoyo ulingululwe nombetekesi weGota lyopezhulu, yoyo angapa kusimatija, kukolola kana kusendeka kumbali kana kupa lumwe luyeyo lwaanoyeya kuti lulokwelela. 172 IGota lyezwemishingo

(1) IGota lyezwemishingo igota lina zwilokwalwa akale lishanganisila—

(a)

umbetekesi unkulwana; akale”.

(b) bamwe babetekesi beGota lyozwemishingo banoba babigwa unshaji nonshaji. [Chibabi chechikamu chashandulwa nechikamu 3 choMulawu wegole lya2017]

(2) IGota lyezwemishingo linamasimba onganu pezhulu penyaya jinanechokuta nozwabashingi nemishingo sokunoba kwabigwa muMulawu wePalamende.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende ungabiga masimba okutonga munganu weGota lyozwemishingo, nokudalo lingapa masimba kupa untongo wegota. 173 IGota linobona nonkwendeselo wonshingo

(1) IGota linolondota iwhalo jemilandu inokwendelana nokukwendeswa kwemishingo linazwilokwalwa akale lishanganisila

(a)

umbetekesi unkulwana; akale

(b) mbetekesi upi azwe weGota linobona nonkwendeselo wonshingo unoba wabjgwa unshaji nonshaji. [Chibabi chechikamu chashandulwa nechikamu 4 choMulawu 10 wegole lyua 2017]

(2) IGota linobona nonkwendeselo wonshingo linamasimba onganu pezhulu penyaya jonkwendeselo wonshingosokulebwa noMulawu wePalamende.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende ungabiga masimba eGota linobona nonkwendeselo wonshingo nokudalo lingapa masimba okuta milawo yegota. 174 Amwe makota namakota anobumbilwa kutonga inyaya jonshaji wakaleyo

(1) Mulawu wePalamende ungabiga kuti kubumbwe, kushanganisila namasimba—

(a)

amakota opasi, kupa untongo pezhulu penyaya jisinabugebenga nemilandu jinabugebenga;

(b) makota anolinga nezwamagalilo, anamasimba anoshanganisila kasakasa pakupiwa untongo wenyaya jamagalilo;

(c)

amwe makota aipo pasi peGota lyopezhulu; akale

(d) makota anobumbilwa kulingisisa jimwe inyaya pakalepo, okulandula, nokusngwinila nokukolola kukakabajana.

(2) Zwiwhililana nchikamu 188(4) cheBumbilo lyeMilawu, indipilo, jimwe imali jokushinga kana zwimwe zwabangapiwe sendipo yokushinga sababetekesi beGota lyezweMishingo neGota linolinga unkwendeselo wemishingo baipo pezwibhula kana kuti balobhata zwibhula zwokubetekesa pezhuba panotanga kushinga mulawu yoyu azwitodelejwa. [Chibabi chechikamu chabigijwa nechikamu 5 choMulawu 10 wegole lya 2017]

175 Masimba aMakota pezhulu penyaya jozwebumbilo

(1) Yopo igota palinobiga untongo pezhulu pokusozhajikijwa kwenyaya jozwebumbilo pamulawu upi zwawo kana chitiko joNtungamili weNyika kana iPalamende, untongo autoshinga kuzhe kwokuti ukasimatijwa neGota lyozweBumbilo.

(2) Igota linopa untongo unotondeja kusosimatijika kwebumbilo lyadomwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) lingamisa kupiwa kwontongo kwonshaji umfupi kunoyo umubetekeswa, kana kusenga mbeli unshaji wokubetekeswa, kukamililwa chisungo cheGota lyozweBumbilo pezhulu pokusimatijika kwomulawu kana yechochitiko.

(3) Unhu ani naani unanechokuta nenyaya angasenga chililijiko mbeli, kana kukwalila kugota lyozweBumbilo esimatija kana kupa unsiyano wontongo upo pezhulu pokusimatijika kwebumbilo wapiwa negota zwikwendelana nezwimo muchibabi chechikamu (1).

(4) Kana kwamuka inyaya inanechokuta nozwebumbilo mukubetekeswa kupi zwako, yoyo umupa untongo mugota yelo kana akumbilwa noyo unanechokuta nenyaya, anga senga inyaya yiye mbeli kuGota lyozweBumbilo kuzhe kwokuti kana elibweni sokoti inyaya yiye ngeyamazatwa.

(5) Mulawu wePalamende kana mulawu wegota ulokwelela kupa unshaji wokuti inyaya isengwe kuGota lyozweBumbilo pontongo unanechokuta nenyaya yokusosimatijika kwebumbilo unoba walebwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) negota limwe zwalyo lisili iGota lyozweBumbilo.

(6) Kana kulingisiswa pezhulu penyaya inanechokuta nebumbilo, mumasimba alyo igota—

(a) lingapa chisungo chokuti mulawu upi zwawo kana chitiko chisinokwendelana neBumbilo lyeMilawu achitosunga, zwindililana nokusokwendelana kwawo;

(b) lingata chisungo chilolulama akale chikwelana, kushanganisila untongo unojibilija unolinga kushule kwonshaji ulotalwa kutila kuti yoyo unamasimba ewane unshaji wokukolola palolunduka. 176 Masimba osiyilwa eGota lyozweBumbilo, iGota lyopezhuluzhulu neGota lyopezhulu IGota lyozweBumbilo, igota lyopezhuluzhulu, neGota lyopezhulu ana masimba osiyilwa kukwabilila nokukwendeleja untilo wabo kukubusa mulawu ulozibikana kana mulawu wamagalilo abo, belingisa zwido zwomene nezwimo muBumbilo lyemilawu IMHINGU 2 Kugajikwa Nokumusilijwa Kwabamilili Bezokubetekeswa Kwemilandu 177 Bumhizha bwababetekesi beGota lyozweBumbilo

(1) Unhu unotubula kugajikwa sombetekesi weGota lyozweBumbilo kana eli chizwalwa chomu Zimbabwe, elinamakole angaswika kumakumi mana okuzwalwa akale elinoluzibo luzhele lweBumbilo lyemilawu, phezhulu pezwi ulokwelela kuba neimwe yendaijo jinotobela—

(a)

kuba wakabeli umbetekesi wegota ebetekesa milandu yenyaya jisinabugebenga nejobugandanga munyika inamulawu ulozibikana weRoma nechiDachi kana Chikuwa, akale Chikuwa chili lulimi lulolingilwa kushingiswa kasa; kana

(b) kubeli kana kuba wakabeli nendayijo yokushinga somilili womulawu kwamakole angaba igumi lina mabili—

(i) eli mu Zimbabwe; kana

(ii) munyika inamulawu ulozibilika weRoma nechiDachi neChikuwa, akale Chikuwa chili ilo lulimi lulolingilwa kushingiswa akale panshaji wakalewo elinendayijo yokuta unshingo.

(2) Kuti unhu edomwe kuba umbetekesi weGota linolinga nozweBumbilo ulokwelela kuba eli unsimu akale ekalulamila kutola chibhula chokuba untongi. 178 Bumhizha bwombetekesi weGota lypoeZhuluzhulu

(1) Unhu unotubula kugajikwa sombetekesi weGota lyopeZhuluzhulu kana eli chizwalwa chomu Zimbabwe akale elinamakole angaswika makumi mana,pezhulu pezwi—

(a)

2013

kubeli kana kuba wakabeli umbetekesi wegota ebetesa milandu yenyaya jisina bugebenga nejobugandanga munyika inamulawu ulozibikana weRoma nechiDachi neChikuwa, akale Chikuwa chili lulimi lulolingilwa kushingiswa kasa; kana

(b) kuba wakabeli nendayijo yokushinga somilili womulawu kwamakole angaba igumi etobelana kana esinotobelana—

(i) eli mu Zimbabwe; kana

(ii) munyika inamulawu ulozibikana we Roma nechiDachi ne Chikuwa, akale Chikuwa chili ilo lulimi lulolingilwa kushingiswa akale panshaji wakalewo elinendayijo yokuta unshingo.

(2) Kuti unhu edomwe kuba untongi weGota lyopezhuluzhulu ulokwelela kuba eli unsimu akale ekalulamila kutola chibhula chobubetesi. 179 Bumhizha bwababetekesii bemilandu yeGota lyopezhulu, iGota lyemishingo nawe Gota lyonkwendeselo wemishingo

(1) Unhu unotubula kugajikwa sombetekesi wemilandu weGota lyopeZhulu iGota lyemishingo kana iGota lyonkwendeselo wemishingo angobeli namakole makumi mana okuzwalwa, pezhulu pezwi unokwelela—

(a)

kubeli kana kuba wakabeli umbetekes wemilandu mugota munyaya jisina bugebenga kana munyaya jobugebenga munyika inazwobutongi zwe Roma nechiDachi kana ChiKuwa akale ChiKuwa chili icho luji lunoshingiswa kasa lulokosheswa; kana

(b) kwamakole angaba mashanu anamabili, angaba etobelana kana pe, angabeli nendayijo yokushinga sonshingi wezwemilawu—

(i) mu Zimbabwe;

(ii) munyika inabutongi bwezwemilawu bweRoma nechiDachi neChiKuwa chili icho luji lulokosheswa; kana

(iii) elichizwalwa chomuZimbabwe, munyika inaChiKuwa somulawu ulozibilika akale Chikuwa luli luji lwenyika;

(iv) panshaji wakale yoyo elinobumhizha bwokuta unshingo.

(2) Kudomwa sombetekesi weGota lyopeZhulu, iGota lyemishingo kana iGota linobona nonkwendeselo wemishingo, unhu unokwelela kubekasima akale elinhu ulokwelela kutola chigalo chontongesi. 180 Kudomwa kwababetekesi

(1) UNtongi Unkulugulu, uNtobeli woNtongi uNkulugulu noMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyoPezhulu nabamwe babetekesi bose banodomwa noNtungamili weNyika kutobelwa chikamu yechi.

(2) Untongi unkulugulu, uNtobeli woNtongi uNkulugulu noMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyoPezhulu,banodomwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mwokuwha indangalilalo jeKomishini inobona nezwokushinga kwamakota.

(3) Kana kudomwa kwoNtongi unkulugulu, untobeli woNtongi unkulugulu kana uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyoPezhulu kusinokwendelana nozwasumwa neKomishini yezwokushinga kwamakota kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (2), uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuta kuti iGota lyeSeneti lizibiswe nokukambija kunotubulika: Kuli kuti, kutila kuti zwibe pachena, kwasumwa kuti indangalilo jatolwa noNtungamili weNyika pokudoma yoku ndijokupejeleja.

(4) Pose palokwelela kudomwa uMbetekesi kuzhe kwoNtongi uNkulugulu, uNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu kana uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyoPezhulu, iKomishini inobona nozwamakota inokwelela—

(a)

kushambaja zwibhula yezwi; akale

(c)

kubhuzila mukati mwabanhu mibhuzo yokungwina mishingo kwabanhu badomwa; akale

(b) kukoka unTungamili weNyika nabanhu zwabo kuti bedome banhu bangatola zwibhula yezwo; akale

(d) kubambanyila mazina abanhu batatu bangabhudilila kuta unshingo; akale

(e)

kupa uNtungamili weNyika mamazina ashalwa, akale kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (5), uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kudoma umwe wabanhu bashalujwa kuti etole chibhula yecho.

(5) Kana uNtungamili weNyika eyeya kuti apana unhu pamazina abuzwa kwaali kutobelwa chibabi chechikamu (4)(e) ulokwelela kugala pechibhula, unokwelela kubuja iKomishini inobona nezwemilawu kuti iumpe amwe mazina matatu abanhu banotubula kuvumilwa kutola chibhula, akale uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kudoma unhu umwempela kuti etole chibhula yecho.

(6) UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuta kuti kushambajwe banhu bose banoba bashalwa kuti betole zwibhula kutobelwa chikamu yechi chinobhuda muGazeti.

(7) Zwibhula zwombetekesi unkulwana weGota lyezweMishingo nombetekesi unkulwana weGota linolinga nezwonkwendeselo wemishingo zwinokwelela kugalwa nomwe umbetekesi kana umbetekesi wokupamhijila kana kuti ulowana ekabhata chibhulao chobubetekesi, somwazulokwelela, wegota lyakalelo, akale banodomwa noNtongi unkulugulu mushule mwokuwhijana neKomishini inobona nezwemilawu. [Chikamu chatolelwa bugalo na6 choMulawu 10 wegole lya 2017]

181 Kuta unshingo wokubhatila zwokubetekeswa kwemilandu

(1) Kana chibhula choNtongi unkulugulu chisina unhu kana umbhati wechibhula esinotubula kuta unshingo uNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu unobhatila pechigalo chiche, koga kana zwigalo zwibili yezwi zwisina banhu kana kuti babili bekonelwa kuta unshingo wabo untongi unkulugulu unotobela we Gota linolinga nozweBumbilo unobhatila soNtongi uukulugulu.

(2) Kana ihofesi yo—

(a)

Mbetekesi unkulugulu weGota lyopeZhulu;

(c)

umbetekesi unkulwana weGota linobona nezwonkwendeselo wemishingo:

(b) umbetekesi unkulwana weGota lyezweMishingo; kana

isina unhu kana kuti umbhati wechibhula ekonelwa kuta unshingo wehofesi yeyo, umbetekesi unkulugulu unotobela wegota ilokwelela unobhatila s Mbetekesi unkulugulu. [Chibabi chechikamu chashandulwa nechikamu 7 choMulawu wegole lya 2017]

(3) Kana mishingo yomwe umbetekesi weGota lyopeZhulu, iGota lyemishingo kana weGota linobona nonkwendeselo wemishingo ishakwa kwonshanji unshomanana uNtungamili, ulobhatila sokulebejwa nabegapo lyezwokubeteswa kwemilandu, unogajika umbetekesi uchabhatila chibhula konshaji usinopinda myeji gumi inembili, unshaji unokwelela kumusilijwa kamwempela kwemweji gumi linembili.

(4) Banhu bagajikwa kubhatila zwiva mu chibabi chechikamu (3) banondila mbeli beshinga sabatongi mazhuba abo okugajikwa kushinga sababetekesi apindila, kukushinganywa inyaya jakatulwa mbeli kwabo bechinobhatila.

182 Kugajikwa kwababetekesi nabamwe bezwokubetekeswa Kwemilandu Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kubona nezwokugajikwa kwababetekesi bamakota opasi nabamwe bashingi bezokubeteswa kwemilandu basi babetesi, koga—

(a)

Babetekesi bamakota opasi banokwelela kugajikwa nabegapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu;

(b) bezokubetekeswa kwemilandu basi babetekesi bamakota opasi kana babetesi banokwelela kugajikwa kuli nokuvumilwa nabeKomishini linolinga nezwokutongwa kwemilandu;

(c)

kose kugajikwa yoku kunokwelela kutiwa pachena akale kusina lutyo, kushaluja, kulingila pasi kana bumenemene.

183 Bashingi bezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu besigajikwe pamakota anopinda Limwempela Kuzhe kwokuti muna imwe inzila zwikatulwa muBumbilo lyemilawu yeli, unhu aatokwelela kugajikwa sechishingi chokubetekeswa kwemilandu chamakota anopinda limwempela. 184 Kugajikwa kwabanobona nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu kunokwelela kutondejela bugali Kugajikwa kwabezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu kunokwelela kutondeja nokuzhele unsiyano nokukwelaniswa kwabanhu bechinhulume nabechinhukaji bomu Zimbabwe. 185 Ikowhe jokutola chibhula

(1) Mushule mwokuti uNtongii Unkulugului kana UNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu etange kushinga, unokwelela kuma mbeli koNtungamili weNyika kana washalwa noNtungamili weNyika ete ikowhe lyezwokubeteskewa kwemilandu somwakulomiswa muShejulu yaTatu.

(2) Umbetekesi, usili uNtongi unkulugulu kana uNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu esanu atanga kushinga, unokwelela kuta ikowhe lyezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu mbeli kwoNtongi unkulugulu kana umwe umbetekesi unkulwana unotobela unobelipo.

(3) Milawu yePalamende inoba iyo chinogajikwa babetekesi nabamwe bezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu, basi batongi chinokwelela kupa ikowhe lichatiwa nabo bezokubetekeswa kwemilandu. 186 Bulefu bwonshaji wokushinga wababetekesi

(1) Babetekesi beGota lyozweBumbilo banogajikwa konshaji usinomusilijwa usinopinda makole gumi lina mashanu, koga—

(a)

banokwelela kusiya unshingo makole abo esanu aswika kana baba namakole makumi mashanu anamakumi mabili; akale

(b) mushule mokuswikisa unshaji wabalotalilwa, bangagajikwa sababetekesi beGota lyopeZhuluzhulu kana iGota lyopeZhulu, bezwidila kana bezwitubula zwibhula yezwo.

(2) Babetekesi beGota lyopeZhuluzhulu, beGota lyopeZhulu nabamwe babetekesi banotanga unshingo kuva izhuba lyabanogajikwa pezwibhula kunoswika baba namakole makumi mashanu anamakumi mabili, pabanokwelela kuma mishingo.

(3) Unhu angagajikwa kuba umbetekesi weGota lyopeZhuluzhulu, iGota lyopeZhulu namwebo makota konshaji ulotalwa, koga kana unhu agajikwa, esijimubhatila, unojiba kuba umbetekesi angoba namakole makumi mashanu anamakumi mabili kojoba unshaji wokugajikwa kukwe usanu waswincha.

(4) Kojoba umbetekesi asiya unshingo kana kuti aba namakole makumi mashanu anamabili kana, angobeli umbetekesi weGota lyeBumbilo kana umbetekesi ulotulwa mu chibabi chechikamu (3), aswincha unshaji walotalilwa kushinga, angendila mbeli eshinga sombetekesi etila kushinganya milandu yakabatulwa kwali echili umbetekesi.

(5) Umbetekesi angasiya unshingo kungaba lini nokukwala lukwalo lokuzibisa kuNtungamili weNyika ipinda nokuKomishini inolinga nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu.

(6) Ihofesi yombetekesi aitokwelela kuvalwa panshaji wokushinga kukwe. 187 Kubhiswa kwabatongi pemishingo

(1) Zwingatatisa umbetekesi pemishingo—

(a)

kusotubula kuta mishingo yanokwelela kuta, nokuda kwobulwaji bwendangalilo kana bukosha pombili uwe;

(b) kusotubula kuta unshingo nenzila yomene kulopamululwa;

(c)

kulundula pemishingo kulopamululwa; umbetekesi angatatwe pemishingo zwisimo pechikamu yechi.

(2) Kana uNtungamili weNyika ebona kuti kubhiswa kwoNtongi unkulugulu pechibhula kunoda kupenyengulwa, uNtungamili weNyika unogajika igota linobumbwa kutila kulinga inyaya panshaji wakaleyo.

(3) Kana iKomishini inolinga nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu ilayila uNtungamili weNyika kuti kubhiswa kombetekesi, zwishanganisa noNtongi unkulugulu wezwobululami pechibhula zwinoda kupenyengulwa, uNtungamili weNyika unogajika ibungano linoshakisisa nezwenyaya yadeni.

(4) IBungano linoshakisisa lyagajikwa zwiva muchikamu yechi linokwelela kuba nabanhu bangaba batatu bashalwa noNtungamili weNyika, pabanhu yaba—

(a)

umwempela unokwelela kubeli;

(i) akashinga sombetekesi weGota lyopeZhuluzhulu kana weGota lyopeZhulu mu Zimbabwe; kana

(ii) umbetekesi kana wakabeli umbetekesi wegota ebetekesa inyaya jisina bugebenga nejobugandanga munyika inamulawu ulozibilika weRoma nechiDachi kana Chikuwa, ChiKuwa luli ilo luji lulokosheswa lunoshingiswa kasa;

(b) umwempela unokwelela kubekashalwa pabanoshinga nezwemilawu batatu kana banji bashinga kwamakole mashanu anamabili zwinda mbeli bashalwa negapo, zwiva muMulawu wePalamende, chinomila bamilili bezwemilawo mu Zimbabwe.

(5) Igapo lilonangwa muchibabi chechikamu (4)(b) linokwelela kubambanya mazina alolebwa nezwawo muchibabi chechikamu chakalecho kana akumbilwa noNtungamili weNyika.

(6) UNtungamili weNyika unodoma umwe wabegota linolinga nozwenyaya yonshaji wakaleyo bagajikwa zwiva muchikamu yechi kuba ungalichibhula wegota linolinga inyaya yonshaji wakaleyo.

(7) IBungano lyagajikwa zwiva muchibabi chechikamu (2) kana (3) inokwelela kushakisisa nezwokubhiswa pechibhula kombetekesi, lyata alo linopilija inyaya yalyo kuNtungamili weNyika lyisimatija kuti umbetekesi angabhiswa pechibhula kana pe.

(8) UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuta sokusimatijwa kwebunga linoshakisisa zwiva muchibabi chechikamu (7).

(9) IBunga lyagajikwa zwiva muchikamu yechi lina nelulamilo namasimba sa ama komishina pasi pechisungo chekomisina cholingula [Chisiyaniso 10:07], kana umwe mulawu ungatola bugalo bwechisungo yechi.

(10) Kana inyaya yokubhiswa kwombetekesi pechibhula yasengwa kugota linolinga inyaya yonshaji wakaleyo muchikamu yechi, umbetekesi unobhiswa pechibhula kujine uNtungamili weNyika etobela zwisungo zwegota linolinga inyaya yonshaji wakaleyo kumbwiliza pechibhula kana kumbhisa pechigalo chokubetekesa.

(11) Mulawu chePalamende ungapa isimba kugapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu kana lyeBunga lyezwokushakisisa lyakagajikwa pasi pechikamu yechi kukumbila untongi angaba upi zwe kunolingwa nabezwobutano bubuya, kunabo banozwilinga, kutila kuti bezibe mamilo amafupa awe nendagalilo. 188 Mamilo onshingilo nombhatilo wabanolinga nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu

(1) Babetekesi banokwelela kuwana imali jabo jokushinga, nezwimwe zwose zwibokeso panshaji wose wose ulokwelela kuva kuna banolinga nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu zwisimitijwa noNtungamili wenyika mushule mokulebelekesana neGulukota ulomila indulamo zwivumilwa akale na Minister unolinga nezwemali.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kulinga mamilo namashingilo abashingi bezokubetekeswa kwemilandu basi babetekesi, akale ubone kuti kunyamulwa kwabo pemishingo, kushanduka kowkushingila nokumiswa mishingo, akale kwose kulangwa kwabo kunokwelela kutiwa—

(a)

kusumitijwa negapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu lyeKomishini inolinga nezwokutongwa kwemilandu; akale

(b) kukwelaniswa nokuba pachena akale pesina lutyo nokushaluja.

(3) Indipilo, nezwimwe zwose zwibokeso zwokushinga kwabegapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu ngeinova pemali jinotolwa kumabunga alosiyana siyana.

(4) Indipilo, nezwimwe zwose zwibokeso zwokushinga kwabegapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu aitokwelela kudelejwa beli ponshingo kana kuti bekabhatila pechigalo chilokwelela IMHINGU 3 Igapo Lyezokubetekeswa Kwemilandu 189 Kusungulwa nokumiswa kwegapo lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu

(1) Kuna negapo lyezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu lina nezwinotobela—

(a)

uNtongi Nkulugulu;

(c)

uMbetekesi unkulugulu weGota igulwana lyopezhulu;

(b) uNtobeli woNtongi nkulugulu;

(d) uMbetekesi umwempela washalwa nabamwe batongi be Gota lyeBumbilo, iGota lyopezhuluzhulu, iGota lyopezhulu, iGota lyemishingo neGota linolinga nezwamakwendeselo emishingo;

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

iGweta Igulwana lyaFulumende; uMbetekesi uNkulwana; ungalichibhula weKomishini yabashingi bafulumende; batatu banoshinga nezwomulawu bashinga kwamakole mashanu anamabili bagajwa negapo lipo pasi pechisungo chePalamende;

(i) unzinandayijo kana undayiji nkulugulu wezwo mulawu ulogajwa negapo linomila balayiji banji bezwemilawo kumayunivesithi omuZimbabwe kana, agajikwa noNtungamili wenyika pesina igapolyakadalo;

(j) unhu umwempela washinga muZimbabwe sonhu unolinga unkwendeslo wejimali jafulumende kana unopenyengula kushingiswa kwemali jafulumende kwamakole mashanu anamabili, abigwa negapo lilobumbwa pasi poMulawu wePalamende chinomila banhu bakadalo; akale

(k) unhu umwempela unamakole mashanu anamabili eliponshingo wokukwendeleja unshingilo wabanhu, agajikwa noNtungamili weNyika.

(2) UNtobeli woNtongi unkulugulu unokwendeleja mabunga egapo unoshinga nazwemilawu kana uNtongi unkulugulu esipo, akale kana babili bebunga yelo banoshala umwempela wabo kuti etungamile ibunga yelo.

(3) Banhu banoshinga nezwomulawu badomwa mundima (d), (h), (i), (j) na (k) wechibabi chechikamu (1) banogajikwa kwamakole mashanu elimwempela konshaji mwempela usinomusilijwa. 190 Unshingilo wegota lyezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu

(1) Igota lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu lingazibisa Fulumende penyaya jingaba jipi zwajo jinokwendelana nobutongi kana kukwendelejwa kwobululami, akale Fulumende ulokwelela kuzwitola.

(2) Igota lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu linokwelela kukuja nokukwendeleja lusungunuko nokuzwimila kwobutongi nokukwendeleja bululami muZimbabwe zwanana zwinandyo akale zwilipachena, akale lina masimba ose okuta yezwi.

(3) Igota lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu, livumilwa neGulukota lilomila zwobululami, lingabumba milawu yezwingaba zwipi zwazo zwilotulwa muchikamu yechi.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende ungabiga pemishingo yeKomishini inolinga nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu, pamwempela nokungwina mishingo, kuzwibhata, namashingilo abanhu banoshinga kugota lyezweBumbilo lyemilawo, gota lyopezhuluzhulu, igota lyopezhulu,igota lyemishing, iGota linobona nonkwendeselo wemishingo namwe makota. 191 Kukwelanisa nokuba pachena konkwendeselo wegota lyezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu Igota lyezokubetekeswa kwemilandu linokwelela kuta unshingo walo nenzila inabululami, kukwelanisa akale zwipo pachena. IMHINGU 4 Zwimwe Zwazo 192 Mulawu ulokwelela kukwendelejwa Mulawu ulokwelela kukwendelejwa namakota omuZimbabwe ndiyoyo wakabeshinga panshaji wakaloyo, sokushandulwa kwawo.

193 Kubetekeswa kwobugebenga mumakota Makota anotobela ndiwo chete angashinganya kana kupiwa butongi bwenyaya jobugebenga—

(a)

Igota lyeBumbilo lyemilawu, iGota lyopeZhuluzhulu, igota lyopezhulu negota lyopasi;

(b) Igota kana ibungano linolingisisa inyaya jipo pasi pomulawu wokuzwibhata, kuswikila pakunoshakwa kupa untongo kusimatija kuzwibhata yopo palokwelela. CHISIYANISO 9

Zwisungo Zwokukwendelejwa Nokutungamilwa Kwabanji 194 Milawu ilokosha yokukwendelejwa kwabanji

(1) Kukwendelejwa kwabanji mumapazu ose afulumende, kushanganisila makubungano nabamilili benyika, mapazu anokwendelejwa nafulumende namwe makapo abanji, kunokwelela kukwendelejwa nemilawu inokwelanisa nokusupula wose unhu zwimo muBumbilo lyeMilawu, pamwempela neyi imwe milawo—

(a) bumhizha bunabunhu bwopezhulu bulokwelela kunyamulwa nokugala bulipo;

(b) kushingisa zwanana zwiwanikwa kunokwelela kunyamulwa;

(c)

kukwendelejwa kwabanji kulokwelela kukubuswa nokutambulwa;

(e)

zwido zwabanhu zwinokwelela kutiwa munshaji ulokwelela, akale banhu banokwelela kusungwajwa kuti bewanikwe pakubumbwa kwemilawu;

(d) unshingo unokwelela kutiwa zwanana, zwinabululami, kukwelaniswa akale pesina kushaluja;

(f)

kukwendelejwa kwabanji kunokwelela kutubula kupa inshandulo kuPalamende nokubanhu;

(g) makubungano nabamilili bafulumende pezwikamu zwose banokwelela kubhatana;

(h) kubapachena kunokwelela kukubuswa nokupa zwizibiso panshaji ulokwelela, zwinobhatika akale zwechokomende;

(i)

(j)

kukwendeleja zwishingi zwanana nezwito zwokukubusa nshingo, kunyamulwa kutubula konhu, kunokwelela kusungwajwa; kukwendelejwa kwabanji kunokwelela kuba nobumilili bulokwazabuka bwamapazu alosiyana siyana omuZimbabwe;

(k) kungwina unshingo, kufundila nezwito zwokunyamuka zwinokwelela kulingisa bubuya, kutubula, chinangwa, kusoshaluja, kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabechinhukajana nokutambulwa kwabanabukosha;

akale inyika inokwelela kuba nechisungo, chinoshanganisila chemilawo, kusungwaja milawu nezwisungo yezwi.

(2) Kugajikwa muma hofesi mumapazu ose afulumende, kushanganisa inhabi jafulumende nabamilili namakampani anokwendelejwa nafulumende namwe makampani abanji anokwelela kutiwa kutanga kulingisiswa bubuya.

195 Makampani ezwokutengeselana anolulamiswa nafulumende

(1) Makampani namwe makampani ezwokutengeselana aimo mumaboko kana kuti anolulamiswa naFulumende anokwelela kuti, pezhulu pokutobeleja milawo ilotulwa muchikamu 194(1), eshinge enyamula nokusenga mbeli kutengeselana nokuma mumilawo mibuya yokukwendeleja.

(2) Makampani namwe makampani ezwokutengeselana alonangwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) anokwelela kutondeja kubapachena, kutambulika nenzila jokuwana zwinhu jina kugundujana. 196 Kuzwibhata kwabanoshingila fulumende nemilawu yobutungamili

(1) Masimba alopiwa kuchishingi chabanji kubimbwa nabanhu kunokwelela kukwendelejwa munzila—

(a)

inokwendelana nezwito nezwinangwa zweBumbilo lyeMilawuo yeli;

(c)

inonyamula kubimbwa nabanhu muchibhula chilopiwa chishingi chafulumende.

(b) inotondeja kulemekeja banhu nokuzwipila kubashingila pe kubatonga; akale

(2) Zwishingi zwafulumende zwinokwelela kuzwibhata, pabanhu nomubupenyu bwabo bwamazhuba ose, kutabilija kukakabajana pakati pezwido zwabo nezwabanhu kana zwemishingo yabo, akale besiyane nezwito zwinodeleja mishingo yabo.

(3) Zwishingi zwafulumende zwimo muzwibhula zwobutungamili zwinokwelela kutobeleja miyeyo yobutungamili inotobela—

(a)

kuba nechinangwa nokusoba nolushalulo mukuta zwinhu;

(c)

kubanenshandulo kubanhu panezwatulwa nezwatiwa; akale

(b) kubimbika mukuta mishingo;

(d) kuzwibhata nokuzwipila pakushinga nabanhu.

197 Mashingilo echigalo chamakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende Chisungo chePalamende chingadeleja mashingilo ondulamisi wekampani, kana bakulwana bamakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende namwe makampani ezwokutengeselana namakampani ose enyika kana alolulamiswa nenyika. 198 Masimba okusimisa Chisiyaniso 9 Mulawu wePalamende chinopa chisinikijo chokubhudililisa zwilotulwa muchisiyaniso yechi, zwishanganisila zwisimatijo—

(a)

zwinoda kuti zwishingi zwabanji zwigale zwibhisila pachena zwibiya zwopemishingo;

(b) zwinopa milawu yokuzwibhata ichatobelwa nezwishingi zwabanji;

(c)

zwinotondeja zwimo zwibuya zwokukwendeleja zwinokwelela kutobelwa namakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende namwe makampani ezwokutengeselana namakampani ose enyika kana alolulamiswa nenyika.

(d) zwinovumila kulangwa kwabanhu banoshotoka milawu ilopiwa muchisiyaniso yechi kana imwemilawu yokuzwibhata nezwimo zwilotulwa mundima (b).

CHISIYANISO 10 Kushingila Fulumende 199 Kushingila Fulumende

(1) Kunebunga imwempela lyokushingila Fulumende, linobona nezwokukwendelejwa kwenyika ye Zimbabwe.

(2) Imhingu yenhabi yokushingila Fulumende banhu banoshingila inyika basi—

(a)

begapo lezwokulondotwa kwenyika nabamwe bose begapo lezwokulondotwa kwenyika lingabazhulwa;

(b) batongi, babetekesi nabanhu banotungamila pamakota alogajikwa noMulawu wePalamende;

(c)

makota ekomishini ilogajikwa neBumbilo lyezwemilawu;

(e)

unhu umwe azwe unechigalo kana chibhula chilodomwa, neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli kana Mulawu wePalamende, chisinobumba chikamu chezwishingi zwafulumende.

(d) bebunga lyePalamende; kana

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutula kulongeka, mamilo, kukwendeleja, mulawu, kuzwibhata zwanana akale, somuchikamu (203), unshingilo wezwishingi zwafulumende. 200 Kuzwibhata kweZwishingi zwaFulumende

(1) Zwishingi zwafulumende zwinokwelela kushinga zwitobeleja zwinova mubumbilo lyemilawu nomulawu wenyika.

(2) Akuna chishingi chafulumende chinokwelela kutobela zwisipo pomulawu zwachinowhi chite.

(3) Akuna chishingi chafulumende chinokwelela, kuti pakuta mishingo—

(a)

chishinge chikakabajana nabamwe;

(c)

chikudeluje zwido zwebhazu limwe zwalo lyezwamatongelo enyika zwipo pamulawu; kana

(b) chikutaije kuta kana zwido zwelimwe bhazu lyezwamatongelo enyika;

(d) kuvuna ilulamilo kana kusupuka komwe unhu

(4) Zwishingi zwafulumende azwitokwelela kuba nezwibhula mubhazu lyezwamatongelo enyika lingaba lipi zwalo.

(5) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutola chisimatijo chokuta kuti zwishingi zwafulumende zwisibe nechokuta mumapazu anobona nezwamatongelo enyika. 201 IGulukota linomila Zwishingi zwaFulumende UnTungamili wenyika unogajika iGolokota linomila zwishingi zwafulumende. 202 Kumiswa nechimo cheKomishini yeZwishingi zwaFulumende

(1) Kunanekomishini yezwishingi zwafulumende ina nezwinotobela—

(a)

Ungalichibhula noNtobeli uwe; akale

(b) banhu babili zwinda kubashanu begapo yelo, bashalwa noNtungamili wenNyika.

(2) Begota lyekomishini yezwishingi zwafulumende banokwelela kushalwa kulingisiswa luzibo lwabo nobulefu bonshaji wababa nawo mukukwendeleja, kutungamilila kana mukushingila fulumende. 203 Kushinga kweKomishini yeZwishingi zwaFulumende

(1) IKomishini yeZwishingi zwaFulumende inota zwinotobela—

(a)

kugajika banhu balokwelela akale banabumhizha bwokutola zwigalo zwezwishingi zwafulumende;

(b) zwiva muchikamu 65(5), kumisa nokukwendeleja mashingilo abashingi, kushanganisila indipilo jabashingi, jimwe mali jokuta unshingo nezwimwe zwinokwelela kuwanwa, nezwishingi zwafulumende;

(c)

kushingisa masimba okululamisa nokukolola zwishingi zwafulumende;

(d) kushakisisa bukukutu bunanezwishingi nokukolola bukukutu bwezwishingi zwafulumende zwilonanga zwisungo zwezwishingi kana kusiywembali;

(e)

kuta zwanana zwinandyo muzwishingi zwafulumende, akale namamilo mabuya, ezwishingi zwafulumende;

(f)

kubona kuti zwishingi zwafulumende zwinota nshingo wazo zwanana akale zwisinalushalulo;

(g) kuzibisa uNtungamili weNyika neGulukota inyaya jina chokuta nezwishingi zwafulumende;

(h) kukubusa muzwishingi zwafulumende milawu ilotulwa muBumbilo lyemilawo yeli; akale

(i)

kuta zwimwebo zwakwejiswa kana zwatulwa kukomishini neBumbilo lyeMilawu kana kuti Mulawu wePalamende.

(2) Ikomishini yeZwishingi zwaFulumende, nechisimatijo cheGulukota lyenhabi yezwishingi zwafulumende, ingabumba zwokutobela zwechinangwa chingaba chipi zwacho chilotalwa muchibabe chechikamu (1).

(3) Ikomishini yezwishingi zwafulumende inokwelela kuta mishingo yayo itobela ingaba yipi zwayo milawu yatulwa inobapiwa neGulukota lyenhabi yezwishingi zwafulumende.

(4) Pakulonganyila indipilo yezwishingi, jimwebo mali jokushinga nezwimwebo zwinopiwa zwishingi zwafulumende, ikomishini yezwishingi zwafulumende inokwelela kuta ivumilwa noNtungamili weNyika apiwa busumikiji neGulukota lyenhabi lyezwemali mushule mokuvumilana neGulukota lyenhabi yezwizhingi zwafulumende. 204 Bamilili benyika nabamwe balokosha banomilila iZimbabwe kunejimwe inyika UNtungamili weNyika angagajika banhu kuba bamilili kana bamwe bamilili balokosha beZimbabwe kunejimwe inyika kana kupa isimba lyokumila makapo okuzhe kwenyika akale, panshaji ungaba upi zwawo, kubhisa banhu yabo pezwibhula. 205 Bakwali bakulwana munhabi

(1) Bakwali bakulwana munhabi banogajikwa noNtungamili wenyika mushule mokushaka luyeyo lwekomishini yezwishingi zwafulumende.

(2) UNkwali nkulwana wenhabi unoshinga kwamakole angaswika kumashanu, akale angapamhulwa kamwempela kulingiswa kutubula, mashingilo mabuya nezwinobhuda mukushinga konkwali nkulwana. CHISIYANISO 11 Igapo Lyezokulondotwa Kwenyika IMHINGU 1 Zwilotulwa 206 Kulondotwa kwenyika

(1) Zwinangwa zwezokulondotwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe zwinokwelela kutondeja kukutaija zwizwalwa zwomunyika yeZimbabwe kulalama mukukwelana, lunyalalo, kusupuka mulutyo, nomukubhudilila.

(2) Kulondotwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe kunokwelela kusimatijwa kutobelwa iBumbilo lyemilawo yeli nomulawu.

(3) Chimwe chezwilokosha ngechokuti, kukwabililwa kokulondotwa kwenyika kunokwelela kutiwa kulemekejwa—

(a)

ilulamilo jilokosha nolusungunuko nokusupuka kupa miyeyo nemilawo ilotulwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli; akale

(b) kukwendelejwa komulawu.

207 Igapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyika

(1) Igapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe lilofukatila zwinotobela—

(a)

baTabilijili benyika;

(c)

baShakisisi bezwokushotokwa kwemilawu;

(e)

lingaba lipi zwalo Igapo lezokulondotwa kwenyika lamiswa noMulawu wePalamende.

(b) Mapolisa;

(d) beZwomumajele nezokukololwa kwabalolunduka; akale

(2) Igapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyika lipo pasi pomulawu weBumbilo lyemilawo yeli, uNtungamili wenyika naBatobeli babe akale baipo pasi pobutongi bwe Palamende.

(3) Kuba mugapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyikakunokwelela kutondeja kushangana kwabanhu bomu Zimbabwe. 208 Kuzwibhata kwabegapo lwezokulondotwa kwenyika

(1) Begapo lyokulondotwa kwenyika banokwelela kushinga betobela zwimo muBumbilo lyeMilawu nomumulawu.

(2) Igapo lyokulondotwa kwenyika kana angaba ani azwe upo pasi pegapo yeli, pakushinga kwabo abatokwelela—

(a)

kuba nebhazu lyezwamatongelo enyika lyabanotungamija mbeli besinowhilila miyeyo yabamwe:

(b) kukubusa zwido zwelimwe bhazu lyezwamatongelo enyika kana kuta kuti;

(c)

2013

zwido zwelimwe bhazu lyezwamatongelo enyika zwipo pamulawu zwilingilwe pasi kana kuta kuti; kana

(d) kukanganisa ilulamilo kana lusungunuko lwomwe unhu.

(3) Begapo lyokulondotwa kwenyika abatokwelela kuba mugapo kana kuba nezwibhula munelimwe bhazu lyamatongelo enyika kana limwe gapo zwalo.

(4) Banoshinga kulondota inyika abatokwelela kungwiniswa unshingo kana kubhatikana muna amwe makapo asi okulondota inyika kuzhe kokuti ulinshaji wobukukutu unoshaka lubhancho nokukambija. 209 Ikansili yokulondotwa kwenyika

(1) Kuna nekansili yokulondotwa kwenyika inanoNtungamili wenyika elimbhati wechibhula, uNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika naMakulukota nabegapo lokulondotwa kwenyika nabamwe banhu balomisikwa muMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Zwinotiwa nekansili yokulondotwa kwenyika—

(a)

kukubusa milawo yokulondotwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe inokwelela kutobelwa;

(b) kuzibisa nokuchenjeja uNtungamili weNyika pezwinhu zwokulondotwa kwenyika; akale

(c)

kuta mishingo ilotalwa muMulawu wePalamende.

(3) MaKomanda okulondotwa kwenyika anokwelela kukwala mamakwalo anotondeja chimo chokulondotwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe epe iKansili yokulondotwa kwenyika somwainoba ikalingilila ikansili. 210 Inzila yokukwendeleja zwililijiko ilozwimila Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa inzila yokutambula nokupenyengula zwililijiko zwabanhu pezhulu pokusozwibhata zwanana kwabashingi begapo lyokulondotwa kwenyika ilozwimila akale inoshinga zwanana, akale nokukolola bukukutu bunobabuzilijwa nokusozwibhata kwakadalo. IMHINGU 2 Batabiliji Benyika 211 Batabiliji benyika

(1) Batabiliji benyika yeZimbabwe banoshanganisila masoja anolwa eli mumpepo neimwe mishingo inobamiswa pasi poMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Batabiliji benyika ndibo boga bayipo pomulawu muZimbabwe.

(3) Batabiliji benyika banokwelela kukuja ilulamilo nolunyalalo lwabanhu bose akale besitole ibhazu, kuzwipila kushingila inyika, kuda inyika yabo, kuba nobumhizha akale bebepasi pobutongi bwenyika somwakulotulwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(4) Batabiliji benyika banokwelela kundila mbeli beligapo lokutabilija lilozwibhata zwanana.

(5) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa imhingu, chimo, kukwendelejwa, milawu, kuzwibhata, nokunyamulwa nokudelujwa kwabashingi nabamwe begapo akale, zwiva muchikamu 218, mashingilo ezwishingi zwabatabiliji benyika.

212 Unshingilo waBatabiliji beNyika Unshingo wabatabiliji benyika ngowokutabilija iZimbabwe, banhu bayo, kukwabililwa kwenyika yabo nezwido nokubhatana pakukwabilila inyika nokutabilija iBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli. 213 Kugajikwa kwBatabiliji beNyika mumatunhu alosiyanasiyana

(1) Zwiva muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, uNtungamili weNyika chete, saKomanda nkulugulu waBatabiliji benyika, unamasimba—

(a)

okuvumila kusengwa kwabatabiliji benyika kumatunhu okushingila; kana

(b) okupa mashingilo aBatabiliji benyika.

(2) Peli nokuvumilwa noNtungamili weNyika, Batabiliji benyika bangasengwa kunoshingila muZimbabwe—

(a)

betabilija iZimbabwe;

(c)

bebhanchana naMapolisa nabamwe batungamili benyika kungoshakika nokukimbija kana kungoba nobukukutu.

(b) bebhanchana naMapolisa kukwendeleja kulongeka kwabanhu; akale

(3) Peli nokuvumila koNtungamili wenyika, Batabiliji benyika bangasengwa kunoshingila kuzhe kwenyika—

(a)

mukulondoteja lunyalalo pasi pegapo lyokubhatana kwenyika jopasi pose kana limwe lyamakapo ejimwe nyika kana omunyika iZimbabwe mwaili imhingu yegota;

(b) kutabilija ishango lyenyika yokuzhe;

(c)

kuzhajisa kuzwipila kushingila jimwe inyika; kana

(d) kutabilija kukwabililwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe kana zwido zwenyika.

(4) Pakushangana kwechikamu chotubili muchitatu (3/4) chabamilili bePalamende bebunga lyeGota Seneti negota lyabamilili bomuPalamende, iPalamende ingavumilana kushandula kusengwa kwabatabiliji benyika kunoshingila kuzhe kwenyika yeZimbabwe.

(5) Kana iPalamende yamisa kusengwa kwabatabiliji benyika kuzhe kwenyika, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kumisa kusengwa kwabatabiliji benyika, elingisa chido chokubona kuti zwishingi zwenyika yeZimbabwe nezwishingiso zwilokwabililwa kubukukutu. 214 Kumila kusengwa kwaBatabiliji beNyika kunoshingila kuzhe nabamili bezwamatongelo enyika Kana batabiliji benyika basengwa kunoshingila—

(a)

muZimbabwe bebhancha kukwendeleja kubambika kwabanji; kana

(b) kuzhe kwenyika;

uNtungamili wenyika unokwelela kuzibisa iPalamende, nokukimbija akale nokulungulula zwibuya, zwinangwa zwokusengwa kwezwishingi akale—

(i) kana basengwa mubugalo bweZimbabwe, bugalo bwabasengwa;

(ii) kana basengwa kuzhe kwenyika yeZimbabwe, inyika yabasengwa.

215 IGolokota lyenhabi yaBatabiliji beNyika UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kugajika iGulukota linokwendeleja Batabiliji benyika. 216 Kululamiswa kwaBatabiliji beNyika

(1) Mulawu wePalamende ungamisa kuti—

(a)

Batabiliji benyika banoba pasi pokululamiswa nakomanda umwempela; kana

(b) zwishingi zwaBatabiliji benyika mugapo mwempela, kana mabili kana manji eshingila pamwempela; anoba pasi pobululamisi bwamakhomanda alosiyana.

(2) Umwe komanda wose waBatabiliji beNyika, nomwe wose komanda webunga lyezwishingi zwaBatabiliji benyika, unogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mwokulebejana neGulukota lyenhabi yaBatabiliji beNyika.

(3) Makomanda aBatabiliji benyika namakomanda emishingo yaBatabiliji beNyika, anogajikwa konshaji usinopinda makole mashanu, akale unhu atokwelela kushinga pezwibhula yezwi kwemishaji mibili ilotalwa.

(4) Unhu unobashinga sakhomanda wonshingo waBatabiliji benyika angagajikwa sakomanda waBatabiliji beNyika, koga unhu unobashinga sakhomanda waBatabiliji benyika angagajikwe sakomanda wemishingo yaBatabiliji benyika kana kubululamisi bwe lingaba lipi zwal igapo lyezwokulondotwa kwenyika.

(5) Wose komanda waBatabiliji beNyika, naiye wose komanda wonshingo waBatabiliji benyika, unokwelela kushingisa bose bululamisi bubwe somwakulotalwa mumulawu unova kuGulukota lyenhabi yaBatabiliji benyika lyishinga pasi pobutongi boNtungamili weNyika. 217 Kutangwa nokushangana kweKomishini yemishingo yaBatabiliji beNyika

(1) Kuna neKomishini inolinga nemishingo yaBatabiliji benyika inanoNtungamili unokwelela kubaNtungamili weKomishini yezwishingi zwaFulumende, akale nabamwe banhu babili zwinda kubashanu nomwempela bagajwa noNtungamili weNyika.

(2) Begapo lyeKomishini yemishingo yaBatabiliji beNyika banokwelela kushalwa nokuda kwoluzibo kana bumhizha mukululamisa, kukwendeleja, inyaya jabalwi behondo, indayijo jabo jobumhizha bwokuta mishingo, kana kukwelela kwabo kugajikwa, akale—

(a)

chikamu chabo chinokwelela kuba banhu basi akale basijekebeli begapo lyaBatabiliji benyika;

(b) umwempela wabo unokwelela kuba wakabeli nechibhula chopezhulu muButabiliji bwenyika kwonshaji mwempela kana minji ingaswika kumakole mashanu. 218 Mishingo yeKomishini yaBatabiliji beNyika

(1) IKomishini yabatabiliji benyika inamishingo inotobela—

(a)

kungwinisa mishingo banhu banabumhizha akale banotubula kuba pezwibhula kana pezwigalo muButabiliji bwenyika;

(b) kugaja nokukwendeleja mashingilo, kushanganisa indipilo , jimwe mali jonshingo,nezwibokeso ,zwabashingi begapo lyaBatabiliji benyika;

(c)

2013

kulingisisa kugajikika nokululamiswa kwegapo lyaBatabiliji beNyika nokubanobumhizha bwopezhulu mukukwendeleja mishingo;

(d) kulingisisa kuti begapo lyabatabiliji benyika banotobeleja chikamu 208;

(e)

(f)

kunyamula kuwhanana nokuwhisisana pakati paBatabiliji benyika nabanhu bamwe bomunyika;

kulayaa uNtungamili weNyika neGulukota penyaya ingaba ipi zwayo yezwaBatabiliji benyika; akale

(g) kuta imwe mishingo yalebejwana kana yatulwa kuKomishini neBumbilo lyemilawo kana Mulawu wePalamende.

(2) Ikomishini yaBatabiliji beNyika, ivumilwa neGulukota lyenhabi yaBatabiliji benyika, ingatula milawu yezwinangwa zwipi zwazo zwilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu

(1).

(3) Pakutala indipilo, jimwe mali jonshingo nezwimwe zwibokeso zwabegapo lyaBatabiliji benyika, iKomishini yonshingo waBatabiliji benyika inokwelela kushinga ivumilwa noNtungamili wenyika ilopiwa pakukutaija kweGulukota lyenhabi yezwemali akale mushule mwokulebejana neGulukota lyenhabi yaBatabiliji benyika. IMHINGU 3 Unshingo Wamapolisa 219 Mapolisa nonshingo wawo

(1) Kuna Mapolisa anoshinga ku—

(a)

bona, kushakisisa nokukwabilila milandu;

(c)

tabilija nokukwabilila bupenyu nefuma yabanhu beZimbabwe;

(e)

mila iBumbilo lyeMilawuo nokubona kuti mulawu umutobelwa pesina lutyo kana lushalujo.

(b) londoteja kukwabililwa kwenyika yeZimbabwe;

(d) kwendeleja mulawu nokulongeka; akale

(2) Mapolisa anokwelela kushinga pamwempela na—

(a)

begapo lyabeMishingo inolinga nezwensiswa lingaba lipi zwalo lingaba miswa nomulawu;

(b) angaba ani azwe unhu angaba miswa nomulawu nechinangwa chokubona, kushakisisa nokukwabilila mamwe makapo emilandu; akale

(c)

makapo omumatunhu enyika nawokuzhe kwenyika anobumbilwa kulwisa kupalamulwa kwemilandu

(3) Mapolisa aatokwelela kutola ibhazu munelimwe igapo kana zwamatongelo enyika, anokwelela kutondeja chido chenyika yawo, kuda inyika yawo, kuzwibhata zwanana akale kuba pasi pamasimba abanhu bomunyika basi batabiliji benyika somwakulotulwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa kulongeka, chimo, kukwendelejwa, kuzwibhata nokunyamula nokudelejwa kwabashingi nabamwe banhu akale, zwiva muchikamu 223, mamilo amashingilo aMapolisa. 220 Golokota lyenhabi inobona nezwokushinga kwaMapolisa UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kugajika iGulukota lichabona nezwokushinga kwaMapolisa.

221 Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa

(1) Mapolisa aipo pasi poButongi bwa Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa unogajikwa noNtungamili wenyika mushule mwokulebejana neGulukota lyenhabi linobona nezwokushinga kwamapolisa.

(2) Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa unogajikwa kwonshaji wamakole mashanu ungamusilijwa kamwempela chete.

(3) Unhu unobashinga saKomishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa angagajikwe kululamisa lingaba lipi zwalo Gapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyika.

(4) Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa unokwelela kushingisa kululamisa kukwe zwikwendelana nezwilulamiso zwose zwilokwalwa zwinobapiwa neGulukota lyenhabi inobona nezwokushinga kwamapolisa eshinga pasi pamasimba obululamisi boNtungamili weNyika. 222 Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yaMapolisa

(1) Kunane Komishini yamapolisa inanoNtungamili, unokwelela kuba uNtungamili weKomishini yezwishingi zwaFulumende, akale nabamwe banhu balogajwa noNtungamili weNyika bangaba pababili kana bashanu nomwempela.

(2) Begapo lyeKomishini yamapolisa banokwelela kushalwa nokuda kwoluzibo lwabo kana unshaji wababa nawo beshinga mukubambanywa kwemilawu nechilulamiso, kukwendeleja, kana bumhizha bwabo bwemishingo kana kukwelela kwabo kugajikwa, akale—

(a)

chikamu chabo chinokwelela kuba banhu basi kana kuti bakabeli mapolisa;

(b) umwempela wabo unokwelela kuba wakabeli nechigalo chopezhulu kuchipolisa kamwempela kana kwemishaji ingaswika kumakole mashanu. 223 Unshingo weKomishini yaMapolisa

(1) IKomishini yeyi inanshingo unotobela—

(a)

kungwinisa mishingo balokwelela akale banabumhizha bokuba pemishingo kana pechigalo chopezhulu mumishingo yechipolisa;

(b) kululamisa nokukwendeleja mashingilo,kushanganisila indipilo,jimwe mali jemishingo nezwimwe zwibokeso zwokuta nshingo, kubashingi bokuchipolisa;

(c)

kusungwaja bubuya nokukwendeleja kubuya kwechipolisa nokululamisa kopezhulu mukutubula;

(d) kusungwaja bashingi bechipolisa kutobeleja chikamu 208;

(e)

(f)

kukutaija kulyijana kubuya nokuwhisisana pakati pamapolisa nabagali bomunyika; kupa luyeyo kuNtungamili weNyika neGulukota penyaya ingaba ipi zwayo inachokuta naamapolisa; akale

(g) kuta imwebo mishingo yalebejanwa kana yatulwa peKomishini yeyi neBumbilo lyemilawo kana kuti Mulawu wePalamende.

(2) IKomishini yamapolisa, ivumilwa neGolokota l yenhabi inolinga nezwamashingilo amapolisa, ingakwendeleja chingaba chipi zwacho chinangwa chilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (1).

(3) Pakutala indipilo, jimwe mali jokuta nshingo nezwimwe zwibokeso zwamapolisa, iKomishini yamapolisa inokwelela kuta alo ivumilwa noNtungamili wenyika apiwa kusimatija neGulukota lyenhabi inobona nezwemali akale mushule mokulebejana neGulukota lyenhabi inobona nezwamashingilo amapolisa. IMHINGU 4 Mishingo Inolinga Nezwensiswa 224 Kumiswa kweMishingo inolinga nezwensiswa

(1) Igapo lyose lyeMishingo inolinga nozwensiswa yenyika, lisigapo lyezwensiswa lyaBatabiliji benyika kana Mapolisa, linokwelela kumiswa zwiva mumulawu kana butungamili bwenyika kana chimiso cheGota lyamakulukota enhabi jilosiyanasiyana jafulumende kana chilulamiso.

(2) Mishingo inolinga nezwensiswa yenyika inokwelela kusotola ibhazu munelimwe igapo kana zwamatongelo enyika, kutondeja chido chenyika, kuda inyika, kuzwibhata zwanana akale kuba pasi pamasimba abanhu bomunyika basi batabiliji benyika somwakulotulwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli. 225 IGulukota lyenhabi yezweMishingo inolinga nezwensiswa yenyika UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kugajika iGulukota lyenhabi lichalinga nezweMishingo inolinga nezwensiswa yenyika. 226 Kululamisa kana kukwendelejwa kweMishingo inolinga nozwensiswa yenyika

(1) Mishingo inolinga nezwensiswa yenyika inokwelela kuba pasi pobululamisi nokukololwa ko Nkwendeleji-Nkulugulu weMishingo inolinga nensiswa unokwelela kugajikwa noNtungamili weNyika konshaji ulotalwa wamakole mashanu ungamusilijwa kamwempela.

(2) UNkwendeleji-Nkulugulu weMishingo inolinga nezwensiswa unokwelela kululamisa kana kukolola zwinova mumilawu yokwendeleja ilokwalwa inopiwa neGulukota lyenhabi yezweMishingo inolinga nozwensiswa eshinga pasi pamasimba okwendeleja koNtungamili weNyika.

(3) Unhu wakashinga so Nkwendeleji-Nkulugulu weMishingo inolinga nozwensiswa unotubula kusogajikwa kululamisa lingaba lipi zwalo Gapo lyezwokulondotwa kwenyika. IMHINGU 5 Majele Nokukololwa Kwabanota Milandu 227 Majele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu namashingilo awo

(1) Kunegapo lyaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu linolinga nezwo—

(a)

kukwabililwa kwabanhu kuva kubugandanga mukubiga mumajele nokukolola bawanika belinondandu nabo balolingililwa kuvalilwa zwipo pamulawu, akale nokubabwiliza kunoba balotambulika kunabamwe; akale

(b) kukwendelejwa kwamajele nobugalo bwokukolola balolunduka.

(2) BaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu banokwelela kusotola ibhazu, kutondeja chido chenyika, kuda inyika, kuzwibhata zwanana nokuba pasi pamasimba abanhu bomunyika basi batabiliji benyika somwakulotulwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa kulongeka, chimo, kukwendeleja, kuzwibhata nokunyamula nokudelejwa kwabashingi nabamwe banhu akale, zwiva muchikamu 223, mamilo abegapo lyaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. 228 IGulukota lyenhabi yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu UNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kugajika iGulukota lyenhabi lichalinga nezwaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. 229 Komishina-Nkulugulu waMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu

(1) Majele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu zwipo pasi pokululamisa kwaKomishina-Nkulugulu wamajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu unogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mokulebejana neGulukota lyenhabi inobona nezwaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu.

(2) Komishina-Nkulugulu waMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu unogajikwa pechibhula kwonshaji wamakole mashanu ungamusilijwa kamwempela.

(3) Unhu unobashinga saKomishina-Nkulugulu waMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu angagajikwe kululamisa lingaba lipi zwalo Gapo lyezokulondotwa kwenyika.

(4) Komishina-Nkulugulu waMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu unokwelela kushingisa kululamisa kukwe zwikwendelana nezwilulamiso zwose zwilokwalwa zwinobapiwa neGolokota lyenhabi inobona nezwokushinga kwabaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu eshinga pasi pamasimba obululamisi boNtungamili weNyika. 230 Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu

(1) Kunane Komishini yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu inano Ntungamili, unokwelela kuba uNtungamili weKomishini yezwishingi zwaFulumende, akale nabamwe banhu balogajwa noNtungamili weNyika bangaba babili kana bashanu nomwempela.

(2) Begapo lyeKomishini yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu banokwelela kushalwa nokuda koluzibo lwabo kana unshaji wababa nawo beshinga kukwendeleja, kubambanya, munyaya jokulondota, kana bumhizha bwabo bwemishingo kana kukwelela kwabo kugajikwa, akale—

(a)

chikamu chabo chinokwelela kuba banhu basi kana kuti bakabeli bashingi bomuMajele nokukololwa kwemilandu;

(b) umwempela wabo unokwelela kuba wakabeli nechigalo chopezhulu muMajele nomukukololwa kwabanota milandu kamwempela kana kanji zwingaswika kumakole mashanu. 231 Mishingo yabomuMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu

(1) IKomishini yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu inota mishingo inotobela—

(a)

kungwinisa mishingo banhu balokwelela akale banabumhizha bokutola zwigalo kana zwibhula zwopezhulu muMajele nomukukololwa kwabanota milandu;

(b) kubumba nokukwendeleja mashingilo, pamwempela nendipilo, jimwe mali jemishingo nezwimwe zwibokeso, zwabashingi bomuMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu;

(c)

2013

kusungwaja bubuya nokukwendelejwa kwaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu akale nokubambanya kwayo mubumhizha bopezhulu bokuta unshingo;

(d) kusungwaja bashingi bomuMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu kutobela zwimo muchikamu 208;

(e)

(f)

kukutaija kulyijana kubuya nokuwhisisana pakati pabaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu nabagali bomunyika;

kupa luyeyo kuNtungamili wenyika neGulukota penyaya ingaba ipi zwayo inachokuta nabanoshinga muMajele nomukukololwa kwabanota milandu; akale

(g) kuta imwebo mishingo yalebejanwa kana yatulwa peKomishini yeyi neBumbilo lyemilawo kana kuti Mulawu wePalamende.

(2) IKomishini yaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu, ivumilwa neGolokota l yenhabi inolinga nezwamashingilo omuMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu, ingakwendeleja chingaba chipi zwacho chinangwa chilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (1).

(3) Pakutala indipilo, jimwe mali jokuta nshingo nezwimwe zwibokeso zwabanoshinga muMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu, iKomishini yabaMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu inokwelela kuta alo ivumilwa noNtungamili wenyika apiwa kusinikija neGolokota lyenhabi linobona nezwemali akale mushule mokulebejana neGolokota lyenhabi inobona nezwamashingilo abomuMajele nokukololwa kwabanota milandu. CHISIYANISO 12 Makapo Alozwimilila Oga Anobhancha Butongi Bwabanhu Banji IMHINGU 1 Zwimwe Zwazo 232 MaKomishini alozwimila Anotobela maKomishini alozwimila—

(a)

ikomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe;

(c)

iKomishini inobona nezwokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabunhukaji muZimbabwe;

(b) iKomishini inobona nozwelulamilo abanhu muZimbabwe;

(d) iKomishini inobona nezwokukwendeswa kwenyaya muZimbabwe; akale

(e)

iKomishini inobona nezwolunyalalo lunyalalo nokulekelelana muZimbabwe.

233 Zwibabalilo zwamaKomishini alozwimila Makomishini alozwimila anazwinangwa zwikulwana pezhulu pezwo zwalopiwa umwe nomwe—

(a)

kubabalila nokukwabilila ilulamilo jabanhu nobutongi bwabanhu banji;

(c)

kusungwaja kukwendelejwa nomulawu wenyika ulotulwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeNyika;

(b) kutabilijila lusungunuko nezwido zwabo;

(d) kusungwaja kuta zwinhu nenzila ipo pachena isina isiswa nokutondeja mashingisilo efuma lyenyika mabuyanana mumabunga enyika alosiyanasiyana;

(e)

(f)

kulondota kutobelejwa kwezwisungo zwilokosha zwobutongi nemilawo yenyika namabunga ose nabamilili bafulumende, namakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende; akale kubona kuti bubi bose bunotilwa banhu bunokololwa.

234 Bashingi bamaKomishini alozwimila MaKomishini alozwimila anamasimba okungwinisa banhu mishingo akale, zwiva mumulawu, kukwendeleja mashingilo abo. 235 Lusungunuko lwaMaKomishini

(1) MaKomishini alozwimila—

(a) alozwimilila akale aatolulamiswa kana kukololwa naani ani zwe;

(b) anokwelela kuta mishingo yawo ekasezamila kuBumbilo lyemilawo yeli; akale

(c)

anokwelela kushinga esina lutyo, kushaluja kana kuba nemiyeyo isina busumikiji bulopelela;

kojoba beshandula mibhuzo kuPalamende pezhulu pamashingilo abo mabuya.

(2) Inyika namabunga ose abanhu nabamilili bafulumende pezwikamu zwose, zwinda nomubutongi nezwimwe zwisungo, inokwelela kubhancha MaKomishini alozwimila akale inokwelela kukwabilila lusungunuko lwawo, kusotola ibhazu, kupelela nokutubula.

(3) Akuna banu bangajonga mashingilo amaKomishini alozwimilaa. 236 Begapo lyamaKomishini alozwimila banokwelela kusotola ibhazu kuna zwamatongelo enyika

(1) Begapo lyamaKomishini alozwimila abatokwelela, mukushinga kwabo—

(a)

kushinga betola ibhazu;

(c)

kuba nolubengo nezwido zwegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika zwipo pomulawu kana kusimatija; kana

(b) kukubusa zwido zwegapo lingaba lipi zwalo lezwamatongelo enyika kana kusinmatija; kana

(d) kukanganisa ilulamilo jilokosha kana lusungunuko lonhu angaba upi zwe.

(2) Banhu baimo mugapo lyezwamatongelo enyika kana ibunga pakungwina mishingo kuKomishini ilozwimila banokwelela kusiyana negapo kana ibunga yelo nokukambija akale mukati mwamazhuba makumi matatu batanga kushinga.

(3) Kana unhu umo mugapo lyeKomishini ilozwimila—

(a)

aba umwe wegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika kana ibunga; kana

(b) mushule mokuba umwe wegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika kana ibunga angwina mishingo kuKomishini akonelwa kusiyana nezwamatongelo enyika mumazhuba angaswika kumakumi matatu atanga kushinga;

unokwelela kuma kuba umwe weKomishini yabangwina mishingo.

237 Kungwina nokumiswa mishingo kwabegapo lyeKomishini ilozwimila

(1) Nechinangwa chokushala unhu kungwina mishingo kugapolyeKomishini lilozwimila lingaba lipi zwalo, ikomiti yezwilango nezwilulamiso inokwelela—

(a)

kushambaja chigalo;

(c)

kuba nekwejiso ipo pachena nabamilili balolingililwa;

(e)

kupa mazina ashalwa kuNtungamili weNyika.

(b) kukoka banhu koshala balokwelela;

(d) kubambanya mazina abashalwa kugajikwa; akale

(2) Umo mugapo lyeKomishini ilozwimila angabhiswa mishingo kana—

(a)

esichinotubula kuta unshingo uwe nokuda kwobukosha pombili kana kuba nobulwaji bwendangalilo;

(b) akonelwa kuta unshingo zwanana kanjinji;

(c)

awanikwa eli nondandu wokupamhulula kuta zwisilotambulika pemishingo; kana

(d) aba usichilotambulika kugajikwa kuKomishini ilokwelela.

(3) Inzila yokubhisa Babetekesi pezwibhula inoshingabo kubhisa unhu wegapo lyeKomishini ilozwimila. IMHINGU 2 iKomishini Yeshalujo Jomunyika yeZimbabwe

238 Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe

(1) Kuna neKomishini inozibwa nokuwhi Komishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe ina nezwinotobela—

(a)

uNtungamili wagajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mwokushaka luyeyo lweKomishini inolinga nezwokutongwa kwemilandu nekomiti inolinga milawu nezwilulamiso;

(b) imhingu jimwe shanu nenhatu jinodomwa noNtungamili wenyika pamazina asipo pasi pegumi lina mabili abashalwa nekomiti yemilawu nezwilulamiso zwilotulwa.

(2) UNtungamili weKomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe unokwelela kuba umbetekesi kana wakabeli umbetekesi kana unhu unabumhizha bokugajikwa sombetekesi.

(3) Kana kugajikwa koNtungamili weKomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe kusinokwendelana nobusumikiji bwegapo linobona nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuta kuti ikomiti zwiva mumilawu nezwilulamiso zwipo izibiswe nokukambija somwakunotubulika.

(4) Imhingu jeKomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe jinokwelela kuba banizi akale beshalilwe bunhu bwabo nokuba nonshaji beli pemishingo akale nokutubula kwabo kukwendeleja indebo mumakapo abanji kana abashoma.

(5) Imhingu jeKomishini yeshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe jinogajikwa konshaji wamakole mashanu nelimwempela akale jingagajikwa kabili konshaji unanga sowakale yoyu, koga akuna unhu angagajikwa kushinga neKomishini mushule mokuba ekebeli

mhingu kwegole kana kupinda, zwingabendila mbeli kana kuti pe, zwiswika kumakole gumi namabili. 239 Mashingilo eKomishini yenshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe IKomishini yeshalujo jomunyika yeZimbabwe inamishingo inotobela—

(a) kubambila, kukwendeleja nokulingula—

(i) ishalujo jechigalo choNtungamili wenyika namakulukota ePalamende;

(ii) ishalujo jobutungamili bomunalaunda namakansili omumatunhu nobukwendeleji bobutungamili bomumatunhu matukutuku;

(iii) ishalujo jemhingu jegota lyamashe lyakagajikwa nechikamu 285; akale

(iv) nama referendamu;

akale kubona kuti ishalujo yejo nama referendamu zwinotiwa zwanana, mukusupuka, pesina kukwibila, zwipo pachena akale kutobelwa mulawu;

(b) kulingula ishalujo joNtungamili weSeneti nondebelekeli akale kubona kuti ishalujo yejo jakwendelejwa zwanana zwimo mumulawu;

(c)

kukwala bachavota;

(e)

kubona kuti makwepele abachavota namakwalo amazina alolondotwa nokushingiswa zwanana;

(d) kubamba igwepele lyabachavota namakwalo amazina;

(f)

kutala miganu yobugalo bozweshalujo, mawadi neimwe miganu yobugalo bweshalujo

(g) kubumba, kukwala nokukobanya makwalo okuvotela, kuvumila makwalo nokushaka mabhokesi okuvotela, akale kugajika nokukwendeleja bugalo bokuvotela;

(h) kukwendeleja nokulingula indayijo yokuvota;

(i)

(j)

kupa banolingisisa nozweshalujo nama referendamu isimba lokukwendeleja nshingo wabo;

kupa zwilulamiso kubanhu banongwina mishingo yenyika kana yokukwendeleja mudunhu lyabo nechinangwa chokusungwaja kutubula, kusupuka, kukwelanisa, bubuya akale nokukwendeleja ishalujo nama referendamu zwipo pachena; akale

(k) kutambula zwililijiko zwabanhu nokulingisisa akale bete yezwo zwabanobona zwikakwelela kutiwa pezhulu pezwililijiko zwapiwa. 240 Zwingakoneja kuti unhu engwine unshingo kuKomishini yezweshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe Kupamhija kubanhu balolebwa nezwabo muchikamu 320(3), banhu banotobela abatotubula kungwina mishingo kuKomishini yeshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe—

(a)

zwishingi zwafulumende, zwisili babetekesi;

(c)

imhingu nezwishingi zwegapo linolulamiswa nemilawo namakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende.

(b) zwishingi zwomunhalaunda namakansili omumatunhu nabatungamili bomumatunhu;

241 IKomishini yeshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe inozibisa nezweshalujo nama referendamu Pezhulu pechizibiso inokwelela kutobeleja zwimo muchikamu 323, iKomishini yeshalujo jenyika yeZimbabwe inokwelela nokukambija, akale ishingisa iGulukota lilokwelela, kusenga chizibiso kuPalamende chokukwendelejwa kwatiwa ishalujo iliyose ne referendamu ipi zwayo . IMHINGU 3 Ikomishini Yelulamilo Jabanhu BomuZimbabwe 242 Kumiswa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe

(1) Kuna neKomishini inozibwa nokuti iKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe ina nezwinotobela—

(a) uNtungamili wagajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mwokushaka luyeyo lweKomishini inolinga nezwokutongwa kwemilandu nekomiti inolinga nezwemilawo nezwilulamiso;

(b) imhingu jimwe shanu nenhatu jadomwa noNtungamili weNyika pamazina asipo pasi pegumi lina mabili ashalwa nekomiti yemilawo nezwilulamiso zwilotulwa.

(2) UNtungamili weKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe unokwelela kuba unhu wabanobumhizha bokushinga kwamakole mashanu ana mabili somilili womulawu munyika yeZimbabwe.

(3) Kana kugajikwa koNtungamili weKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe kusinokwendelana nobusumikiji bweKomishini inobona nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kuta kuti ikomiti zwiva mumilawu nezwilulamiso zwipo izibiswe nokukambija somwakunotubulika.

(4) Imhingu jeKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe jinokwelela kushalwa nokuda kobunhu noluzibo nokuwhisisa, akale nokuba nonshaji, mukukwabilila ilulamilo jabanhu. 243 Mashingilo eKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe

(1) IKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe inamishingo inotobela—

(a) kusungwaja kuba noluzibo, nokulemekeja ilulamilo jabanhu nolusunungunuko panshaji wose mudunhu;

(b) kusungwaja kutabilija, kunyamuka nobumhizha bwelulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko;

(c) kulingisisa, kulingula nokubona kuti ilulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko jilolingiswa;

(d) kutambula zwililijiko zwinova kubanhu nokuta zwilokwelela kutiwa samabonelo abo;

(e) kutabilijila banhu bose mukuvunwa kwamasimba nokusokwendeswa zwibuyanana kwamashingilo afulumende, makapo nenhabi jayo pamwempela nezwishingi;

(f) kushakisisa zwito zwobukwendeleji kana unhu, pakunowanikwa kuti ilulamilo nolusungunuko jilotulwa pakutondeja ilulamilo javunwa nobukwendeleji kana nonhu sokubhaunulwa kwelulamilo;

(g) kumisa kubwilulula kubuyanana kushanganisila kusumikija kutongwa kwabapalamula milandu, panobavunwa ilulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko;

(h) kululamisa Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa kushakisisa milandu yobugebenga iloyeyeselwa kuvuna ilulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko akale kuzibisa kuKomishini nezwichababhuda pakushakisisa kwakadalo;

(i)

(j)

kusumikija kuPalamende zwilokosha zwingatiwa kunyamula ilulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko; kushakisisa inyaya jilonangana nelulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko nokulebejana kubuyanana; akale

(k) kushanyila nokulingula—

(i) majele, bugalo bunogala banenyaya jichinoshakisiswa, mankamba jinolondotwa banotizha munyika jabo nobumwe bugalo bunowanikwa basungwa; akale

(ii) bugalo bunogala banolwa bulwaji bwendangalilo kana kunovalilwa banamiyeyo ilolunduka;

kutila kubona mamilo abanolondotwa nawo banhu kwakaleko, nokupa busumikiji bwamamilo kuGulukota linolinga nezwokukwendelejwa komulawu ulonangana nobugalo yobo.

(2) Komishina-Nkulugulu waMapolisa unokwelela kutobela kululamiswa kwanobapiwa neKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe pasi pechibabi chechikamu (1)(h). 244 Zwizibiso kunda nokuva kuKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu

(1) IKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu ingakumbila kuti angaba upi zwe unhu,mabungano kana bamilili, bangaba kana balongwiniswa mishingo nafululumende kana mune imwenzila—

(a)

kuzibisa iKomishini nezwabatala kubhudililisa ilulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko lulotulwa muchitalwa chelulamilo; akale

(b) kupa ikomishini inyaya jayinoda kubambanya lukwalo lozibisa lunokwelela kusengwa kugapo lingabalipi zwalo lomuno nelokuzhe kwenyika pasi pebungano lyabelulamilo jabanhu, chivumilwano chilokwalwa kana chivumilano inyika yeZimbabwe yachilobhatana nacho.

(2) Pezhulu polukwalo lozibisa lwayilokwelela kusenga zwiva muchikamu 323, iKomishini yelulamilo jabanhu bomuZimbabwe ingasenga makwalo ozibisa kuPalamende pezhulu penyaya jelulamilo jabanhu nolusungunuko, kupindila muGulukota lilokwelela, muluyeyo lweKomishini, zwinokwelela kuswikiswa kuPalamende. IMHINGU 4 Ikomishini Yokukwelaniswa Kwabanhulume Nabanhukaji BomuZimbabwe 245 Kugajikwa nokubumbwa kweKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe

(1) Kuna neKomishini inozibwa nokuti iKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji inanezwinotobela—

(a)

uNtungamili unogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mokushaka luyeyo kukomiti pemilawu nokululamisa kupo; akale

(b) bamwe banhu bashanu nabatatu, banobeli—

(i) bashanu nababili banogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika kuva pabakwalwa bakashalwa igumi linababili bakabuzwa nekomiti pemilawu nokululamisa kupo; akale

(ii) umwempela unobashalwa negapo lyamashe lyenyika, agajikwa noNtungamili weNyika.

(2) Imhingu jeKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe jinokwelela kushalilwa kulemekejeka kwajo noluzibo lwajina nalo pezhulu penyaya jokukwelanisa banhu bechinhulume nabanhu bechinhukaji mukulebejana, mumagalilo, mukunyamuka nokudelela kwefuma yenyika mukutengeselana nomune zwamatongelo enyika, akale ikomishini inokwelela kumilwa zwilokwelana nabanhukaji nabanhulume. 246 Mashingilo eKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe Ikomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe inamishingo inotobela—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

(e)

(f)

(g)

(h)

(i)

kubona unkwendeselo wokukwelanisa banhu bechinhulume nabechinhukaji somwakulotulwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli; kushakisisa inyaya jokuvuna kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji; kutambula nokutola zwililijiko zwinova kubanhu nokutobeleja zwabanobona zwikakwelela kutiwa; kushakisisa pezhulu penyaya jokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji nokulyijana mubululami, akale kusimatija kushandulwa kwemilawu nezwito zwinotikana kukushalujana,nokubhatila kukanangwa kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji; kuchenjeja banhu banji nabomumabungano asinolulamiswa naFulumende zwokuta kuti banhulume nabanhukaji bekwelaniswe; kusimatija zwilobambwa zwokuta zwechokomende kutubulisa kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji; kusimatija kutongwa kwemilandu yokuvunwa kwelulamilo jokukwelaniswa kwabanhukaji nabanhulume; kushaka kubwilulula kubuya yopo panobavunwa kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji; akale kuta zwose zwilokwelela kunyamula kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji.

247 Zwizibiso zwilokwalwa zwinotiwa neKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji bomuZimbabwe Kupamhija pechizibiso chilokwalwa chailolingililwa kuvuma kutobeleja zwiva muchikamu 323, iKomishini yokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji, ishingisa iGulukota lilokwelela, ingasenga zwizibiso zwilokwalwa kuPalamende pezhulu penyaya jinokwendelana nokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji, mumiyeyo yeKomishini, zwinokwelela kuzibiswa iPalamende. IMHINGU 5 Ikomishini Yokushambajwa Kwendebo muZimbabwe 248 Kugajikwa nokumiswa kweKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo muZimbabwe

(1) Kuna neKomishini inozibwa nokuti iKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo yomuZimbabwe ina nezwinotobela—

(a)

2013

unTungamili unogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mokushaka luyeyo kukomiti pemilawu nokululamisa kupo; akale

(b) bamwe banhu bashanu nabatatu banogajikwa noNtungamili wenyika kuva pabakashalwa igumi linababili bakabuzwa nekomiti pemilawo nokululamisa kupo.

(2) Imhingu jeKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo muZimbabwe jinokwelela kushalilwa kulemekejeka kwajo nokutubula mukubambanya nomuluzibo lwabo nokuwhisisa inyaya jokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji nezwito zwopezhulu munyaya jokushanbajwa kwendebo. 249 Mashingilo eKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo muZimbabwe

(1) IKomishini yokushambajwa kwendebo muZimbabwe inamishingo inotobela—

(a)

kubhata, kunyamula nokukubusa lusungunuko lokushambajwa kwenyaya;

(c)

kubona kuti kumushambajwa muzwido zwabanji akale, kasakasa, kusungwaja bululami nokushanganisa miyeyo inomila banhu bomunyika yeZimbabwe;

(b) kunyamula nokusimatija zwito zwibuya nobunhu mukushambajwa kwendebo;

(d) kusimatija kubumbwa kwezwisungo zokuzwibhata kwabanoshinga kushambaja indebo akale, pasina milawo yokuzwibhata, kuyibumba nokubona kuti imutobelwa;

(e)

(f)

kutambula nokutola zwililijiko zwinova kubanhu akale, palokwelela, bete zwinokwelela kutiwa kubashaki benyaya nabamwe banhu banoshinga kushambaja kana kushambajwa kwenyaya banobawanikwa bavuna umwe mulawo kana zwisungo zwokuzwibhata zwabanokwelela kutobeleja; kubona kuti banhu bomu Zimbabwe banowana inyaya munzila ilozhulika akale ilinokukwelanisa;

(g) kusimatija kushingisa nokukubusa indimi jose jilovumilwa kushinga munyika yeZimbabwe;

(h) kusimatija kushingiswa kwetekinoloji impya mukushambajwa kwenyaya akale nomukukwendeswa kwenyaya;

(i)

(j)

kusungwaja kukwikwijana kusinalushalulo nokushanganisa mukushambajwa kwendebo; akale kushakisisa munyaya jolusungunuko lokushambaja nokuzwishinula, nokudalo kusungwaja kushandulwa kwemilawo.

(2) Chisungo chePalamende chingapa isimba kuKomishini yokushambaja yomuZimbabwe ku—

(a)

ta ishakisiso nokubhuzosa pezhulu—

(i) pechingaba chipi zwacho chito kana zwimo zwinokanganisa lusungunuko lwezwokushambajwa kwenyaya; akale

(ii) ponkwendeselo wokushambajwa kwenyaya; akale

(b) tola kana kusimatija chilango chingapiwa kubashambaji benyaya banobawanika bataluka mulawo kana kuzwibhata munzila yabasilokwelela kuzwibhata nayo.
