(3) Mulawu wePalamende chingakwendeleja kushambajwa kwenyaya.

250 Zwizibiso zweKomishini yokushambajwa kwenyaya muZimbabwe zwilokwalwa Pezhulu pechizibiso chilokwalwa iKomishini yeyi inokwelela kutobela zwimo muchikamu 323, iKomishini yokushambajwa kwenyaya yomuZimbabwe, ishingisa iGolokota lilokwelela, ingasenga zwizibiso zwilokwalwa kuPalamende pezhulu penyaya jopezhulu pokushambajwa, jingazibiswa iPalamende zwinda nomuluyeyo lweKomishini. IMHINGU 6 Ikomishini Yolunyalalo Nokulekejelana Munyika 251 Kugajikwa nokumiswa kweKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika

(1) Kwamakole gumi mushule mwokutanga kushinga, kuna neKomishini ichozibwa nokuti iKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika ina—

(a)

ungali wechibhula unodomwa noNtungamili wenyika mushule mwokushaka luyeyo lwegapo linobona nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu nekomiti yemilawu nokululamiswa kupo; akale

(b) jimwe mhingu shanu nenhatu jinogajikwa noNtungamili wenyika kuva kuna balokwalwa bashalwa babuzwa neKomiti yemilawu nokululamiswa kupo.

(2) UNtungamili weKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika unokwelela kubeli unhu wabeli nobumhizha bokushinga somilili womulawu kwamakole mashanu anamabili munyika yeZimbabwe.


(3) Kana kugajikwa kwoNtungamili weKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika kusinokwendelana nokusimatija kwe Komishini inobona nezwemilawo, uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela kubona kuti iKomiti yemilawu nokululamiswa kupo inozibiswa nokukambija.

(4) Imhingu jeKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika jinokwelela kushalilwa kulemekejeka kwajo noluzibo lwajo nokuwhisisa, nonshaji wajaba nawo jishinga, kulebelela, kubhatanisa nokukwabilila kukakabajana nokukwendeleja, kulekejelana pokukakabajana kwakapinda nokubumba lunyalalo. 252 Kushinga kweKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika



IKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika ina mishingo inotobela—

(a)

kubona kuti kukakabajana kwapinda kwalandulwa mubululami, kuchiliswa nokulekejelana;

(b) kukubusa nokusungula zwilongwa zwokunyamula kuchiliswa kwenyika, kubhatana nokushingila pamwempela kwabanhu muZimbabwe nokupeja makakatano mulunyalalo;

(c)

kubuza kulekejelana munyika nokusungwaja banhu kuleba ichokomende nezwakapinda nokukwendeleja kushandulwa kwezwipo nokubuza bululami;

(d) kukubusa makwendeselo namabungano pobululamisi bwafulumende kukwendeleja kulebejana pakati pamakapo ezwamatongelo enyika, bugalo bulosiyanasiyana, mabunga namwe makapo, kukwabilila kukakabajana nokusowhisisana kunomuka mumazhuba anotobela;

(e)

(f)

2013

kukubusa zwilongwa zwinobona kuti banhu balomilila kutongwa, kulangwa nokumwe kwose kushungulujwa banowana kulapwa nokubhanchwa kubwilila pezwimo zwabo zwantolo;

kutambula nokutola zwililijiko zwabanhu nokuta yezo zwabanobona zwikakwelela kutiwa pezwililijiko yezwo;

(g) kukubusa inzila jokukimbija kubona yopo pangaba nokukakabajana nokusowhisisana, akale nokutabilija nenzila jilokwelela;

(h) kuta zwose zwinokwendelana nokukwabilila kukakabajana nokunyamula lunyalalo;

(i)

(j)

kupeja nokumilana nokukakabajana pakati pobugalo bulosiyana-siyana, mabungano, makapo nomubanhu umwe nomwe; akale kusimatija milawo kubona kuti lubhancho, nokukwalwa kwezwilokwelela, kunopiwa kubanhu balolwajiswa nokukakabajana, malwaji nezwimwebo zwitiko.

253 Zwizibiso zwilokwalwa zweKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika Pezhulu pechizibiso chilokwalwa chailolingililwa kusenga zwiva muchikamu 323, iKomishini yolunyalalo nokulekejelana munyika , ishingisa iGulukota lilokwelela inokwelela kusenga zwizibiso zwilokwalwa kuPalamende zwenyaya jilonangana nolunyalalo nokulekejelana kwenyika, muluyeyo lweKomishini, zwichabekakwelela kuzibiswa iPalamende. CHISIYANISO 13 Mabungano Okulwisa Buboli Nemilandu IMHINGU 1 IKomishini Inolwisa Buboli muZimbabwe 254 Kugajikwa nokumiswa kweKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe

(1) Kuna neKomishini ichazibwa seKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe inanezwinotobela—

(a)

ungali wechibhula unogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika mushule mwokulebejana nekomiti yemilawu nezwilulamiso zwilotulwa; akale

(b) jimwe imhingu ishanu nenhatu jinogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika kuva kubashalwa basipo pasi pabaligumi linababili banobabuzwa nekomiti yemilawo nezwilulamiso zwilotulwa.

(2) Imhingu jeKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe jinokwelela kushalilwa kutubula kwajo, luzibo, nonshaji wajaba najo mukukwendeleja kana mukutongesa kana kushakisisa milandu kana kutubula kwabo kutola chibhula, akale—

(a)

unhu unokwelela kubeli nobumhizha bwokushinga somilili wemilawu muZimbabwe, akale ebeli aba nobumhizha yobo kwamakole angaba mashanu ana mabili;

(b) unhu unokwelela kubeli nobumhizha bwokushinga sonhu unoshinga kulinga imali jafulumende kana kuti unopenyengula mashingisilo emali jafulumende muZimbabwe, akale ebe eli aba nobumhizha yobo kwamakole mashanu anamabili; akale

(c)

unhu unokwelela kubeli namakole angaba iguni ashinga mukushakisiswa kwemilandu.

255 Mishingo yeKomishini yobuboli muZimbabwe

(1) IKomishini yobuboli muZimbabwe ina mishingo inotobela—

(a) kushakisisa isiswa nokubhisa pachena milandu inabuboli mumakampani aFulumende nayo alozwimila;

(b) kulwisana nobuboli, bubava, kutola zwinhu mukusobimbika, kusoshingisa zwibuyanana masimba kwaabo bana namasimba nokusozwibhata zwibuyanana mumakampani aFulumende nayo alozwimila;

(c)

kusungwaja kubimbika, kulondota nokushingisa imali zwibuyanana nokuta zwinhu pachena mumakampani aFulumende nomunayo alozwimila;

(d) kutambula zwililijiko zwinova kubanhu nokuta zwilokwelela kutiwa pezhulu pezwililijiko yezwo mwayinobebona zwikakwelela;

(e)

(f)

kubuja Komishina Nkulugulu wamapolisa kuti epotesese inyaya jinanokuta nobuboli etukozibisa zwawanika kukomishini mukushakisisa kwatiwa;

kusenga inyaya kunabo balopiwa masimba okubetekesa inyika kubetekesa milandu;

(g) kushaka lubhancho lunova kumhingu jokuchiPolisa nabamwe bose banoshinga kushakisisa munyika; akale

(h) kusimatija kunaFulumende nabamwe banhu nezwingatiwa kukubusa kulemekejeka nokumila nokukwabilila kusozwibhata zwanana mumakampani aFulumende nomunayo alozwimila.

(2) Komishina Nkulugulu waMapolisa unokwelela kuta zwatulilwa neKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe pasi pechibabi chechikamu (1)(e).

(3) Fulumende unokwelela kubona kuti, kupindila mukubumbwa kwemilawu nejimwe inzila, iKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe inamasimba okusimatija kusunga nokuvumila kutonga banhu banoyeyeselwa kubeli nobuboli, banobeshingisa masimba abalopiwa nenzila isilokwelela nokumwe kusozwibhata zwanana kumo mubukwendeleji bweKomishini. 256 Kushingiswa kwezwilotulwa muChisiyaniso chegumi linatubili kuKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe Zwikamu 234, 235, 236, na 237 ngozweKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe nemhingu jayo sofana iKomishini ilozwimila ilotulwa neChisiyaniso chegumi linatubili. 257 Zwizibiso zwilokwalwa zweKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe Pezhulu pechizibiso chilokwalwa chailolingililwa kusenga zwiva muchikamu 323, iKomishini inolwisa buboli muZimbabwe, ingashingisa iGulukota lilokwelela, kusenga zwizibiso kuPalamende pezhulu penyaya jinokwendelana nokusozwibhata zwanana mumakampani aFulumende nayo alozwimila, muluyeyo lweKomishini, anokwelela kuzibiswa iPalamende.

IMHINGU 2 Igapo Linamasimba Okubetesa Munyika 258 Kugajikwa nemishingo yegapo linamasimba okubetesa inyika Kunegapo linamasimba okubetekesa linamasimba okumisa nokubetekesa milandu likamila inyika nokuta ingaba ipi zwayo mishingo ilokwelela kana inokwendelana nokubetekesa yoko.


259 UNdungululindandu unkulugulu nabamwe bashingi

(1) Kuna noNdungululindandu unkulugulu unoba eli iye uNtungamili wegapo linamasimba okulungulual nokubetekesa milandu.


(2) Ihofesi yoNdungululindandu unkulugulu ihofesi yafulumende koga aitobeli imwe yezwishingi zwaFulumende.

(3) UNdungululindandu unkulugulu unogajikwa noNtungamili weNyika abujwa neKomishini inoshinga zwemilawu kutobelwa inzila yokugajikwa kontongi.


(4) UNdungululindandu unkulugulu unokwelela kuba unhu unabumhizha bwokugajikwa ssombetekesi wemilandu weGota lyopezhuluzhulu.

(5) Unshaji wokushinga kwoNdungululindandu unkulugulu makole mashanu nelimwempela akale unomusilijwa kamwempela konshaji wakale yoyu.

(6) Esanu atanga kushinga, uNdungululindandu unokwelela kuta ikowhe, mbeli koNtungamili weNyika kana unhu ulopiwa isimba lokuta alo noNtungamili wenNyika, munzila ilotulwa muShejulu yaTatu.

(7) Zwilotulwa zwilonangana nokubhiswa kombetekesi pemishingo zwinoshinga kukubhiswa kwoNdungululindandu unkulugulu pamishingo.

(8) Mashingilo oNdungululindandu unkulugulu, kushanganisa indipilo, zwinokwelela kupiwa muMulawu wePalamende, koga indipilo aitokwelela kudelejwa panshaji wokushinga koNdungululindandu unkulugulu.

(9) Indipilo yooNdungululindandu unkulugulu inova kumali inobunganiswa iva kukubhadaliswa kwabose bamakampani alosiyanasiyana omunyika.

(10) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutondeja kugajikwa kwebunga linongwinisa banhu mishingo yokubhancha uNdungululindandu unkulugulu mukushinga kukwe, akale unokwelela kutondeja—

(a)

bumhizha bwabanhu yabo;

(c)

kuti pakuta mishingo yabo, banhu yabo banokwelela kuzwimila nokusotola ibhazu nokukwendelejwa nemilawo akale nokutungamilwa nokululamiswa noNdungululindandu unkulugulu;

(b) mashingilo, zwito nokuzwibhata kwabanhu yabo;

(d) mamilo nokubumbwa kwegapo linamasimba okubetekesa munyika; akale

(e)

zwilozibilika, kushinga kulolongeka akale nobululami bwegapo linamasimba okubetekesa munyika.

(11) UNdungululindandu unkulugulu angabuja Komishina – Nkulugulu waMapolisa kushakisisa nokuzibisa kwali pezwingaba zwipi zwazwo, muluyeyo looNdungululindandu unkulugulu, zwinokwendelana nemilandu kana kupiwa kana kuyeyeselwa kubandandu, akale Komishina – Nkulugulu waMapolisa unokwelela kutobeleja bukwendeleji yobo. 113




260 Lusungunuko loNdungululindandu unkulugulu

(1) Zwiva muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, uoNdungululindandu unkulugulu—

(a)

ulosupuka akale atokwendelejwa kana kululamiswa nonhu; akale

(b) unokwelela kushinga esinotola ibhazu akale esinotya, shaluja, kusobimba kana kubenga.

(2) Undungululindandu – Nkulugulu unokwelela kubumba ezibise banhu bose mashingilo aloyeya kumisa nokukwendeleja kutongwa kwemilandu. 261 Kuzwibhata kwaBashingi begapo linamasimba okubetekesa munyika

(1) UNdungululindandu unkulugulunabashingi begapo linamasimba okubetekesa munyika banokwelela kushinga zwinokwendelana nezwimo muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, nomumulawu.

(2) Akuna unshingi wegapo linamasimba okubetekesa inyika mukushinga kukwe anga—

(a) shinga etola ibhazu;

(b) kubusa zwido zwelimwe ibhazu lezwamatongelo enyika kana kuta;

(c)

miyeyo inolingila pasi zwido zwelimwe ibhazu lezwamatongelo enyika kana kuta; kana

(d) kuvuna ilulamilo jilokosha kana lusungunuko lonhu angaba upi azwe.

(3) Bashingi begapo linamasimba okubeteskea abatokwelela kuba imhingu kana bashingi belimwe bhazu lezwamatongelo enyika kana mabunga.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende ungedila mbeli ubona kuti bashingi begapo linamasimba okubetekesa abatotola ibhazu kunezwamatongelo enyika. 262 UNdungululindandu unkulugulu unokwelela kuzibisa pegole kuPalamende UNdungululindandu unkulugulu unokwelela kuba pasi pobululamisi bwePalamende, eshingisa iGulukota lilokwelela, chizibiso chilokwalwa chegole chamashingilo nezwinotiwa negapo linamasimba okubetekesa milandu munyika, chinosengwa kuPalamende kusanu kwapinda makole mashanu nelimwempela mushule mokutanga igole linotobela igole lilotulwa muchizibiso. 263 Amwe masimba obetekesa Mulawu wePalamende ungapa masimba okubetekesa kubanu basi begapo linamasimba okubetekesa, koga masimba yayo atokwelela kudeluja kana kulwisa wegapo linamasimba pechikamu yechi.

CHISIYANISO 14 Fulumende Wedunhu Nomubugalo MATANGISILO Zwilokosha munelimwe ibhazu kubona kuti:

(a) kubhatana kulolondotejwa munyika yeZimbabwe nokukwabililwa zwimo zwose zwokusobhatana nokuzwishalula;

(b) bagali nobugalo bose bomunyika yeZimbabwe bunosupuka kushingila inyika yabo; akale

(c)

ifuma yenyika ilokobwa zwinokwelana nokushinga kwenalaunda kuzwipila kunyamula zwilokosha mubugalo bwabo;

kunokwelela kuba nokudelejwa kwamasimba nezwokuta zwinda kunabo bezwibhula zwopasi zwafulumende muZimbabwe. IMHINGU 1 Matangisilo 264 Kudelejwa kwamasimba afulumende nezwokuta

(1) Pakulokwelela, masimba afulumende nezwokuta zwinokwelela kudelujwa kunalaunda nokumatunhu amakansili amaguta nomumatunhu matukutuku anabumhizha bwokuta mishingo yeyi zwanana akale zwinandyo.

(2) Zwinangwa zwokudelejwa kwamasimba afulumende kubugalo nomumatunhu amakansili omumakuta nomumatunhu matukutuku anokwelela—

(a)

kupa masimba okukwendeleja bugalo kubanhu nokunyamula kubhatikana kwabo mukushingisa masimba enyika nomukuta zwashalwa zwinobaguma;

(b) kusungwaja kusupuka, kutubula, kuba pachena, kuzwimila nokubhatana kwafulumende munyika yose yeZimbabwe;

(c)

kulondoteja nokusungwaja lunyalalo, kubhatana munyika nokusopambukana kwenyika yeZimbabwe;

(d) kuvumila ilulamilo yamatunhu yokukwendeleja inyaya jabo nokuzwinyamula;

(e)

(f)

kukwelanisa kukobelwana kwefuma yomumatunhu nomunyika; akale;

kubhisa zwokuta nefuma kunafulumende wenyika kutila kusimatija igombana lyemali kunalaunda imwe neimwe, nekansili yemetropolitan nobutungamili bose bomumatunhu.

265 Zwisungo pezhulu pafulumende wedunhu nomubugalo

(1) Makhansila edunhu nawo mumaguta nabakwendeleji bomubugalo banokwelela kuti, mubugalo bwabo—

(a)

bebone kuti kuna bukwendeleji bubuya nokubhudilila, kubapachena, kuzwimila nokubhatana mubungano;

(b) bete yezwo zwilotulwa kwabali neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli kana Mulawu wePalamende;

(c)

bete mishingo yabo munzila isinongwinila mubugalo, mashingilo kana kulemekejeka kwebungano lyeimwe bhazu lyafulumende;

(d) beshingile pamwempela, kasakasa ku—

(i) zibisana, nokubhuzana, penyaya jinodisiswa kasa nabanji;

(ii) buza lunyalalo nokushanganisa zwabanota;

(e)

(f)

belondoteje lunyalalo, kubhatana munyika nokusopambulika kweZimbabwe; bekwabilile bupenyu bwabanji;

(g) bebone bumilili bubuya nokukwelaniswa kwabanhu mubugalo bwabo bwamasimba okutonga.

(2) Jose imhingu jinamasimba okukwendeleja inalaunda jinokwelela kushalwa nabanhu balokwalisa kuvota mubugalo bulogajikwa banamasimba okukwendeleja mumatunhu

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kupa inzila jilokwelela namakwendeselo anobuza kubhatana pakati pafulumende wopakati, namakansili edunhu nawomumaguta nabakwendeleji bomubugalo. 266 Kuzwibhata kwaBashingi bomudunhu nomubugalo bafulumende

(1) Bashingi bamakansili omudunhu nomukuta nabakwendeleji bomubugalo banokwelela kushinga betobela zwimo muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli nomulawu.

(2) Akuna chishingi chekansili yomudunhu kana muuta kana unkwendeleji womubugalo, mukushinga unokwelela—

(a)

kushinga elelekela kunamwe mapazu;

(c)

kulingila pasi zwido zwelimwe igapo lyamatongelo enyika zwipo pemilawu;

(b) kusungwaja zwido zwelingaba lipi zwalo ibunga lyezwamatongelo enyika, kana kuti;

(d) kukanganisa ilulamilo nolusungunuko lonhu angaba upi azwe;

(3) Zwishingi zwamakansili amatunhu namaguta nabatungamili bomubugalo abatokwelela kuba nezwibhula munelimwe bhazu lezwamatongelo enyika.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutula kusotola ibhazu kwezwishingi zwamakansili amatunhu na amaguta nawabatungamili bomubugalo. IMHINGU 2 Matunhu, Namakansili Amatunhu Namakuta 267 Matunhu nomubugalo bomumatunhu, madistiliki eZimbabwe

(1) Matunhu Zimbabwe yailokamulwa nawo ndiyaya—

(a)

iguta lyeBulawayo Metropolitan;

(c)

idunhu lyeManicaland;

(e)

idunhu lyeMashonaland East;

(b) iguta lyeHarare Metropolitan;

(d) idunhu lyeMashonaland Central;

(f)

idunhu lyeMashonaland West;

(g) idunhu lyeMasvingo;

(h) idunhu lyeMatabeleland North;

(i)

(j)

idunhu lyeMatabeleland South; ne idunhu lyeMidlands;

ane miganu ilomiswa muMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende—

(a)

unokwelela kukwendeleja kuganulwa kwamatunhu muma disitiliki; akale

(b) ungakwendeleja kushandulwa kwemiganu yamatunhu namadisitiliki;

mushule molebejana negapo linobona nezweshalujo muZimbabwe nabanhu bomumatunhu nomuma disitiliki anolinga nezwazo. 268 Makansili omumatunhu

(1) Kunamakansili omumatunhu edunhu limwe nelimwe , kuzhe kwamatunhu amakuta, ane zwinotobela—

(a)

umbhati wechibhula wekansili, unoshalwa zwiva muchikamu 272;

(c)

maShe amaSeneta mabili anoshalwa mudunhu lilokwelela zwiva muchikamu 120(1)(b);

(b) maSeneta anoshalwa kuva mudunhu yelyo;

(d) uNtungamili nontobeli uwe weKansili yaMashe enyika, yopo bugalo bwabo buli mudunhu yelyo;

(e)

(f)

imhingu jose jeGota lyabamilili jina miganu yeshalujo aimo mudunhu yelyo; bamilili muPalamende bechinhukaji banoshalwa zwikwendelana nechikamu 124(1)(b) kuva mudunhu lyakalelo;

(g) mameya nabagali bezwibhula, kana beshobwa nelipi izina zwabo, bamakansili okumaluwa nomumaguta mudunhu yelo; akale; and

(h) banhu igumi banoba bashalujwa kushingiswa buwandi bwokumilila zwadomwa muchibabi chechikamu (3).

(2) Unhu unatubula kushalujwa kuba imhingu yekansili yedunhuzwikwendelana nezwimo mu chibabi chechikamu (1)(h) kana etubula kushalwa kuba iMhingu yabamilili muPalamende.

(3) Kushalujwa kuba imhingu yekansili yomudunhu kunotiwa kutobelejwa noMulawu weShalujo, ulokwelela kubanda kuti unhu wadomwa mu chibabi chechikamu (1)(h) washalujwa eli pasi pegapo lyezwamatongelo enyika emililla chibabi chilodomwa—

(a)

zwikwendelana namavoti aposwa pakushaluja yabo balomilila mabungano aimo mudunhu munshalujo jenyika pakunoshalwa bamimili; akale

(b) kana banokwikwija beli bechinhulume kana bechinhukaji bekakwalwa zwitobelana, wechinhukaji eli unotanga.

(4)BChigalo chomukansili chemhingu yegota lyedunhu chadomwa mu—

(a)

indima (b), (c), (e) kana (f) yechibabi chechikamu (1) inoba isina umilili kana imhingu yasiya chigalo chokuba umilili muPalamende;

(b) indima (g) muchibabi chechikamu (1) inoba isina umilili kana imhinhu yasiya kuba meya kana umbhati chibhula wekansili mudunhu yelo;

(c)

indima (h) muchibabi chechikamu (1) inoba sina umilili sezwo muchikamu 129, sofana imhingu yabeli umilili muPalamende.

269 Makansili amakuta

(1) Pedunhu nedunhu lyeguta kuna nekansili lyeguta lina nezwinotobela—

(a)

kuBulawayo, meya weguta lyeBulawayo, uli iye umbhati wechibhula cheKansili yeNalaunda yeBulawayo;

(b) ku Harare—

(i) meya weguta lyeHarare, uli iye umbhati wechibhula cheKansili yeGuta lye Harare; akale

(ii) meya kana umbhati wechibhula unokwendeleja inalaunda inotobela ihulwana yomuchikuwa, unoba eli uNtobeli wombhati wechibhula weKansili yeGuta lye Harare;

(c)

jose imhingu jeGota Lyabamilili jina bugalo bwemiganu yenshalujo jajo aimo muguta lyenalaunda yeyo;

(d) imhingu jabechinhukajana jeGota Lyabamilili banoshalwa zwiva muchikamu 124(1)(b) kuva muguta lye nalaunda yeyo;

(e)

(f)

maSeneta aloshalwa kuva kuguta lyenalaunda yeyo; akale

ma meya nabatobeli bama meya nababhati bezwibhula nabatobeli babo, bangaba beshobwa bewhini, bobutungamili bose bwenalaunda mudunhu lyenalaunda yeyo.

(2) Chibhula chemhingu yekansili ilotulwa mu—

(a)

indima (a) kana (b) yechibabi chechikamu (1) chinosala chisina unhu kana imhingu isichili meya, uNtobeli wa meya kana umbhati wechibhula, so mwakunobeli;

(b) indima (c), (d) kana (e) yechikamu chechibabi (1) chinosala chisinanhu kana imhingu yasiya chigalo chayo muPalamende;

(c)

indima (f) yechibabi chechikamu(1) chinosala chisinanhu kana imhingu isichi meya, uNtobeli wa meya umbhati wechibhula kana uNtobeli wombhati wechibhula, somwakunobeli, woNtungamili womubugalo mudunhu lyeguta yelo.

270 Mishingo yamakansili amatunhu namakuta

(1) Ikansili yedunhu kana yeguta inandandu wokubhudililisa bupenyu bwabanhu nezwemali yedunhu lyayo, zwishanganisila—

(a)

kulonganya nokugajika zwito zwokubhudililisa magalilo abanhu nezwemali mudunhu lyayo;

(b) kukwendeleja nokugajika zwilongwa zwafulumende mudunhu lyayo;

(c)

kulonganya nokugajika zwitaliso zwokukwabilila, kushandula nokukwendelejwa kwezwiwanikwa mudunhu lyayo;

(d) kunyamula zwokushanyila mudunhu lyayo, nokukubusa zwinhu zwonshingo yoyo;

(e)

(f)

kulingisa nokutokoloja unshingisilo wezwiwanikwa mudunhu lyayo; akale kuta imwe mishingo inoshanganisila mishingo yokulinga nezwemilawu, ingapiwa kana kutulwa kwayili ne kana pasi poMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutula zwokugajikwa, mamilo nabashingi bamakansili amatunhu namaguta, nenzila yabanota mishingo yabo nayo.

(3) Imhingu jekansili yamatunhu kana jamaguta jinopa inshandulo, pamwempela nomwe nomwe kubagali bedunhu lyajo nokuna fulumende wenyika pakutiwa kwemishingo yajo. 271 Makomiti amakansili amaguta Pakuta mishingo yawo zwanana, makansili amatunhu nawamakuta angagajika makomiti koga ikomiti imwe ne imwe yakadalo inokwelela kutungamililwa nemhingu ilotulwa muchikamu 268(1)(h) kana 269(1)(h), somwakunobeli. 272 Babhati bezwibhula zwamakansili amatunhu

(1) Pakushangana kwayo kokutanga mushule mweshalujo, ikansili yedunhu inokwelela kushala umbhati wechibhula kuva kumazina angaba mabili abanhu balokwelela abuzwa ne—

(a) gapo lyezwamatongelo enyika lina zwibhula zwinji muGota Lyabamilili mudunhu yelo; kana

(b) kana kusina igapo lyezwamatongelo enyika sozwazulotulwa mundima

(a), igapo lyezwamatongelo enyika lyakawana mavoti manji akaposwa mudunhu mushalujo yemhingu jeGota Lyabamilili.

(2) Unhu unotubula kushalwa sombhati wechibhula wekansili yedunhu kana etubula kushalwa semhingu yeSeneti.

(3) Chibhula chombhati wechibhula chekansili yedunhu chigalo chinoshinga nabanhu banji koga achitoba chezwishingi zwafulumende.

(4) Esanu atanga kuta nshingo uwe, umbhati wechibhula chekansili yedunhu unokwelela kuta ikowhe lyokubimbika nelyechibhula mbeli kwonkwali wekansili yedunhu munzila jilotulwa muShejulu yaTatu.

(5) Umbhati wechibhula chekansili yedunhu angasiya unshingo nokuzibisa kusiya kukwe unshingo iye womene kukansili yedunhu.

(6) Umbhati wechibhula chekansili yedunhu unokwelela kusiya chibhula chiche—

(a) pezhuba ikansili yedunhu payinotanga kushangana mushule mweshalujo;

(b) angowhi atotubula kuba imhingu yekansili yedunhu;

(c)

kana kubambanywa kokubhiswa kukwe pechibhula kwavumilwana nemhingu imbili pakati penhatu jose jekansili yedunhu; kana

(d) kana abhiswa pechibhula negota lilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (7).

(7) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kubona nezwokugajikwa kwegota lilozwimila kuta unshingo wokubhisa babhati bezwibhula bamakansili amatunhu pechibhula, koga kubhiswa kungaba kupi zwako kunokwelela kuba—

(a) kusotubula kuta mishingo yechibhula chabo nokuda kobulwaji bwendangalilo kana bukosha pambili;

(b) bulunda bukulugulu pakuta mishingo;

(c)

kuta zwisilovumilwa panshingo wabo zwilopindilija;

(e)

kuvuna mulawu nokuda, kushanganisila mulawu ulotulwa nabakwendeleji benalawunda.

(d) kupiwa undandu wokusobimbika, kushingisa masimba nenzila isilokwelela kana kushingisa masimba echibhula nenzila isili iyo; kana

(8) Umbhati wechibhula chekansili yedunhu atosiya chibhula chiche kuzhe kwezwilotulwa muchikamu yechi. 273 Butongi bwamakansili amatunhu namaguta

(1) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuta, zwinokwendelana nechisiyaniso yechi, pakugajikwa nemishingo yamakansili amatunhu nawamaguta akale, kasakasa, pe—

(a)

nzila jamakansili amatunhu nawamaguta;

(c)

zwimo zwamashingilo emhingu jamakansili amatunhu namaguta; akale

(b) mishingo yababhati bezwibhula zwamakansili amatunhu namaguta;

(d) nzila yokugajikwa, mashingilo nokubhiswa mishingo kwabashingi bamakansili amatunhu namaguta.

(2) Mulawu weshalujo unokwelela kuta, zwikwendelana nezwimo muchisiyaniso yechi, pakungwinisa mishingo pezwibhula zwemhingu jamakansili amatunhu zwilotulwa muchikamu 268(1)(f) akale pezwibhula zwababhati bezwibhula bamakansili amatunhu, zwinokwelela kuzhajiswa—

(a)

nabanhu baiko kugakapo limwempela lezwamatongelo enyika nabo bekebeli pezwibhula yezwo, kana mishingo; akale

(b) kuzhe kwababhati bezwibhula, nonhu unokwendelana pobunhulume kana bunhukaji nabanhu bekebeli pezwibhula yezwo.

IMHINGU 4 Fulumende Womunalaunda 274 Bukwendeleji bwobugalo bwomuchikuwa

(1) Kuna bakwendeleji benalaunda jomuchikuwa bokumila nokukwendeleja malalamilo abanhu bomuchikuwa munyika yose yeZimbabwe.

(2) Bakwendeleji benalaunda jomuchikuwa banokwendelejwa namakansili alomilwa namakansila aloshalwa nabakakwalisa kuvota mubugalo bwechikuwa yobo akale betungamililwa namameya nababhati bezwibhula, bangaba beshobwa nenzila ingaba ipi zwayo.

(3) Mabunga abakwendeleji benalaunda angagajikilwa bugalo bomuchikuwa bulosiyanasiyana, akale bugalo bomuchikuwa bubili kana bunji bungatulwa pasi pobukwendeleji bwenalaunda imwempela.

(4) Bumhizha nenzila yokushalwa kwabanhu balotulwa muchibabi chechikamu

(2) zwilokwelela kutondejwa muMulawu weshalujo.

(5) Mulawu wePalamende ungapa masimba okutonga kunameya kana kumbhati wechibhula chabakwendeleji bwomubugalo bwomuchiKuwa, koga meya angaba upi azwe kana umbhati wechibhula ulopiwa masimba yaya unokwelela kushalwa nabakakwalisa kuvota mubugalo bwagajikwa bakwendeleji bwomubugalo. 275 Bukwendeleji bwomubugalo bwokusha

(1) Kuna bakwendeleji bomubugalo bwokusha, banogajikwa zwiva muchikamu yechi, kumila nokukwendeleja malalamilo abanhu bokusha.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kumila—

(a)

kugajikwa kwabakwendeleji benalaunda jokusha;

(c)

kushalujwa kwababhati bezwibhula, namazina angaba api zwawo abangashobwe nawo, kutungamila makansili adomwa mundima (b); akale

(b) kushalwa, nabalokwalisa kuvota mubugalo bokusha bulolingiswa, kwamakansili kukwendeleja malalamilo abakwendeleji benalaunda balodomwa mundima (a);

(d) bumhizha bwemhingu jamakansili adomwa mundima (b).

(3) Mabunga abakwendeleji benalaunda angagajikilwa bugalo bokusha bulosiyanasiyana, akale bugalo bulosiyanasiyana bubili kana bunji bungatulwa pasi pobukwendeleji bwenalaunda imwempela. 276 Mishingo yabakwendeleji bomubugala

(1) Zwiva muBumbilo lyeMilawo nomuMulawu wePalamende ungaba uipi zwawo, unkwendeleji wenalaunda unanelulamilo yokululamisa, pamiyeyo Yiye, malalamilo abanhu baimo mubugalo yobo bwabalogajikwa, akale unamasimba anooshaka kuta alo.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende ungatula mashingilo kubakwendeleji benalaunda, kushanganisa—

(a)

masimba okubumba milawo, kululamisa kana milawo yokubhudililisa kukwendelejwa kwobugalo bwabalogajikilwa;

(b) masimba okubhadalisa imali jilotalwa nemitelo nokuwana imali inokwana kunyamula zwinangwa nemishingo yabo.

277 Ishalujo jabakwendeleji bomubugalo

(1) Ishalujo jamakansela obukwendeleji b bomubugalo jinokwelela kutiwa—

(a)

pakushalwa kwamameya namakansela pamwempela nokushalwa kweMhingu jePalamende noNtungamili weNyika;

(b) pakushalwa, kusi kweshalujo jenyika, kuzhajisa chibhula chimwempela kana zwinji, nokukambija somwakulokwelela mushule mokuzhulika kwezwibhula yezwo.

(2) Ishalujo jamameya nababhati bezwibhula zwabakwendeleji bomubugalo, zwisi zwamameya kana babhati bezwibhula balopiwa masimba okushinga pasi pechikamu 274(5), jinokwelela kutiwa pakushangana kwamakansili alokwelela kotanga kutobelwa ishalujo jenyika.

(3) Kuzhe kwezwilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (2) kana Mulawu wePalamende, mameya, babhati bezwibhula namakansila abatungamili bomubugalo banotanga nshingo muzhuba lyashanu lina mana mushule mokuzibiswa kwezwabhuda mushalujo jenyika jekebeshala makansela. 278 Bulefu bwomshaji wezwibhula zwemhingu jabakwendeleji benalaunda

(1) Chibhula chaMeya, umbhati wechibhula kana kansela wabakwendeleji bomubugalo chinosala chisina unhu muzwimo zwilotulwa muchikamu 129, sokuti wekebeli imhingu yePalamende, zwingaba zwipi zwilotondejwa kuNdebelekeli kana uNtungamili weSeneti muchikamu 129(1)(k) zwilowhisiswa sezwilotulwa kuGulukota lilomila fulumende womubugala.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutondeja kugajikwa kwegota lilozwimila kuta unshingo wokubhisa mishingo mameya, babhati bezwibhula namakansela, koga kubhisa kose kwakadai kunokwelela kubeli ko—

(a)

kusotubula kuta mishingo yezwibhula zwabo nokuda kobulwaji bwendangalilo kana bukosha pambili;

(b) bulunda bukulugulu pakushinga;

(c)

kuta zwisinovumilwa nonshingo wabo zwilopindilija;

(e)

kuvuna mulawu nomwaza, kushanganisila mulawu ulotulwa nabakwendeleji bomubugalo.

(d) kupiwa ndandu wokusobimbika, kushingisa masimba nenzila isilokwelela kana kushingisa masimba echibhula nenzila isili iyo; kana

(3) Meya, umbhati wechibhula kana kansela wabakwendeleji benalaunda atosiya chibhula chiche kuzhe kwezwilotulwa muchikamu yechi. 279 Untilo wabakwendeleji benalaunda Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutula inzila jichatobelwa namakansili abakwendeleji benalaunda. CHISIYANISO 15 Batungamili Bebhatuko 280 Butungamili bwebhatuko

(1) Ibungano, chimo, namashingilo abatungamili bebhatuko pasi pomulawu wechibanhu zwilotambulika.

(2) UNtungamili webhatuko ulomila kutiwa kwamagalilo echibanhu, unshingo waShe wechibanhu nebhatuko, mulisa kana sabhuku, somwakunobekamiswa, mubugalo bubwe. 281 Zwisungo zwilokwelela kutobelejwa nabatungamili bebhatuko

(1) Batungamili bebhatuko banokwelela—

(a)

kushinga betobela zwimo muBumbilo lyeMilawu lyeZimbabwe;

(c)

kubhata bose banhu mubugalo bwabo zwilokwelana zwisina lushalulo.

(b) kutobeleja chibanhu pabukwendeleji bwebhatuko nokuta unshingo wabo nechinangwa ibungano lyobutungamili bwebhatuko mwainolingilwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu; akale

(2) Batungamili bebhatuko abatokwelela—

(a)

Kuba imhingu jegapo lingaba lipi zwalo lezwamatongelo enyika kana muneimwe inzila bebe nokubhatikana munezwamatongelo enyika;

(b) kuta zwinotondeja kulelekela kunelimwe ibhazu lezwamatongelo enyika;

(c)

kukubusa zwido zwegapo lezwamatongelo enyika lingaba lipi zwalo kana kuta kuti zwido zwalo zwikubuke; kana

(d) kuvuna ilulamilo jilokosha nolusununguko lonhu angaba upi azwe. 282 Unshingo wabatungamili bebhatuko

(1) Batungamili bebhatuko bana mishingo inotobela mubutongi bwabo—

(a)

2013

kunyamula nokumisa kukosha kwechibanhu mubugalo bwabo akale, kasakasa kunyamula zwilokosha mumhuli;

(b) kuba nechokuta kulondoteja malalamilo, ibhatuko, zwechibanhu nefuma yechibanhu yobugalo bwabo, kushanganisa gugalo bunokwijwa;

(c)

kukwendeleja kunyamuka;

(e)

kumisa kukakabajana pakati pabanhu mubugalo bwabo zwikwendelana nomulawo wechibanhu; akale

(d) zwiva muchisungo chePalamende, kukoba inyika nokukwabilila bugalo;

(f)

kuta ingaba ibi zwayo mishingo ilopiwa kana kutulwa kwabali muMulawu wePalamende.

(2) Kuzhe kwezwilotulwa muMulawu wePalamende, batungamili bebhatuko bana masimba, butongi, nokululamisa bugalo bokusha kana bumwe bugalo bwabalogajikwa kukwendeleja, nopezhulu pabanhu baimo mubugalo bokusha kana bumwe bugalo zwabo.

(3) Pakushinga kwabo, batungamili bebhatuko abaipo pasi pobukwendeleji kana bululamisi bonhu angaba upi azwe kana masimba, kuzhe kwokuti kwakatulwa alo muMulawu wePalamende.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutula milawu yokuzwibhata kwabatungamili bebhatuko. 283 Kugajikwa nokubhiswa pechibhula kwabatungamili bebhatuko Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutula zwinotobela, zwikwendelana namagalilo abanhu, malalamilo, ibhatuko, namatilo obugalo yobo—

(a)

kugajikwa pechibhula, kumiswa mishingo, kutola chibhula chomwe nokubhiswa kwabatungamili bebhatuko;

(b) kubumbwa nokumusilija bushe; akale

(c)

kupeja kukakabajana kokugajikwa pechibhula, kumiswa, kutola chibhula chasiyiwa nomwe nokubhiswa koNtungamili webhatuko pechibhula; koga—

(i) kugajikwa, kubhiswa pechibhula nokumiswa kwamashe kunokwelela kutiwa noNtungamili wenyika sokusimatija kwegota lyabamilili baMashe amatunhu kushingiswa iKansili yaMashe eNyika neGulukota linomila batungamili bebhatuko akale zwikwendelana nontilo webhatuko nebhatuko lyobugalo yobo;

(ii) kukakabajana pezhulu pokugajikwa pechibhula, kumiswa kana kubhiswa koNtungamili webhatuko kunokwelela kulandulwa noNtungamili wenyika sokusimatija kweGota lyabamilili baMashe amatunhu kushingiswa iKansili yaMashe eNyika;

(iii) Mulawu unokwelela kutula zwitaliso zwokubona kuti jose nyaya yeji jinolingiswa klinoku kwelanisa akale kusina kulingwa koluyeyo lwezwamatongelo enyika;

(iv) Mulawu unokwelela kutula zwitaliso kulondoteja lulemekejo lwamabungano ebhatuko nolusununguko lwawo kuva kukungwinila kwezwamatongelo enyika.

284 Indipilo nezwibokeso zwabatungamili bebhatuko

(1) Mulawu wePalamende chinokwelela kutula zwendipilo nezwibokeso zwabatungamili bebhatuko zwinolungiswa kulinokuvumila koNtungamili wenyika

zwipiwa nobusimitiji bweGota linomila zwemali akale mushule mokulebejana neGulukota linomila batungamili bebhatuko.

(2) Indipilo yoNtungamili webhatuko inokwelela kutolwa nokubhadalwa iva kumali ilobunganiswa iva kumakampani alosiyana-siyana omunyika.

(3) Indipilo yoNtungamili webhatuko aitokwelela kudelejwa elipechibhula kana ebhatila muhofesi ilokwelela. 285 Ikansili yenyika namakota amatunhu aMashe

(1) Kuna neKansili yaMashe enyika inobumbwa iva muMulawu wePalamende, unomila Mashe ose omunyika yeZimbabwe.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kugajikila idunhu limwe nelimwe, lisiguta lyamatunhu, igota lyaMashe amatunhu linanaMashe mudunhu yelo.

(3) Somwakulokwelela kutiwa Mashe mudunhu limwe nelimwe anokwelela kumilwa zwilokwelana muKansili yaMashe eNyika.

(4) Ishalujo joNtungamili wenyika, uNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika nemhingu jeKansili yaMashe enyika jinokwelela kukwendelejwa neKomishini yezweshalujo yeZimbabwe.

(5) lgota lyaMashe edunhulimwe nelimwe, linokwelela kushala, zwinova mumulawo weShalujo, Mashe achamila idunhu muSeneti zwiva muchikamu 120(1)

(b).

(6) UNtungamili noNtobeli woNtungamili weKansili yamaShe banoshalwa konshaji unoswika kumakole mashanu akale banotubula kushalwa kabili konshaji unokwelana noyo ulotulwa, koga unhu unobashinga kabili kwemishaji ilotalwa soNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika unotubula kushalwa soNtungamili wenyika.

(7) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutondeja zwinotobela—

(a)

kushalwa kwaMashe kuKansili yaMashe enyika, nobumhizha nokukonejwa kwabamilili kushalwa;

(b) ikowhe yehofesi ichatolwa nemhingu jekansili yaMashe enyika namakota aMashe enyika;

(c) kumusilijwa kwehofesi yemhingu jeKansili yaMashe enyika;

(d) indipilo, ipencheni nezwimwe zwibokeso zwoNtungamili wenyika, uNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika nemhingu jekansili yamaShe eNyika;

(e) inzila ichatobelwa pamabunga eKansili yaMashe omunyika nopaMakota aMashe omudunhu, akale;

(f) kusungulwa kwonkwali weKansili yaMashe omunyika namaKota aMashe amatunhu. 286 Unshingo weKansili yenyika namakota aMashe amatunhu

(1) Ikansili yaMashe omunyika akale, mudunhu lyayo, igota lyaMashe omudunhu lina unshingo unotobela—

(a)

kulondoteja, kunyamula nokukubusa magalilo abanhu bomuZimbabwe nebhatuko;

(b) kumila luyeyo lwabatungamili bebhatuko akale kusenga mbeli kulemekejeka nechimo chamabunga ebhatuko;

(c) kukwabilila, kunyamula nokukwendesa zwido zwabatungamili bebhatuko;

(d) kutambula bumilili nezwililijiko zwinobuzwa kwalili nabatungamili bebhatuko;

(e)

kupandulula nokuta kuti banhu betobele zwito zwilolulama akale zwinabunhu kubhazu lyabatungamili bebhatuko nokunyamula kutubula kwabo mukutungamila;

(f) kukwendeleja kupeja kukakabajana pakati pezwilonangana nabatungamili bebhatuko;

(g) kuta imwe yose mishingo ingabapiwa kana kutulwa kwayili neChisungo chePalamende.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende chinokwelela kubona kuti—

(a)

iKansili yaMashe omunyika namakota aMashe amatunhu banotubula kuta mishingo yabo bekazwimila akale bebhudililisa zwinangwa; akale

(b) banhu balongwiniswa mishingo neKansili yaMashe omunyika akale makota aMashe amatunhu banota mishingo yabo nokusungwala nokusoshaluja. 287 Ikomiti yoKulemekejana nokuzwibhata Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutondeja kubumbwa, kuba imhingu nenzila yeKomiti yaMashe yokulemekejana nokuzwibhata, kuta mishingo inotobela—

(a)

kukubusa nokutula kulemekeja nokuzwibhata kulotambulika kubatungamili bebhatuko;

(b) kupeja kukakabajana pakati pabatungamili bebhatuko;

(c)

kulingisana nezwililijiko pezhulu pabatungamili bebhatuko. CHISIYANISO 16 Ivu

288 Kupandululwa kweChisiyaniso 16 MuChisiyaniso yechi— “ivu” linane zwalinoleba zwilopiwa kwalili muchikamu 72. 289 Zwisungo pezhulu pevu Kushandula zwilolunduka nechimo chokusokwelanisa kuwanwa kwevu zwakabuzilijwa nobutongi bwabapambi, nokobelwa kwevu nokukwelanisa pakukobelana, kwabanhu benyika yeZimbabwea kwose kufuma, Milawu yokulima inokwelela kukwendelejwa nezwisungo zwinotobela—

(a) ivu alitopela akale alitojiba nokudalo, chibabi chobufumi bweshango yeZimbabwe;

(b) zwiva muchikamu 72, chose chizwalwa chomuZimbabwe china nelulamilo yokuwana, kutola, kugala, kushingisa, kusiyila umwe, kubiga imali, kuchenekesa kana kutengesa minda wokulima zwisinani noluji lulwe kana lubala lweganda lile;

(c)

kupiwa nokukobelwa kwemiminda yokulima kunokwelela kutiwa kusinoshalujwa akale kukwelaniswa, kulingiswa kukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji nezwido zwilosiyana-siyana zwabagali;

(d) bulefu bwonshaji wokushingisa minda bunokwelela kusungwaja kukoloka kopezhulu nokubigwa kwemali nonshaji muminda yokulima nezwizwalwa zwomuZimbabwe;

(e)

(f)

kushingiswa kweminda yokulima kunokwelela kupamhija bunji bwezokulya zwinokwana unhu wose, bupenyu bubuya, nezwokulya zwinobaka umbili nokuwanisa mishingo, nokulondotejwa kweshango kutila bachalalama munelyamangwana; akuna unhu angashayiswa nokusindamijwa ilulamilo yiye yokushingisa kana kugala pominda wokulima.

290 Kundila mbeli kwelulamilo yafulumende muminda yokulima

(1) Minda yokulima yose—

(a)

yakakwalwa muShejulu yashanu nembili yeBumbilo lyeMilawo lyakabelipo ntolo; kana

(b) izhuba lyokutanga kushinga kweBumbilo lyeMilawu lisanu lyaswika, zwakaboneka muchikamu 16B(2)(a)(ii)kana (iii) weBumbilo lyeMilawu lyakabelipo ntolo;

linolamba lili mumaboko afulumende.

(2) Kukakabajana kungaba kupi zwako kungaba pakati pechinhu chingabachipi zwacho chimo mu—

(a)

chizibiso chilokwalwa muShejulu yashanu nembili yeBumbilo lyeMilawu lyakabelipo ntolo; kana kuti

(b) chizibiso china chokuta neminda yokulima chakatulwa muchikamu 16B(2)

(a)(ii) kana (iii) cheBumbilo lyeMilawu lyakabelipo ntolo;

nolukwalo lokuba mweni nechipi zwacho chakalundulwa chingabeli muchizibiso yechi, azwitokoneja kushinga kwechibabi chechikamu (1) kana kushayisa inyika kuba mweni weminda yokulima ichalungululwa nezwayo sokupiwa kwazwakatiwa muchibabi chechikamu yechi. 291 Kundila mbeli kwelulamilo yabanobeli peminda yokulima Zwikwendelana nezwimo muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli, unhu angaba upi azwe,wakabeshingisa kana kugala, kana wakabeli nemvumo yokushingisa kana kugala pominda wokulima ungaba upi zwawo zhuba lokutanga kushinga kweBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli lisanu lyaswika, zwiva muchivumilwano nafulumende, unondila mbeli eli nelulamilo yokushingisa kana kugala pominda yoyo panshaji yoyo kana kuti mushule mwezhuba lyakatalwa limulebwa, zwikwendelana nezwakakwalilanwa kana chimwebo chivumilwano chakatiwa. 292 Kulondotejeka kokuba mweni kwabanhu bagala beli peminda Yokulima INyika ilokwelela kutola zwitaliso kana zwijibiljo zwilokwelela, kushanganisa zwitaliso zwomulawu, kuti ipe kulondotejeka kobumweni kunhu wose unobeli mweni kana ungali wopaminda wokulima zwipo pomulawu. 293 Kukobelwa kweminda yokulima naFulumende

(1) Fulumende angakoba minda ungaba upi zwawo umo mumaboko awe kunomwe unhu kuti ebe mweni kana unhu waalochenekesa kana umwe wanopa imwe mvumo yokugala kana kushingisa, koga kukobelwa kungaba kupi zwako, kunokwelela kutiwa kutobelwa zwilokosha zwinokwelela kutobelwa zwimo muchikamu 289.

(2) Fulumende aangakobele unhu minda inopinda pomwempela.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kutala inzila ingatobelwa pakutolwa nokukobwa kweminda yokulima naFulumende, akale mulawu wakadalo unokwelela kukwendelana nezwilokosha zwinokwelela kutobelwa zwilotulwa muchikamu 289. 294 Kukobwa kweminda yokulima nabeni bayo kana kuti banogala paili Zwikwendelana nezwimwe zwingalambijwa nomulawu, mweni kana unogala pominda wokulima unelulamilo lokuchincha bweni, yokubhatisa pechikweleti, kulojesa kana kupa umwe ilulamilo iye muminda wokulima. 295 Indipilo ilonangana nokutolwa kweminda yokulima yakabeli nabeni

(1) Chizwalwa chose chomuZimbabwe chinaminda wokulima wakatolwa naFulumende unshaji umulebwa nezwawo usanu waswika, chinokwelela kulipwa naFulumende pominda yoyo, kulipwa zwakwale kubhudilila kwose kwakabatiwa minda utolwa.

(2) Angaba upizwe unhu unaminda wokulima wakatolwa naFulumende izhuba lyokutanga kushinga kweBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli lisanu laswika akale panshaji yoyo ilulamilo jije pominda yoyu jakabeli jabimbiswa kana kulondotejwa nechivumilano chakatiwa naFulumende weZimbabwe naFulumende weimwe inyika, unokwelela kuwana indipilo kuva kuna Fulumende yevu nokumwe kubhudilila kwakababa ipo zwinda nechivumilano.

(3) Angaba upi azwe unhu, kuzhe kwonhu ulolebwa nezuzwe muchibabi chechikamu (1) kana (2), baneminda yakatolwa naFulumende izhuba limulebwa nezwalo lisanu laswika, unokwelela kubhadalwa naFulumende indipilo yezwakababhudiliswa pakutolwa kwominda.

(4) Indipilo inolipwa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (1), (2) na (3) inokwelela kulingisiswa nokulipwa zwinokwendelana noMulawu wePalamende. 296 Kugajikwa nokubumbwa kweKomishini inobona nezwevu muZimbabwe

(1) Kuna neKomishini ichazibwa nokuti iKomishini inobona nezwevu muZimbabwe ina nezwinotobela—

(a) umbhati wechibhula nontobeli wombhati wechibhula; akale

(b) imhingu jinotanga pembili zwinda kubashanu banababili;

banodomwa noNtungamili wenyika.

(2) Imhingu jeKomishini inobona nezwevu muZimbabwe inokwelela—

(a)

kushalilwa kulemekejeka, nobumhizha bokuta unshingo, pamwempela nokuziba nokuwhisisa, zwilokosha mukushingiswa nokulondotejwa kwevu; akale

(b) kutondeja kusiyanasiyana kwabanhu bomuZimbabwe, kasakasa zwido zwamatunhu alosiyanasiyana nokukwelaniswa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji.

(3) Chikamu 237 chinoshinga pakubhiswa pezwigalo kwemhingu jeKomishini inobona nezwevu muZimbabwe sokuti iKomishini ilozwimila yoga sozwakulotulwa muChisiyaniso 12. 297 Mishingo yeKomishini inobona nezweminda

(1) IKomishini inobona nezweminda muZimbabwe ina mishingo inotobela—

(a) kubona kuti kukobelanwa kwevu lyenyika, kunokwendeswa nezila ilolulama, isina kushalula akale isina isiswa;

(b) kugala ilingisisa nokubonisisa unshaji nonshaji mashingisilo nokukobelanwa kwevu;

(c)

kupa miyeyo yokusimatija kuna Fulumende pezhulu po—

(i) kutolwa kweminda isili yaFulumende kuti ishingiswe pezwinangwa zwabanji;

(ii) kuwaniswa zwilokwelana pakuwana kana kugala peminda yokulima, kasa kasa— A. kubhisa zwimo zwose zwokushaluja zwibi, kasakasa kushalujwa kwabanhulume nabanhukaji; B. kusimiswa komulawu ungaba upi zwawo unoleba bunji bweminda yokulima yonhu umwempela kana banhu bopengumba imwempela;

(iii) mashingisilo evu namakubukilo eminda yokulima;

(iv) kulelupiswa kwezwinodiwa pakuwana nokushandula ilulamilo yeminda;

(v) nzila jina chokuta nobulefu bobumweni pominda; akale

(vi) kulipwa zwilokwanilana kweminda yokulima nokubhudilila kose kwababa ipo peminda isanu yatolwa naFulumende;

(vii) kukobelwa nokupilijwa kunomwe kwelulamilo jeminda yokulima;

(d) kushakisisa nokutonga inyaya jezwililijiko nokusowhanana kuna chokuta nokutungamililwa, kukwendelejwa nokukobelwa kweminda yokulima.

(2) IKomishini inobona nezweminda muZimbabwe, mushule mwokupiwa imvumo neGulukota linobona nezweminda, ingatubula kubumba milawu ilonangana nechimwe chezwinangwa zwakalungululwa muchibabi chechikamu (1).

(3) IKomishini inobona nezweminda muZimbabwe inokwelela kuta unshingo wayo itobela zwisungo zwakakwalwa zwingaba zwipi zwazo zwabanopiwa neGolokota linobona nezweminda.

(4) Mukuta mishingo yayo, iKomishini inobona nezweminda muZimbabwe inokwelela kutungamililwa nezwilokosha zwilotulwa muchikamu 289.

(5) INyika namabunga ose nabamilili baFulumende pechikamu chipi zwacho, kushingiswa zwitaliso zwomulawu nezwimwebo, inokwelela kubhancha iKomishini inobona nezwevu muZimbabwe mukushinga kwayo akale inokwelela kukwabilila lusununguko lwayo, kusolelekela kunelimwe bhazu, kulemekejeka nokushinga zwanana.

(6) Fulumende unokwelela kubambanyila zwilokwana, pamilawu nejimwe nzila jilokwelela, kubona kuti—

(a)

iKomishini inobona nezweminda muZimbabwe inotubula kuta unshingo wayo zwanana akale ikasupuka; akale

(b) banhu banoshingila iKomishini inobona nezweminda muZimbabwe bata unshingo wabo nenzila imbuya, zwisina kushaluja akale besinolelekela kunelimwe ibhazu.

CHISIYANISO 17 Zwina Chokuta Nemali IMHINGU 1 Kushingiswa Kwemali 298 Zwinokwelela kutobelwa pakushingiswa kwemali yafulumende

(1) Zwinotobela yezwi ndizwo zwilokwelela kutobelwa pana zwose zwina chokuta nemali yaFulumende muZimbabwe—

(a) Kunokwelela kutiwa nenzila isina isiswa nokubona kuti zwinhu zwinokwendeswa zwibuyanana zwinechokomende penyaya jina chokuta nemali;;

(b) Inzila yokushingiswa kwemali yafulumende kulokwelela kunanga kubhudililiswa kwenyika yeZimbabwe yose; akale kasakasa—

(i) unshingo woku chekelwa imali yomutelo unokwelela kukobelwana zwilokwelana;

(ii) imali inobabunganiswa nenyika inokwelela kukobelwana zwilokwelana pakatipafulumende wenyika, fulumende wamatunhu nafulumende womunalaunda; akale

(iii) mali inokwelela kushingiswa muzwinhu zwina chokuta nokubhudilila kwenyika yeZimbabwe, akale banhu nobugalo bulosalila banokwelela kuwana lubhancho.

(c)

mishingo nezwibokeso zwokushingiswa kwefuma zwilokwelela kukobelalwana zwilokwelaniswa pakati pabanhu bayipo nasi nabachaba ipo mumakole anotobela;

(d) mali yafulumende inokwelela kushingiswa zwipo pachena, zwina buchenjevu, ilondotejwa isinozaniswa akale zwinandyo;

(e)

(f)

kushingiswa kwemali kunokwelela kutiwa nokuzwipila akale chizibiso chamashingilo chinokwelela kuwhisisika; akale

kukweleta imali kwafulumende nokukwalilana pasi kuna chokuta nechikweleti chenyika kunokwelela kutiwa zwipo pachena akale zwinobhancha inyika yeZimbabwe.

(2) Akuna mitelo ingachekelwa banhu kuzhe kokuti zwavumilwa neBumbilo lyemilawo yeli kana kuti muMulawu wePalamende.

299 Unshingo wePalamende pakubona nezwemali inobunganiswa nafulumende nokushingiswa kwayo

(1) IPalamende inokwelela kulingisisa nokubona mashingisilo email naFulumende, namaKomishini namabunga nabamilili bafulumende pezwikamu zwose, kushanganisila makapo anobona nezwemilawo, makampani anolulamiswa nafulumende, maKansili amatunhu namaguta nabatungamili benalaunda, kutila kubona kuti—

(a) imali yose inobunganiswa inopandululika;

(b) imali yose yakashingiswa iboneke kuti yakashingiswa zwanana; akale

(c)

zwinokwelela kulingisiswa nokutiwa pemali yose yakakobwa zwatiwa.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende chinokwelela kuleba inzila jinoshingiswa nePalamende pakushakisisa nokubona mashingisilo emali alebwa muchibabi chechikamu (1).

300 Panojibila imali ingachenekwa neNyika, chikweleti chafulumende nebimbiso yokobhadala chikweleti

(1) Chisungo chePalamende chinokwelela kutala—

(a)

mali inokweletwa nenyika;

(c)

zwikweleti nezwimwe zwinobimbiswa nenyika kuti ichabhadala kana kuti ichabwiliza;

(b) chikweleti chaFulumende; akale

akale panopelela mali kana zwingakweletwa apatokwelela kupindilija kusapiwa imvumo nabeGota lyabamilili.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende ulokwelela kutula zwinovumilwa nezwinolingilijwa pakubimbisa kwafulumende imali yokukweleta.

(3) Mukati mwamazhuba makumi namashanu anegumi mushule mokuti Fulumende ata chivumilano chokukweleta imali kana chokubimbisa kobhadala, Gulukota linobona nezwemali linokwelela kuta kuti zwakavumilanwa zwibhiswe muGazeti.

(4) IGulukota linobona nezwemali linokwelela kuti—

(a)

konshaji ungaba kabili pegole, kuzibisa iPalamende mashingilo ezwinhu yezwi—

(i) zwikweleti zwakatolwa naFulumende; akale

(ii) zwikweleti zwakabimbiswa kobhadalwa naFulumende;

(b) panshaji wakale yoyo iGota lyabamilili lizibiswa nezwemali inoyeyeselwa kuti ichabunganiswa nokushingiswa zwiva muchikamu 305, itule muPalamende mawhi alokwana pezhulu pechikweleti chenyika yeZimbabwe. 301 Kukobelwa kwemali pakati pafulumende wamatunhu makulwana namatunhu matukutuku

(1) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuzibisa no—

(a)

kukobelwa zwilokwelana kwemali yokushingisa pakati pamaKansili amatunhu nawamaguta nabakwendeleji benalaunda; akale

(b) zwimwebo zwingakobelwa kumatunhu nokubukwendeleji bwenalaunda pamwempela nezwilolingilijwa pakukobelwa kwezwinhu yezwi.

(2) Mulawu ulotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) unokwelela kulingisa, kushanganisila—

(a) zwido zwenyika;

(b) zwilobambwa zwingaba ipo pezhulu pechikweleti chenyika nezwimwe zwinokwelela kutiwa nenyika;

(c)

zwinoshakwa nezwinodiwa nafulumende wenyika, zwishalujwa nenzila yezwinangwa;

(d) chido chokuti kube nezwinobhancha banhu, kushanganisila zwikolo nezwibhadela, imhoswi, migwagwa, bugalo bokuzwibalayija nokubiga magesi kubugalo bulolingilwa pasi;

(e)

(f)

kuba ipo kwemali nokuta zwibuya kwemishingo yamaKansili amatunhu, nawamakuta, pamwempela nawobukwendeleji bwomubugalo

zwokubhudilila nezwimwe zwinodiwa namaKansili amatunhu nawamatunhu pamwempela nobululamisi bwomunalaunda; akale

(g) kusokwelana muna zwobufumi mukati nopakati pamatunhu.

(3) Mali isipo pasi pezwikamu zwishanu kuva muzana inobabunganiswa nenyika mugole lingaba lipi zwalo inokwelela kupiwa kumatunhu nokubululamisi bwenalaunda sechikamu chabo mugole lyakale yelo. IMHINGU 2 Chikwama Yafulumende 302 Chikwama chaFulumende Pana Chikwama chaFulumende chinobunganiswa chinokwelela kubeli indipilo yemali yabhadalwa, mitelo nezwikweleti pamwempela nejimwe mali jinowanwa naFulumende, jiva kungaba kupi zwako, kuzhe kokuti Mulawu wePalamende—

(a)

uda kana kuvumila kuti imali yeyo ibigwe munechimwe chikwama chakabumbwa chikanangana nechimwe chinhu; kana kuti

(b) uvumila undulamisi wakatambula imali yeyi kuti eyilondote, kana kulondota chikamu chayo, kutila kuti etubule kuyishingisa pane zwinoda imali pakutiwa kwemishingo yiye. 303 Kutolwa kwemali iva muchikwama chaFulumende

(1) Akuna imali ingatolwa iva muchikwama chafulumende kuzhe kwokuti inda kunobhadala zwinhu zwinobavumilwa neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli kana kuti noMulwau wePalamende.

(2) Imali inobatolwa mugombana lyemali yaFulumende inokwelela kubhadalwa chete kunhu unokwelela kubhadalwa.

(3) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuleba inzila—

(a)

yokubhiswa kwemali mugombana lyemali yafulumende neimwe yose imali yabanhu banji; akale

(b) yokubhata nokulondota imali imomugombana lyemali yafulumende neimwe yose imali. 304 Indipo inobhiswa muchikwama chaFulumende

(1) Indipo yose inobhadaliswa pezikweleti zwaFulumende inokwelela kutolwa mugombana lyemali yaFulumende.

(2) Imali yose inobashinga pakubunganisa imali nokushingisa imali yomugombana lyemali yafulumende inota indipo yokutanga pechikwama yechi.

(3) Pechinangwa chechibabi chechikamu (1)— “indipo inobhadaliswa pezwikweleti” inoshanganisa imali inozwala, imali inobhatwa sendipo pemali inobakweletwa, imali inobigwa kubhazu kuti ibhanche kubhadala zwikeleti neimwe imali ingangoshakwa kushingiswa imali yomugombana lyafulumende, sechibimbiso chokuti imali yeyo ichobwilizwa, pamwempela nokubhadala zwikweleti zwinobabuzilijwa.

IMHINGU 3 Imvumo Yokushingiswa Kwemali Yomugombana Lyafulumende 305 Kukobelwa kwemali kuva muchikwama chaFulumende

(1) Igole negole iGulukota lilokwelela linobona nezwemali linokwelela kutula kuGota lyabamilili muPlamanende lukwalo lwemali inoyeyeselwa kuti ichabunganiswa pamwempela neichashingiswa naFulumende mugole lyezwemali linotobela.

(2) Imali inoyeyeselwa kuti ichabunganiswa pamwempela neinoyeyeselwa kuti ichashingiswa inokwelela kutulwa kuGota lyabamilili zwiva muchibabi chechikamu

(1) pezhuba lyakunoshangana imhingu jegota yeli igole lyezwemali lisati lyatanga kana kuti mazhuba makumi matatu esanu apinda, igole lyezwemali limwe nelimwe, asi kana iPalamende ingopalajwa kuti aichinotubula kuti inyaya yezwemali inoyeyeselwa kuti ichabunganiswa neinoyeyeselwa kuti ichashingiswa izibiswe kugota yeli panshaji yoyo, zwinokwelela kutulwa kuGota lyabamilili muPalamende mumazhuba makumi matatu mushule mokushangana kweGota lyabamilili kokutanga kuva palakapalajwa.

(3) Chikamu chimwe nechimwe chezwiloyeyeselwa chemali ichabunganiswa neichashingiswa inokwelela kupiwa kunechimwe nechimwe chezwinotobela—

(a)

iKomishini imwe neimwe yakabumbwa zwiva muBumbilo lyeMilawu yeli;

(b) ihofesi yonkulugulu unoshakuluja nezwokushingiswa kwemali yaFulumende;

(c)

inhabi inobona nezwokubetekeswa kwemilandu;

(e)

ibungano lingaba lipi zwalo lyakasimatijwa muMulawu wePalamende.

(a)

kuta kuti pebe nemali ichatolwa muchikwama chaFulumende kubhadala zwinobeda imali zwichabavumilanwa; akale

(d) iKansili yaMashe; pamwempela ne

(4) Kana iGota lyabamilili muPalamende lyavumilana nemali inoyeyeselwa kuti ichashingiswa mugole lyezwemali isi imali inova mugombana lyemali yaFulumende neBumbilo lyeMilawu yeyi kana muMulawu wePalamende, iGulukota linobona nezwemali linokwelela kuta kuti Mulawu uchinofhulwa lyomulawu wokukobelana kwemali ligajikwe muGota lyabamilili, akale Mulawu uchinofhulwa yoyu unokwelela—

(b) kuta kuti imali ikobelwe kuzwinhu zwinobadomwa pane zwinobayeyeselwa kuti zwichada imali, pasi pamavoti alosiyana siyana pemisholo yezwinoyeyeselwa zwavumilwa kuti zwitiwe.

(5) Kana imali inobakobelwa kuti ishingiswe pane zwichabadomwa mumulawo wezokukobelwa kwemali isinokwana, kana kuti imali idilwa zwinhu zwisakakobelwa imali, Golokota linobona nezwemali linobona kuti lyatula kuGota lyabamilili, inyaya yezwemali inoyeyeselwa kuti ichadiwa. Kana iGota lyabamilili lyatambula inyaya yeyi, iGolokota linobona nezwemali lichata kuti iBhili lyezwemilawo yokukobelwa kwemali linotobela lokutanga, litulwe kuGota lyabamilili kutila kuti imali inodikana kuva muchikwama chaFulumende ibhiswe. 306 Imvumo yokushingisa imali inobesanu yakobelwa

(1) Mulwau wePalamende ungavumila uNtungamili weNyika kuti epe invumo yokuti kutolwe imali muchikwama chaFulumende yokushingisa kubhadala zwinoda imali zwisakabazibikana kana kuti zwinoda imali inji inopinda yakabakobelwa pasi pomulawu ungaba upi zwawo, koga—

(a)

2013

mulawu autokwelela kuvumila kutolwa kwemali inopinda chikamu chopakati nopakati kuva muzana chemali yose yakakobwa pakukobelwa kwakapejeleja kutiwa mumulawu wokukobelwa kwemali;

(b) imali ingaba ipi zwayo inotolwa pasi pomulawu wokukobelwa kwemali yeyi inokwelela kushanganiswa pamwempela nemali inosopamijilwa pane inoyeyeselwa kuti ichabunganiswa neyinoyeyeselwa kuti ichashingiswa mukukobelwa kwemali, akale kana iGota lyabamilili benyika lyatambula kubambanywa yoku, imali yeyi inokwelela kuva mugombana lyemali yaFulufulumende kushingiswa umwe mulawu unotobela wokutanga, wokukobelwa kwemali.

(2) Kana Mulawu wokukobelwa kwemali mugole lyezwemali lisanu lyatanga kushinga pakutanga kwegole lyezwemali lyakalelo, Mulawu wePalamende unotubula kupa uNtungamili weNyika invumo yokuti imali ikwanise kutolwa kuva muchikwama chaFulumende kuti pebe nemali yokushingisa mumishingo yaFulumende mumyeji mina yokutanga yeyegole lyezwemali, koga—

(a)

Mulawu autokwelela kuvumila kutolwa kwemali inopinda chikamu chimwempela kuva muzwitatu zwemali yakabeyeyeselwa kuti ichashinga mugole lyezwemali lyakapinda;

(b) imali ipi zwayo ichatolwa pasi pomulawu yoyu inokwelela kushanganiswa mumulawu wezwokukobelwa kwemali mugole lyezwemali lilokwelela, pasi pamavoti alosiyana siyana emisholo ilosiyana yokushingiswa kwemali.

(3) Kana iPalamende yapalajwa pesanhu paba nemali ilokwana inodikanwa pakuta mishingo yaFulumende, mumulawu wePalamende unotubula kuvumila uNtungamili wenyika kupa invumo yokuti imali itolwe kuva mugombana lyemali yaFulumende kutila kuti zwinoda imali zwitiwe kuswikila kwapela myeji mitatu mushule mokuti iGapo lyabamilili benyika lyatanga kukushangana kutobelwa kupalajwa kwalo, koga imali ingaba ipi zwayo inobatolwa kushingiswa mulawu yoyu inokwelela kubigwa mumulawu wokukobelwa kwemali koga yakapasanulwa ibigwa muzwikamu zwilosiyana siyana zwokushingiswa kwemali. 307 Kushingiswa kwemali zwisina imvumo

(1) Kana zwingoboneka kuti imali inji yakashingiswa kupinda yakakobelwa pasi pechikamu yechi, kana kuti zwingoboneka kuti panemali yakashingiswa pezwinhu zwisakabakobelwa imali pasi pechikamu yechi, iGulukota linobona nezwemali linokwelela kutula Mulawu uchinofhulwa kuGota lyabamilili benyika likumbila imvumo yokundila mbeli nokushingiswa kwemali yoku kwakabeli kusavumilwa.

(2) Mulawu uchinofhulwa walebelekwa nezwawo muchibabi chechikamu

(1) linokwelela kutulwa kuGota lyabamilili muPalamende benyika nokukambija, akale kojoba zwata chini, mukati mwamazhuba makumi mashanu kuva panshaji unobazibikana kuti kwakashingiswa imali isakabavumilwa. IMHINGU 4 Kulondotejwa Kwemali Nefuma Yafulumende 308 Mishingo yabalondoti bemali nefuma yafulumende

(1) Muchikamu yechi— “imali yafulumende” inoshanganisila imali ingaba ipi zwayo yenyika kana kuti ingashingiswa naFulumende kana kuti nabamilili baFulumende,

kushanganisila zwikapo zwamatunhu nenalaunda jaFulumende, makapo ezwemilawu, namakampani anolulamiswa naFulumende;

“ifuma yafulumende” zwinoleba ifuma ipi zwayo yafulumende kana kuti ilolondotwa nenyika kana kuti ibungano lipi zwalo kana kuti bamilili bafulumende, kushanganisila zwikapo zwamatunhu nenalaunda jaFulumende, makapo ezwemilawu, namakampani anolulamiswa naFulumende.

(2) Unshingo wonhu wose unobona nezwokulondotwa nokushingiswa kwefuma yafulumende kulondoteja imali yeyi nokubona kuti yashingiswa pezwinhu zwakavumilwa zwipo pomulawu chete akale mubunji bwemali yakavumilwa pemilawu.

(3) Unshingo wonhu wose unobona nezwokulondotwa nokushingiswa kwefuma yafulumende kulondoteja ifuma yeyi nokubona kuti ayijalashika, kupalajwa, kukanganiswa kana kushingiswa pasili ipo kana zwisili izwo.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuta kuti kuvunwa kwezwimo muzwibabi zwezwikamu (2) na (3) zwikambije kubonekwa akale kuti kukololwa kwabanhu banobavuna zwimo muzwikamu yezwi akale, palokwelela, kubwilizwe zwakakanganiswa kana kutolwa. IMHINGU 5 Unkulugulu Wezwemali 309 Nkulugulu wokulingisisa nezwemali nemishingo yiye

(1) Kunokwelela kuba noNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali, yoyo unota unshingo wafulumende koga esi chishingi chafulumende.

(2) Mishingo yoNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali ngeinotobela—

(a) kutokoloja makwalo ezwemali, makwalo anotondeja mashingisilo akatiwa imali namashingisilo emali mumakapo ose afulumende, mumabungano nabamilili bafulumende, ose makansili amatunhu namaguta nabalulamisi bomunalaunda;

(b) angokumbilwa nafulumende, kutokoloja makwalo ezwemali mugapo lingaba lipi zwalo linobona nezwemilawu, kana mumakampani anolulamiswa nafulumende;

(c) kulayila kuti kutolwe zwitaliso zwokululamisa zwisimile zwanana mukushingiswa nokulondotejwa kwemali yafulumende; akale

(d) kuta ingaba ipi imwe mishingo ingabapiwa kana kutulilwa kuti ete kana kuti pasi paMulawu wePalamende.

(3) Zwishingi zwafulumende zwinokwelela kuta zwazwinobalayilwa kuti zwite noNkulugulu wokulingisisa nezwemali somwazwilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (2)

(c). 310 Kugajikwa koNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali

(1) UNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali unogajikwa pechibhula noNtungamili wenyika apiwa imvumo nePalamende.

(2) UNkulugulu unolingisa nezwemali unokwelela kuba chizwalwa chomuZimbabwe ashalilwa kulemekejeka, akale unokwelela kubeli nobumhizha bokushinga sontokoloji wamakwalo ezwemali konshaji ungaba makole gumi.

(3) Bulefu bwonshaji wokushinga koNkulugulu unolingisa nezwemali makole asinopinda mashanu nelimwempela, akale unhu yoyu atokwelela kudomwa kuba

uNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwe mali mushule mokubata unshingo yoyu, kwamakole mashanu nelimwempela kana anopinda, etobelana kana kuti esinotobelana koga makole yaya eswika kugumi lina mabili angoshanganiswa.

(4) Esanu atanga nshingo, uNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali unokwelela, kuta ikowhe lokubimbika nokuzwipila kunshingo yoyu pambeli poNtungamili weNyika kana umwe unhu unobavumilwa noNtungamili wenyika sokutalwa kwazwakatiwa muShejulu yatatu. 311 Lusungunuko loNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali Pakuta unshingo uwe uNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali ulosununguka akale etobeleja zwimo mumulawu chete. 312 Imali inoholwa noNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali

(1) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuzibisa imali nezwimwe zwibokeso zwinopiwa uNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali zwinotalwa nokuvumilwa noNtungamili weNyika zwitobela kupiwa kusimatijwa neGulukota linobona nezwemali.

(2) Imali inoholwa noNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali inokwelela kuva muchikwama chafulumende, akale aitokwelela kudelejwa echi pechibhula yechi. 313 Kubhiswa pechibhula koNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali

(1) UNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali angabhiswa pechibhula nokuda kwezwinotobela chete—

(a)

kusotubula kuta unshingo uwe nokuda kokulwala bulwaji bwendangalilo kana bukosha pombili;

(b) kusotubula kuta unshingo zwilopindilija;

(c)

kutondeja bunhu businotambulika zwilopindilija.

(2) Kana iGulukota linolinga nezwemali, livumilana neKomiti yePalamende inobona nezwemali yafulumende, lingozibisa uNtungamili weNyika kuti inyaya yokubhiswa kwoNkulugulu unolinga nezwemali pechibhula inoda kushakisiswa, uNtungamili wenyika unokwelela kubumba igota linoshakisisa nezwenyaya yeyi.

(3) Igota linoba bumbwa sezwakapiwa muchibabi chechikamu (2) linokwelela kubeli nabanhu banoswika kana kupinda batatu banodomwa noNtungmili wenyika, akale banokwelela kubeli zwinotobela—

(a)

umwempela wabo unokwelela kubeli unhu wakabashinga sontongi; akale

(b) umwempela wabo unokwelela kushalwa kuva pabanhu vatatu banobashalwa nebungano kana igapo linobabumbwa nomulawo limila banolingisisa nezwemali munyika yeZimbabwe.

(4) Ibungano kana kuti igapo lilotulwa muchibabi chechikamu (3)(b) linokwelela kushala banhu balebwa nezwabo muchibabi chechikamu yecho lingobabujwa kuti lite alo noNtungamili weNyika.

(5) Igota linobagajikwa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (2) linokwelela kubhuza penyaya yokubhiswa pechibhula koNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali akale, kana ladalo, linokwelela kuzibisa zwinobabhuda kuNtungamili weNyika akale lisimatija kuti uNkulugulu unolingisa nezwemali angabhiswa pechibhula kana pe,akale kana igota lisimatija uNtungamili weNyika unokwelela, kuti zwiva mwezwanolebeleka zwinodindwa nechidindo chenyika, unobhisa uNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali pechibhula.

(6) Igota linogajikwa zwiva muchibabi chechikamu 2 line lulamilo namasimba anokwelana nawamaKomishini pas poMulawu wamaKomishini anobona nezwokushakisisa [Chisiyaniso 10:07] kana umwe Mulawu unotola bugalo bwoMulawu yoyo. 314 Zwishingi zwipo pasi poNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuzibisa nezwokugajikwa kwebungano lyokushala banhu bokubhancha uNkulugulu unolingisa nezwemali pakuta mishingo yiye, akale chinokwelela kuzibisa zwakale pezhulu—

(a)

pezwinodiwa kuti kuwhi banhu yaba bewhi balokwelela kuta unshingo yoyu;

(b) pamashingilo abanota, zwabalolingililwa kuta nezwinotiwa pakubalanga nokubakolola;

(c) pokushinga zwilosununguka, zwisina kulelekela kubhazu limwempela nokubimbika kwabanhu yaba; akale

(d) pamabambilo namashingilo mabuya ehofesi yoNkulugulu unolingisisa nezwemali. IMHINGU 6 Zwilosiyana-siyana

315 Kutengwa kwezwinhu nezwimwe zwivumilano zinotiwa nafulumende

(1) Mulawu wePalamende chinokwelela kutula inzila jinokwelela kutobelejwa nafulumende pakutengwa kwezwinhu nezwokushingisa namabungano ose nabamilili bafulumende pezwikamu zwose, kutila kuti kutengwa kwezwinhu nezwokushingisa kutiwe nenzila ipo pachena, isinoshaluja, ilobimbika, isinolasha imali inji akale inakugundujana.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuta kuti pebe nokulebejana nokushinga zwanana kwezwivumilwano zwinotobela zwinotiwa nafulumende—

(a) zwivumilano zwokushingila pamwempela nabamwe;

(b) zwivumilano zwokubakwa nokushingiswa kwezwilobakwa nezwokushingisa; akale

(c) zwivumilano zwokucha zwichiwa nejimwebo ilulamilo; kubona kuti pebe nokutila zwinhu pachena, mukubimbika, kusozanisa imali nokugundujana. 316 Kutungamililwa kwamakapo anobona nezwemilawu Mulawu wePalamende unokwelela kuta kuti pebe namashingilo mabuya amakapo anobona nezwemilawu akale, kasakasa, unokwelela kubona kuti batungamili bamakapo banoshinga konshaji ulotalwa akale kuti kana bebwilizwa pezwibhula zwitiwe nokutubula kwabo kuta unshingo wabo. 317 IBhanga igulwana lyeZimbabwe

(1) Kuna nebhanga igulwana, lichazibikana seBhanga iGulwana lyeNyika linachinangwa cho—

(a) kulayila mashingisilo emali;

(b) kutabilija imali yeZimbabwe likoshesa zwido zwilokwelana nokukwendelejwa kwefuma kunondila mbeli;

(c)

2013

kubumba nokutanga chisungo chamashingisilo emali.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende ungatala mamilo namabambilo eBhanga iGulwana lyeNyika pamwempela nokupa kana kulayila imwe mishingo yainokwelela kuta. CHISIYANISO 18 Zwimwe Zwisungo Zwilopamhijwa IMHINGU I Zwimwe Zwisungo Zwamakomishini 318 Mashingisilo eMhingu 1 Yeyi imhingu inoshinga kuKomishini ilobumbwa iva muBumbilo lyeMilawu, nokunhabi jose jinanga seKomishini yeyi. 319 MaKomishini alokwelela kuba mabungano Makomishimi makubungano anondila mbeli akale etubula kusungisa nokusungiswa mumazina awo. 320 Kuba imhingu jamaKomishini nemilawu yamashingilo emhingu jawo

(1) Kuzhe kwezwisilokwalwa muBumbilo lyeMilawu, yose imhingu yeKomishini inobigwa pechigalo kwamakole mashanu, anopamhijilwa kwetemu limwempela chete.

(2) Imhingu jeKomishini, kuzhe—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

makomishini alozwimilila oga; iKomishini yezwamakota nemilawu; iKomishini inolinga nezwobuboli; ne iKomishini yozwemimida; banopiwa unshingo nokuda kwonTungamili weNyika.

(3) Pezhulu peBumbilo lyeMilawu, bamilili muPalamende nemhingu jamakansili amatunhu kana ma makuta, naomubugalo namakubungano anowana masimba kuva kunaFulumende, abatotubula kuba imhingi jeKomishini.

(4) Yopo iKomishini ili noNgalichibhula nontobeli uwe, balokwelela kuba wechinhulume nowechinhukaji.

(5) Besanu batanga mishingo, imhingu jeKomishini jilokwelela kutola imhiko/ ikowhe mbeli koNtungamili weNyika, kana mbeli komwe unobadomwa noNtungamili weNyika sezwazwilokwalwa muShejulu yatatu.

(6) Imhingu jamaKomishini jilokwelela kuwaniswa indipilo, nezwimwe zwilobakwelela sokukwalwa muMulawu we Palamende, akale indipilo jabo ajilokwelela kushomapajwa panshaji beli panshingo wabo.

(7) Indipilo neimwe imali yemhingu jamaKomishini, banolipwa kuva mumali yeChikwama chaFulumende. 321 Mishingo nonkwendeselo wamaKomishini

(1) Mulawu wePalamende ungapamhija imwe mishingo peKomishini akale ungabiga zwitabilijo zwenzila yokushinga kweKomishini, jana kuzwimilila kwayo iKomishini yeyi kusinomanikijwa.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende wadomwa muchibabi chechikamu (1) ungapa imvumo kuKomishini kutila mashingilo ayo, koga iKomishini aijilokwelela kupa masimba okubiga banhu pechigalo, kana kuleba umbono kana kupa indayijo, pezhulu ponshingo upi zwawo ulobambwa neKomishini.

(3) Pezhulu peBumbilo lyeMilawu,zwose zwisungo zwinotiwa neKomishini zwilokwelela kuwana kuvumilana kwemhingu jeKomishini inji, banoba belipo panshaji chisungo yecho chinoba chitiwa.

(4) Mulawu wePalamende ungatondeja unkwendeselo unotambulwa neKomishini, asi kana zwisakabigwa neKomishini, ingazwibigila unkwendeselo waingatobeleje, akale unkwendeselo yoyu ulokwelela kuba ukalulama akale ukutaija kutiwa pachena mashingilo eKomishini. 322 Kuwaniswa imali kwamaKomishini Igota lyePalamende lilokwelela kubona kuti imali ilokwanila inopiwa kumakapo amaKomishini, kutila kuti betubule kuzhajikija zwinangwa zwemishingo yawo nenzila ilolulama. 323 Makomishini alokwelela kusenga chinyeulilo kugota lyePalamende igole negole

(1) Makomishini ose anokwelela kukwala lukwalo lunonda kuGulukota lunotondeja mashingilo awo nemishingo yawo igole negole, lukwalo yolu lulokwelela kusengwa mweji woMwenje usanu wapela mugole linotobela yelyo igole likumukwalwa nezwalyo mulukwalo.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende ungada kuti iKomishini isenge amwe makwalo pezhulu pamakwalo anotondeja unkwendeselo unotambulwa neKomishini, sezwimo muchibabi chechikamu (1) akale ungadoma kuti makwalo yaya ekwalwe chini. IMHINGU 2 Zwimwe Zwazwo 324 Kutobelejwa akale pesina kujilwa kwamashingilo ezwisungo zwomuBumbilo lyeMilawu Zwose zwisungo zwomuBumbilo lyeMilawu zwilokwelela kutobelejwa munzila ilokwelela akale pesina kujilwa. 325 Kupiwa imali kwenhabi jafulumende namakapo amakomishini

(1) Ifulumende ilokwelela kubona kuti imali ilokwanila inopiwa—

(a)

kumakomishini nejimwe inhabi jinobumbwa pezhulu peBumbilo, kutila kuti jishinge munzila inandyo;

(b) kuPalamende, nokumakomiti ayo,kutila kuti itubule kushangana upi wose unshaji ulotalwa; akale

(c)

kunhabi jose jeNyika nejafulumende, kutila kuti jitubule kuta mishingo yajo ilotalwa muBumbilo yeli.

(2) Makomishini nejimwe inhabi jilobumbwa pezhulu peBumbilo yeli jinokwelela kupiwa unshaji ulokwana wokutula mbeli kwekomiti yepalamende mashingisilo emali ijakapiwa mukati mwegole.

326 Milawu yechibanhu yomunyika jose jopasi

(1) Milawu yejimwe inyika jopasi neyechibanhu imhingu yemilawu yeZimbabwe, kuzhe kwokuti kana ikaleyana neBumbilo yeli kana Mulawu wePalamende.

(2) Kana kupandululwa mulawu, lyose igota namakota alobumbilwa kubetekesa undandu woyo unshaji, kulokwelela kutambula kupandululwa kwomulawu unokwendelana nomulawu wechibanhu yomunyika jose unoshinga muZimbabwe, pana kushaluja kupandulula kushingiswa umwe mulawu uloleyana noyo mulawu. 327 Mabungano, zwisungo nezwivumilano zwinotiwa pakati penyika jose jopasi

(1) Muchikamu yechi— “Igapo lyopasi pose” zwinoleba igapo lina nemhingu imbili kana jinopinda, lyenyika jinozwitonga kana jinomilila inyika imbili kana jinpinda jimo mugapo;

“chisungo chenyika jopasi pose” zwinoleba unshangano, chisungo,untobelejelo wokutiwa kana chivumilano chivumilano pakati penyika imwempela kana jinopinda kana fulumende kana igapo lyenyika jopasi pose.

(2) Chose chisungo chenyika jopasi chinotambulwa kana kutiwa noNtungamili weNyika, kana chinotiwa mumasimba awe—

(a) achitosunga inyika yeZimbabwe kana chisanu chatambulwa nePalamende; akale

(b) achitoba chikamu chomulawu weZimbabwe kuzhe kwokuti chibangwiniswa mumulawu kushingiswa Mulawu wePalamende.

(3) Chilangano chisili chivumilano chenyika jopasi jose asi chili—

(a) chakatambulwa kana chakatiwa noNtungamili weNyika kana chakatiwa pezhulu pamasimba oNtungamili weNyika pakati pamakapo kana makubungano enyika jokuzhe, imwempela kana jinopinda; akale

(b) inobiga chisungo chonshingisilo wemali yenyika yeZimbabwe; achitosunga inyika yeZimbabwe kuswikila chavumilwa nePalamende.

(4) Mulawo wePalamende ungaleba kuti chibabi chechikamu (2) na (3)—

(a)

autoshinga kuchivumilano chenyika jopasi jose kana chilangano kana lupi luji lwechilangano kana chivumilano; kana

(b) kushinga chikashandulwa mukulyijana nechivumilano chenyika jopasi kana chilangano kana lupi luji lwechivumilano kana chilangano.

(5) IPalamande ingaba nechimiso chokuti chipi zwajo chivumilano chenyika jopasi jose kana luji lwezwilangano zwenyika jopasi jose achitokwelela kuvumilwa pasi pechibabi chechikamu (2), asi chimiso chakadali achitoshinga pechivumilano chinokwelela kushinga kana chinoshaka—

(a)

kuvubulwa kana kushingiswa kwemali jinova muChikwama chaFulumende; kana

(b) kushandulwa kwomulawu weZimbabwe.

(6) Kana kupandululwa mulawu, lyose igota namakota alobumbilwa kubetekesa undandu woyo unshaji, kulokwelela kutambula kupandululwa kwomulawu unokwendelana nomulawu, zwimiso kana zwivumilano zwenyika jopasi, pose unoshinga muZimbabwe, pana kushaluja kupandulula kushingiswa umwe mulawu uloleyana noyo mulawu, chimiso kana chivumilano.

328 Kululamisa iBumbilo lyeMilawu

(1) Muchikamu yechi— “Mulawu uchinofhulwa ubolinga nozweBumbilo” zwinoleba indangalilo jajompwa pezhulu pokuda kushandula iBumbilo;

“zwimo mukujibijwa kwokushinga” zwinoleba yezwo zwilolingililwa kuva muBumbilo zwinotala majibijilo onshaji wokugala panshingo echishingi kana yecho chinoshingila banji.

(2) Mulawu wePalamende unoshandula iBumbilo ulokwelela kuleba mumawhi aipo pachena.

(3) Mulawu uchinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo umwe unshaji aungatulwe muGota lyeSeneti kana muPalamende kutobelejwa zwimo muchikamu 131 kuzhe kwokuti uNdebelekeli apa chizibiso muwhalo yeGazeti, pezhulu peBhili, mukati mwamazhuba makumi mashanu anamakumi mana.

(4) Pakalepo kana uNdebeleki apa chizibiso pezhulu pomulawu uchinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo kutobelejwa chibabi chechikamu (3), iPalamende ilokwelela kukoka injeyo jabanhu banji kuti belebe imbono jabo pezhulu poMulawu uchinofhulwa, yezwi zwitiwa mumishangano yabanhu banji akale zwikakwalwa, akale ilokwelela kushoba mishangano nokupa zwishingiso kutila kuti banhu betubule kuta alo.

(5) Mulawu cuhinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo ulokwelela kutambulwa,yopo paunobalwa kwokupejisila muGota lyePalamende nomuGota lyeSeneti, mushule mwamavoti anokwana mabili pakati pamavoti matatu emhingu jomugota limwe nelimwe.

(6) Yopo Mulawu uchinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo upota kushandula zwimo muChisiyaniso 4 kana Chisiyaniso 16—

(a)

mukati mwemyeji mitatu mushule mwokutambulwa neGota lyePalamende nelye Seneti zwikwendelana nezwimo muchibabi chechikamu (5), lilokwelela kusengwa kubanhu kuti bevote mushalujo yokutambulwa kwoMulawu uchinofhulwa; akale

(b) kana wavumwa nabanhu banji, peshalujo yokuvuma kana kulamba Mulawu uchinofhulwa, uNdebeleki wePalamende ulokwelela kuta kuti usengwe kuNtungamili weNyika nokukambika, yoyo uchavuma akale etukokwala izina lilye pakalepo.

(7) Zwisineni nezwimo muchikamu yechi, kululamisika kwokulebha kwonshaji wechishingi panshingo, imbhudilo ili yokupamhija unshaji wokuba panshingo wechishingi chinoshingila banji,azwitoshinga kuna yabo banshingi bakashinga yoyo unshingo kana unokwendelana noyo, kusanu kwakwalwa chilulamiso yechi.

(8) Chibabi chechikamu (6) ne (7) azwijilokwelela kululamiswa muMulawu uchinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo imwempela akale zwilulamisio yezwi azwingasengwe kubanhu kuti bevote mushalujo yokuvuma kana kulamba mulawu yoyu.

(9) Chikamu yechi chingalulamiswa kutobelejwa chete, zwimo muchibabi chechikamu (3), (4), (5) ne (6), sofana chikamu yechi chimo muChisiyaniso 4.

(10) Kana Mulawu uchinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo wasengwa kuNtungamili weNyika kuti enoutambula nokukwala izina lilye, ulokwelela kubeli—

(a)

nechitupa chinova kuNdebeleki lunosumikija kuti mukuvota kwopejeleja kwakatiwa muPalamende, Mulawu uchinofhulwa wakavotelwa nemhingu jinokwana imbili pakati penhatu kuva kumhingu jeGota; akale

(b) nechitupa chinova kuNtungamili weSeneti kuti mukuvota kwopejeleja kwakatiwa muGota lyeSeneti, Mulawu uchinofhulwa wakavotelwa nemhingu jinokwana imbili pakati penhatu kuva kumhingu jeGota. 329 Kutanga kushinga kweBumbilo, zwimomukati zwinoshanduka nezwilolondotwa IShejulu yashanu nemwempela inoshinga pakutangisa kwokushinga kweBumbilo, kujimwa kweBumbilo lyantolo nonkwendeselo wokunda kuBumbilo ibhya muBumbilo yeli. IMHINGU 3 Kupandulula 330 Mashingilo eMhingu 3 Yeyi imhingu inoshinga pakupandululwa kweBumbilo kuzhe kwokuti mashingisilo esinodalo. 331 Zwimwe zwazwo zwingashingiswa pakupandulula iBumbilo Chikamu 46 chinoshinga, kana peli nenshanduko inodikana, pakupandululwa kweBumbilo kuzhe kweChisiyaniso 4. 332 Imbhaunulo MuBumbilo yeli— “Mulawu wePalamende” unoleba—

(a)

chibambo chakavumwa neGota lePalamende akale chikavuma nokukwalwa izina lyoNtungamili weNyika zwikwendelana neBumbilo; kana

(b) chibambo chakabeshinga muZimbabwe soMulawu wePalamende chisanu chashinga pezhuba yelyo, zwinoshanganisila—

(i) chose chibambo chimo muchikwejejeliso chomulawu weZimbabwe wakalonganyilwa pasi pamasimba oMulawu wafulumende noMulawu wokuBwilulula mugole lya 1996 pasi pamasimba oMulawu wakabumbwa ukapamhululwa [Chisiyaniso 1:03]; akale

(ii) chose chibambo kojoba chakasiyijwa mukwejejeliso yakalingululwa, ilodomwa muchibabi chendima (i), chakendila mbeli chishinga kojoba chakasiyijwa; akale

(iii) chibambo chakabambwa nePalamende yeZimbabwe mushule mwokushandulwa kwechibambo yechi chadomwa muchibabi chendima (i) asi chisani chashinga pezhuba yelyo; “kuzwibhata pakutiwa kwemishingo” zwinoleba chose chimiso, chitiko kana kusotiwa kwechinhu nonshingilo wabanhu banji kana yoyo unhu ukumuta unshingo wabanhu banji, akale nokusotubula kana kulamba kwoyo unhu kuti ete chimiso yecho kana kuta chitiko yecho; “kululamisa” zwinoleba kusiyanisa, kushandula, kupamhija, kubhisa kana kushandukisa; “inshalujo yokuzhajikija chigaloo chakasiyiwa” zwinoleba inshalujo yokugaja pechibhula chakasiyiwa nomwe muGota lyePalamende kana mukansili; “She” zwinoleba she ulodomwa muChisiyaniso 15;

“Bashingili bafulumende” zwinoleba yezwo zwilobandwa muchikamu 199; “Komishini” zwinoleba ikomishini ilobumbwa pezhulu peBumbilo; “Ikomiti yonshingilo muPalamende” zwinoleba ikomiti yezina yelo ilobumbwa pasi pechikamu 151; “bugalo bwokumamisha”zwinoleba unda ulobigwa kumbali pasi poMulawo wePalamende akale ushinga pasi pomulawo wezwamagalilo abanhu pasi pobutungamili bwaShe; “Mulawu uchinofhulwa unolinga nozweBumbilo” zwinoleba Mulawu uchinofhulwa, unoti kana wabambwa, unoshandula yezwo zwilobandwa muBumbilo;

“Igota lyozweBumbilo lyemilawu” zwinoleba iGota lyozweBumbilo lyeMilawu ilobumbwa nechikamu 162(a); “inyaya inanechokuta nozweBumbilo” zwinoleba inyaya inokwendelana nezwokupandulula, kutabilija kana kushingiswa kweBumbilo; “mulawu wezwamagalilo abanhu” zwinoleba mulawu wezwamagalilo abanhu mubugalo bwabo muZimbabwe;

“mulawu wezwokukabula” zwinoleba mulawu ulokwalwa unokolola kuzwibhata kwabashingi banotabilijila banhu, kushanganisila yabo banoshinga kwonshaji unshoma, yopo beta unshingo wabo kana yopo besinotubula kuta unshingo wabo;

“bashingi banota zwokutabilija” zwinoleba—

(a) zwishingi zwinotabilijila zwili mumakungwa,masoja namasoja omumpepo;

(b) mapolisa;

(c) zwishingi zwomumajele kana yoko kunokololwa banamilandu; kana

(d) lyose ibungano lilobumbwa kutila kutabilijila banhu banji zwiva muMulawu wePalamende akale ulebwa nomulawu yoyo kuti zwishingi zwokutabilijila;

“izhuba pakunotanga kushinga” zwinoleba izhuba yopo iBumbilo lyose yeli palinotanga kushinga sezwimo mundima 3(2) muShejulu yashanu nakamwempela; “Mulawu pezhulu pozweshalujo” zwinoleba Mulawu wePalamende unokolola ishalujo sezwimo muBumbilo yeli; “igole pakunopejiswa kushingiswa kwemali” zwinoleba unshaji wemyeji igumi nemibili unopela nonsi wa 31 Gumigulu; “unshingo” zwinoshanganisila masimba nonshingo kana unkwelelo;

“iGazeti” zwinoleba iwhalo yeGazeti yaFulumende ishanganisila zwikwalwa zwinopamhija iwhalo yeGazeti; “ishalujo jenyika” zwinoleba ishalujo jenyika—

(a)

jokushala uNtungamili weNyika, uNtobeli woNtungamili wenyika, bamilili muPalamende;

(b) imhingu jenhabi jinotonga mumakansili; “Fulumende” zwinoleba Fulumende wenyika yeZimbabwe;

“ibungano lipo pasi pamasimba afulumende” zwinoleba ibungano linamishingo inotungamilila fulumende kana inotungamililwa nomilili wafulumende, zwishanganisila kubhata unshingo wechikamu chinji chinoba chafulumende kana chikamu chitukutuku;

“Igota” kana pesina kuzhajikijwa zwinoleba igota lyeSeneti kana igota lyabamilili muPalamende; “ikomishini ilozwimilila” ikomishini ilozwimila zwinoleba ikomishini yabandwa muchikamu 232;

“umbetekesi” zwinoleba umbetekesi wegota lyozweBumbilo, igota lyopezhuluzhulu, igota lyopezhulu, igota lyezwabashingi negota lyezwamakwendeselo emishingo; “Ikomishini inobona nezwelulamilo namakota” zwinoleba ikomishini yezina yelyo sokubumbwa nechikamu 189; “Mulawu” zwinoleba—

(a) zwose zwimo muBumbilo yeli kana Mulawu wePalamende;

(b) zwose zwimo muchishingiso chemilawo;

(c) wose mulawu usilokwalwa asi ushinga muZimbabwe, kushanganisila mulawu wezwamagalilo abanhu;

“akale ‘zwimomulawu’, ‘zwinovumijwa mumulawu’, ‘zwinotobeleja mulawu’ akale ‘zwinotobelejwa nomulawu’ ilokwelela kuwhisisiswa alo; “unovumijwa kuta mishingo yozwemilawu” zwinoleba unhu unovumijwa kuta unshingo wozwemilawo muZimbabwe

“butungamili bwomubugalo” zwinoleba ikansili yabandwa muChibabi 3 cheChisiyaniso 14;

imhingu, kana kukwendelana—

(a) ikomishini kana limwe ibunga lilobumbwa pezhulu peBumbilo yeli, zwishanganisila ungali wechibhula nontobeli wongali wechibhula;

(b) ibungano lyozwemilawo, ikansili yomumatunhu kana yomumakuta kana yomubugalo, zwinoleba unhu ulodomwa kana kushalwa kuba mukansili, kana mubungano kana una amwe masimba zwawo;

(i) ibungano lyozwemilawu, ikansili yedunhu kana yeguta; kana yomubugalo;

(ii) ina ndandu wokukwendesa mishingo yebungano lyozwemilawo,yedunhu kana yeguta kana yomunalaunda;

“Umilili muPalamende” zwinoleba Seneta kana umilili muPalamende;
